Conclusion: FrozenDrive成功在保持预训练扩散模型知识的同时实现了强一致性，无需场景特定微调即可在恶劣条件下生成高质量多视角驾驶数据，并能有效提升下游自动驾驶模型的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Synthetic data for autonomous driving is surging, powered by diffusion models that promise scalable scene generation. Yet key obstacles remain, as enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency often relies on backbone fine-tuning or added layers, which erodes pre-trained knowledge and weakens text alignment. Models also stay close to the training distribution, struggling under adverse weather and unseen configurations, and fidelity favors frequent over rare classes. We address these gaps with FrozenDrive, a controllable generative framework that preserves a pretrained diffusion models knowledge while achieving strong consistency. FrozenDrive conditions on rich driving-stack signals and text prompts, and introduces knowledge-preserving spatio-temporal attention to impose cross-view alignment and temporal coherence in a single pass within a parameter-free frozen diffusion backbone. An additional object-focused constraint improves per-object fidelity for rare categories. Without any weather- or scene-specific fine-tuning, our model synthesizes globally coherent multi-view driving scenes from text, particularly under adverse and rare conditions, and surpasses prior baselines. On nuScenes, FrozenDrive augmented data significantly improves AD models performance, especially at night and in rain, demonstrating stronger robustness when trained with our scenario-targeted data.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散模型的自动驾驶合成数据正在蓬勃发展，它有望实现可扩展的场景生成。然而，关键障碍依然存在：强制执行多视角和时间一致性通常依赖于骨干网络微调或添加额外层，这会侵蚀预训练知识并削弱文本对齐能力。模型生成结果也贴近训练分布，在恶劣天气和未见配置下表现不佳，且保真度偏向高频常见类别而忽视低频罕见类别。我们通过FrozenDrive解决这些差距——这是一个可控生成框架，在保持强一致性的同时保留了预训练扩散模型的知识。FrozenDrive以丰富的驾驶栈信号和文本提示为条件，并引入知识保留的时空注意力机制，在无参数冻结的扩散骨干网络内，通过单次前向传播施加跨视角对齐和时间一致性。额外的物体聚焦约束提升了罕见类别的逐物体保真度。无需任何天气或场景特定微调，我们的模型就能从文本合成全局一致的多视角驾驶场景，尤其在恶劣和罕见条件下表现突出，并超越先前基线。在nuScenes上，FrozenDrive增强数据显著提升了自动驾驶模型的性能，特别是在夜间和雨天，使用我们场景目标数据训练时展现了更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [61] [SAM3 Self-Distillation for Fine-Grained GOOSE 2D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20130)
*Xuesong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D精细语义分割挑战赛的第四名方案，通过适配SAM3视觉基础模型的图像编码器并结合轻量化解码器，以及自蒸馏、多尺度测试增强和激进光度失真三种技术，在官方测试集上达到69.73%的复合mIoU。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在固定输入尺寸的分割模型中恢复多尺度推理能力，并利用视觉基础模型（SAM3）的强表征能力提升细粒度语义分割性能，同时探索数据增强策略对分割精度的实际影响。

Method: (1) 将近期视觉基础模型SAM3的图像编码器与轻量化解码器结合作为主模型；(2) 自蒸馏方案：以SAM3自身作为教师模型，使用真实边界框提示，在SAM3表现优于本模型的类别上进行知识蒸馏；(3) 图像级多尺度测试增强：对固定输入尺寸模型，通过缩放图像而非模型输入来实现多尺度推理；(4) 采用2025年GOOSE 2D冠军方案中的激进光度失真策略。

Result: 在官方1815张图像测试集上达到69.73%的复合mIoU，位列第四名。研究发现激进光度失真是提升效果最显著的单一因素。

Conclusion: 通过适配SAM3基础模型、自蒸馏、多尺度测试增强和激进光度失真等技术的组合，能有效提升细粒度语义分割性能，其中光度失真策略是最关键的改进来源。

Original Abstract: We describe our 4th-place entry to the ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge, which reached a composite mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) of 69.73% on the official 1,815-image test set. Our model adapts the image encoder of a recent visual foundation model, Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3), with a lightweight decoder. Beyond this, we contribute two techniques and one empirical finding: (i) a self-distillation scheme that re-uses SAM3 itself, prompted with ground-truth boxes, as a teacher on the classes where it outperforms our own model; (ii) an image-level multi-scale test-time augmentation scheme that restores multi-scale inference for a fixed-input-size model by rescaling the image rather than the model input; and (iii) the finding that an aggressive photometric distortion from a winning 2025 GOOSE 2D entry, transplanted onto our pipeline, is its single largest source of improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 我们介绍了在ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战赛中获得第四名的方案，该方案在包含1,815张图像的官方测试集上达到了69.73%的复合平均交并比（mIoU）。我们的模型适配了近期视觉基础模型——分割一切模型3（SAM3）的图像编码器，并搭配一个轻量化解码器。除此之外，我们贡献了两种技术和一项实验发现：(i) 一种自蒸馏方案，在SAM3优于我们模型的类别上，以真实边界框为提示，将SAM3自身重新用作教师模型；(ii) 一种图像级多尺度测试增强方案，通过重新缩放图像而非模型输入，为固定输入尺寸的模型恢复多尺度推理能力；(iii) 实验发现，将2025年GOOSE 2D冠军方案中的激进光度失真策略移植到我们的流水线中，是提升效果最显著的单一因素。

</details>


### [62] [TriFlow: Generating Artist-Like 3D Mesh Topology via Nearest-Vertex Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20131)
*Haoxuan Li,Ziya Erkoç,Daniele Sirigatti,Vladislav Rosov,Lei Li,Angela Dai,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriFlow 是一种生成式方法，通过将网格拓扑表示为表面上的最近顶点向量场（NVF），并利用潜流匹配模型生成该场，再结合拓扑感知的约束 QEM 简化，从而从输入几何条件（如符号距离场）生成具有艺术家风格拓扑的紧凑 3D 网格。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有学习驱动的网格生成方法在拓扑质量、泛化能力和计算效率上存在不足，生成的网格连接性缺乏艺术家风格的结构化特征。TriFlow 旨在解决这些问题，实现高质量、结构化且高效的紧凑网格生成。

Method: (1) 将网格拓扑表示为表面上的最近顶点向量场（NVF），每个点编码其在局部重心坐标系中与最近三角形顶点的关联；(2) 训练一个潜流匹配模型来合成该向量场，实现基于输入几何条件的拓扑生成；(3) 利用生成的 NVF 对表面区域进行聚类，并引导带拓扑感知优化的约束二次误差度量（QEM）网格简化，提取出连贯网格。

Result: 相比最先进的基于学习方法，TriFlow 在泛化能力和拓扑质量上显著更优；Chamfer Distance 降低 90%，速度提升 8 倍。

Conclusion: TriFlow 能够生成与输入几何高度匹配且具有结构化、艺术家风格连接性的输出网格，在性能和质量上大幅超越现有方法。

Original Abstract: We present TriFlow, a new generative approach for producing compact 3D meshes with artist-like triangle topology directly from input geometry conditions such as signed distance fields. Our key insight is to represent mesh topology as a nearest-vertex vector field (NVF) defined over the surface, where each point encodes its association to the nearest triangle vertex in the local barycentric frame. We train a latent flow-matching model to synthesize this field, enabling topology generation conditioned on the input geometry. To extract a coherent mesh, we cluster surface regions using the generated NVF and guide a constrained quadric error metric (QEM) mesh simplification with topology-aware optimization. This yields output meshes that closely match the input geometry while exhibiting structured, artist-like connectivity. Experiments demonstrate that TriFlow achieves stronger generalization and significantly improved topology quality compared to state-of-the-art learning-based approaches, alongside 90% lower Chamfer Distance and an 8x speedup.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了 TriFlow，一种新的生成式方法，能够直接从输入几何条件（如符号距离场）生成具有艺术家风格三角形拓扑的紧凑 3D 网格。我们的关键见解是将网格拓扑表示为定义在表面上的最近顶点向量场（NVF），其中每个点编码其在局部重心坐标系中与最近三角形顶点的关联。我们训练一个潜流匹配模型来合成该场，从而实现基于输入几何条件的拓扑生成。为了提取连贯的网格，我们利用生成的 NVF 对表面区域进行聚类，并引导带拓扑感知优化的约束二次误差度量（QEM）网格简化。这产生的输出网格与输入几何高度匹配，同时展现出结构化、艺术家风格的连接性。实验表明，与最先进的基于学习方法相比，TriFlow 实现了更强的泛化能力和显著提升的拓扑质量，同时 Chamfer Distance 降低 90%，速度提升 8 倍。

</details>


### [63] [SA-VIS: Sparse frame Annotations for training Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20140)
*Edoardo Mello Rella,Ajad Chhatkuli,Shipra Jain,Ender Konukoglu,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SA-VIS方法，通过轻量的过去帧特征传播模块（PFP）和帧特定实例查询，实现基于稀疏帧标注的高效在线视频实例分割，仅使用1/5标注数据即可逼近密集标注的性能（仅下降0.4% AP）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线视频实例分割（VIS）方法依赖密集标注的长序列帧进行训练，计算成本和标注成本高昂。为降低这些成本，探索能否在不使用密集标注帧的情况下有效建模视频中实例及其演变。

Method: 提出Past-frames Feature Propagation (PFP)模块，聚合图像编码器对多帧提取的低维特征，实现稀疏帧标签下的端到端训练；结合轻量级的帧特定实例查询（Instance Queries），构成SA-VIS框架。

Result: 在YouTube-VIS 2019/2021/2022和OVIS数据集上显著超越基线；仅使用1/5标注数据时性能仅下降0.4% AP；在有限标注场景下相比SOTA提升超过1% AP。

Conclusion: 通过简单的PFP模块和实例查询设计，SA-VIS有效弥合了稀疏标注与密集标注之间的精度差距，证明了视频实例分割无需密集标注帧即可高效训练。

Original Abstract: Recent online video instance segmentation (VIS) methods have achieved impressive results, thus becoming the preferred approach to segment instances in videos. Despite the resurgence of impressive single image models, the online (or semi-online) VIS approaches outperform single-image models (e.g., based on SAM) by using long sequences of densely annotated frames during training. However,such a training setup of VIS is expensive in the sense of compute as well as dense annotations required. In order to solve these major flaws, we argue that the effective modeling of the instances and their evolution in videos do not require densely annotated frames. To that end, we propose a simple and effective module, called Past-frames Feature Propagation (PFP) which aggregates low-dimensional features from the image encoder of multiple frames. This simple low-compute module provides tremendous learning capability in using sparse video frame labels for end-to-end training. Combined with a light-weight frame-specific Instance Queries, our Sparse frame Annotation VIS (SA-VIS) significantly improves performance over its baseline. Most interestingly, our simple design that avoids complexities effectively bridges the gap in accuracy between training on sparsely and densely annotated video sequences. This translates to a mere 0.4% drop in performance of SA-VIS when using annotations for only 1/5 of the images in the dataset. Empirically, SA-VIS shows strong improvements over the baseline on YouTube-VIS 2019/2021/2022 and Occluded VIS (OVIS) and an over 1% improvement in AP on the state-of-the-art in a limited annotations scenario.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的在线视频实例分割（VIS）方法取得了令人印象深刻的结果，因此成为视频中分割实例的首选方法。尽管优秀的单图像模型有所复兴，在线（或半在线）VIS方法通过在训练中使用密集标注的长序列帧，性能超越了单图像模型（例如基于SAM的模型）。然而，这种VIS的训练设置在计算和密集标注方面成本高昂。为了解决这些主要缺陷，我们认为，对视频中实例及其演变进行有效建模并不需要密集标注的帧。为此，我们提出一个简单而有效的模块，称为过去帧特征传播（PFP），该模块聚合来自多个帧的图像编码器的低维特征。这种简单的低计算量模块为使用稀疏视频帧标签进行端到端训练提供了强大的学习能力。结合轻量级的帧特定实例查询，我们的稀疏帧标注VIS（SA-VIS）显著提升了其基线的性能。最有趣的是，我们避免复杂性的简单设计有效弥合了在稀疏标注和密集标注视频序列上训练之间的精度差距。这意味着当仅使用数据集中1/5图像的标注时，SA-VIS的性能仅下降0.4%。实验上，SA-VIS在YouTube-VIS 2019/2021/2022和Occluded VIS（OVIS）上相比基线有显著提升，并在有限标注场景下相比当前最优方法在AP上提升了超过1%。

</details>


### [64] [HEad and neCK TumOR (HECKTOR) 2025: Benchmark of Segmentation, Diagnosis, and Prognosis in Multimodal PET/CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20143)
*Numan Saeed,Salma Hassan,Shahad Hardan,Lishan Cai,Xinglong Liang,Moona Mazher,Abdul Qayyum,Yansong Bu,Mengye Lyu,Yue Lin,Mingyuan Meng,Chuanyi Huang,Lisheng Wang,Dalal Chamseddine,Shamimeh Ahrari,Beining Wu,Yifei Chen,Fuyou Mao,Hao Zhang,Baixiang Zhao,Surajit Ray,Muzi Guo,Lei Xiang,Jakob Dexl,Michael Ingrisch,Adrien Depeursinge,Arman Rahmim,Mathieu Hatt,Vincent Andrearczyk,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HECKTOR 2025挑战赛，通过多模态PET/CT影像和电子健康记录构建了头颈癌自动化分析的基准，包括肿瘤分割、复发预测和HPV状态分类三项任务，并分析了15个提交方法的性能表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 头颈癌肿瘤勾画的手动分割耗时且存在观察者间差异，此外从无创影像预测复发风险和HPV状态具有临床价值但极具挑战性。因此需要建立一个全面的自动化分析基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 基于HECKTOR 2025挑战赛，利用包含全球10个中心1100多名患者的多中心多模态PET/CT影像数据集，设置三项互补任务：原发肿瘤和转移淋巴结分割、无复发生存预测、HPV状态分类。参赛方法涵盖多种深度学习架构。

Result: 分割任务Dice相似系数均值0.75，生存预测一致性指数0.66，HPV分类平衡准确率0.56。论文对15个提交方法在不同病灶特征上的性能进行了全面分析。

Conclusion: 本文对参赛方法学进行了系统分析，评估了不同病变特征下的性能表现，讨论了这些方法在自动化肿瘤学工作流和临床决策支持系统中的转化潜力。

Original Abstract: Head and neck cancers (HNC) represent a significant global health burden, with accurate tumor delineation being essential for effective radiotherapy planning. The complexity of the oropharyngeal anatomy, combined with the heterogeneous appearance of tumors on imaging, makes manual segmentation time-intensive and subject to inter-observer variability. Beyond segmentation, predicting long-term clinical outcomes, such as recurrence-free survival (RFS), and determining human papillomavirus (HPV) status from noninvasive imaging, remain challenging yet clinically valuable goals. The HECKTOR 2025 challenge addresses these needs by establishing a comprehensive benchmark for automated HNC analysis using multimodal PET/CT imaging and electronic health records. Building on previous editions (2020-2022), this challenge features an expanded multi-institutional dataset comprising over 1,100 patients from 10 centers worldwide. Participants were tasked with three complementary objectives: (1) segmenting primary gross tumor volumes (GTVp) and metastatic lymph nodes (GTVn), (2) predicting recurrence-free survival, and (3) classifying HPV status. The challenge attracted 35 registered teams, with 15 final submissions evaluated on a held-out test set. Top-performing algorithms achieved a mean Dice similarity coefficient of 0.75 for segmentation, a concordance index of 0.66 for survival prediction, and a balanced accuracy of 0.56 for HPV classification. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of the submitted methodologies, evaluates their performance across different lesion characteristics, and discusses their implications for clinical translation in automated oncology workflows and decision support systems.

中文摘要翻译: 头颈癌（HNC）是全球重大健康负担，精确的肿瘤勾画对于有效的放射治疗规划至关重要。口咽部解剖结构的复杂性，加上影像上肿瘤表现的异质性，使得手动分割耗时且存在观察者间差异。除分割外，从无创影像预测长期临床结局（如无复发生存期RFS）以及确定人乳头瘤病毒（HPV）状态，仍然是具有临床价值但极具挑战性的目标。HECKTOR 2025挑战赛通过建立基于多模态PET/CT影像和电子健康记录的自动化头颈癌分析综合基准来应对这些需求。在前几届挑战赛（2020-2022年）的基础上，本次挑战赛扩展了数据集规模，涵盖来自全球10个中心的1100多名患者。参赛者需完成三项互补目标：（1）分割原发病灶大体肿瘤体积（GTVp）和转移淋巴结（GTVn），（2）预测无复发生存期，（3）分类HPV状态。挑战赛吸引了35支注册团队，最终有15个提交方法在留出测试集上进行了评估。顶级算法在分割任务上实现了平均Dice相似系数0.75，生存预测一致性指数0.66，HPV分类平衡准确率0.56。本文对提交的方法学进行了全面分析，评估了它们在不同病变特征下的性能表现，并讨论了其对自动化肿瘤学工作流和临床决策支持系统中临床转化的意义。

</details>


### [65] [NAMESAKES: Probing Identity Memorization in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20155)
*Morris Alper,Vasudha Varadarajan,Moran Yanuka,Angelina Wang,Hadar Averbuch-Elor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参考照片、无需访问训练数据或模型内部结构的完全黑盒行为探针，用于区分文本到图像模型生成的人脸是记忆重现还是虚构生成，并在新数据集NAMESAKES上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法区分生成人脸是记忆重现还是虚构生成时，需要真实照片、训练数据或模型白盒访问，限制了实际应用场景。本文旨在解决这一局限，提出无需上述条件的黑盒探针。

Method: 设计了一种完全黑盒的行为探针（behavioral probe），仅通过模型输出行为进行分析，无需参考照片、先验训练数据知识或模型内部结构信息，即可区分记忆重现与虚构生成。

Result: 在包含超千名不同知名度公众人物姓名与人脸的数据集NAMESAKES上的实验表明，该探针能够有效预测身份记忆程度，并区分记忆重现的姓名与未被识别的姓名，同时揭示了不同模型家族之间的差异。

Conclusion: 本文提出的黑盒行为探针无需真实照片或训练数据即可有效区分T2I模型中的记忆重现与虚构生成，为隐私保护研究提供了实用的分析工具。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models generate realistic likenesses of some individuals when prompted with their names, raising privacy concerns. However, distinguishing whether a generated face is memorized or fabricated currently requires ground-truth photos, access to training data, or white-box access to model internals, limiting applicability. We introduce a fully black-box behavioral probe that distinguishes between these regimes while requiring no reference photos or prior knowledge of training data. To benchmark this task, we present the NAMESAKES dataset of over one thousand names and faces of public figures spanning a wide range of fame levels, along with perturbed, less famous names. Experiments on state-of-the-art T2I models show that our probe substantially predicts identity memorization and separates memorized from unrecognized names, with further insights into differences across model families.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像（T2I）模型在输入某些人名提示词时，能够生成与真实个体高度相似的面孔，这引发了隐私担忧。然而，目前区分生成人脸是记忆重现还是虚构生成，需要依赖真实照片、访问训练数据或对模型内部结构进行白盒访问，限制了其适用范围。本文提出了一种完全黑盒的行为探针，无需参考照片或事先了解训练数据即可区分这两种情况。为了对该任务进行基准测试，我们提出了NAMESAKES数据集，包含超过一千名覆盖不同知名度水平的公众人物的姓名和面孔，以及经过扰动处理的、知名度较低的名字。在多个最先进的T2I模型上的实验表明，我们的探针能够有效预测身份记忆程度，并区分记忆重现的姓名与未被识别的姓名，同时揭示了不同模型家族之间的差异。

</details>


### [66] [ARTEMIS: Agent-guided Reliability-aware Temporal Mask Evolution for Imperfectly Supervised Video Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20161)
*Tong Wang,Siwen Wang,Yaolei Qi,Jinxing Zhou,Yuting He,Guanyu Yang,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ARTEMIS，一个统一框架，通过智能体引导的可靠性感知时间掩膜演化，解决不完美监督下的视频息肉分割难题，在弱监督（点、涂鸦）和半监督（少量密集标注帧）设定下均达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有不完美监督视频息肉分割方法面临弱对比度、边界模糊、运动模糊和镜面高光等挑战，而SAM2直接生成伪标注存在边界泄漏、未充分利用时间一致性且忽略可靠性问题，亟需一种能融合多源稀疏监督、利用时间信息并感知掩膜可靠性的统一框架。

Method: ARTEMIS包含三阶段：(1) 从监督信号初始化粗掩膜——SAM2转换点/涂鸦，密集标签作为可靠锚点；(2) 辩论-判断视觉语言智能体在弱监督下选择可靠时间锚点，经SAM2双向传播以优化不可靠或无标注帧；(3) 时间可靠性感知鲁棒学习训练分割器，包含可靠性引导参考选择、参考原型传输模块和可靠性感知鲁棒损失，通过降权噪声监督而非丢弃难样本。

Result: 在SUN-SEG和CVC-ClinicDB-612数据集上的涂鸦、点和有限标签设置下，ARTEMIS均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: ARTEMIS通过可靠性感知的时间掩膜演化策略，有效解决了不完美监督视频息肉分割中伪标注质量低、时间一致性利用不足和可靠性被忽视的问题，实现了统一的弱监督和半监督框架。

Original Abstract: Imperfectly supervised video polyp segmentation (VPS) aims to learn dense, temporally consistent masks from inexpensive supervision, including weak annotations (points, scribbles) and semi-supervision with few densely labeled frames. This setting is clinically valuable but challenging due to weak contrast, ambiguous boundaries, motion blur, and specular highlights, compounded by sparse pixel-level guidance. While SAM2 can generate dense masks from sparse inputs, direct pseudo-labeling often yields geometry-degraded masks with boundary leakage, underutilizes temporal consistency, and ignores reliability. To address these issues, we propose ARTEMIS, a unified framework for imperfectly supervised VPS driven by agent-guided reliability-aware temporal mask evolution. ARTEMIS initializes coarse masks from available supervision: SAM2 converts points/scribbles, while dense labels serve as reliable anchors. A debate-and-judge vision-language agent selects reliable temporal anchors under weak supervision, which are propagated bidirectionally with SAM2 to refine unreliable or unlabeled frames. Finally, ARTEMIS trains the segmenter using temporal reliability-aware robust learning, incorporating reliability-guided reference selection, a Reference Prototype Transport Module, and reliability-aware robust loss. These components assess mask reliability, evolve anchors over time, transport target identity across frames, and down-weight noisy supervision instead of discarding difficult samples. Experiments on SUN-SEG and CVC-ClinicDB-612 under scribble, point, and limited-label settings demonstrate that ARTEMIS achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code will be released at https://github.com/wangtong627/ARTEMIS.

中文摘要翻译: 不完美监督下的视频息肉分割（VPS）旨在从廉价监督信号中学习密集且时间一致的掩膜，这些监督包括弱标注（点、涂鸦）以及少量密集标注帧的半监督。该设置具有临床价值，但由于弱对比度、模糊边界、运动模糊和镜面高光等问题，加之稀疏的像素级引导，任务极具挑战性。尽管SAM2可以从稀疏输入生成密集掩膜，但直接生成伪标签往往产生几何退化的掩膜，存在边界泄露、未充分利用时间一致性且忽略可靠性。针对这些问题，我们提出ARTEMIS，一种由智能体引导的、基于可靠性感知的时间掩膜演化驱动的统一框架，用于不完美监督的视频息肉分割。ARTEMIS从可用监督信号初始化粗掩膜：SAM2转换点/涂鸦标注，密集标签则作为可靠锚点。一个辩论-判断视觉语言智能体在弱监督下选择可靠的时间锚点，这些锚点通过SAM2双向传播，以优化不可靠或无标注帧。最后，ARTEMIS使用时间可靠性感知的鲁棒学习训练分割器，包括可靠性引导的参考选择、参考原型传输模块和可靠性感知的鲁棒损失。这些组件评估掩膜可靠性、随时间演化锚点、跨帧传输目标身份，并对噪声监督进行降权处理而非丢弃困难样本。在SUN-SEG和CVC-ClinicDB-612数据集上，针对涂鸦、点和有限标签设置的实验表明，ARTEMIS达到了最先进的性能。代码将发布于https://github.com/wangtong627/ARTEMIS。

</details>


### [67] [Evaluating and Enhancing Negation Comprehension in Remote Sensing MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20177)
*Haochen Han,Jue Wang,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Fangming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RS-Neg基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在遥感图像中的否定理解能力，并提出NeFo测试时学习方法，仅用约5%无标签测试样本即可显著提升模型对否定逻辑的理解与泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在遥感任务中表现优异，但对否定语义（如“非洪水区域”）的理解能力严重不足，导致在实际应用（如应急疏散路线规划）中产生幻觉和性能下降，而此前缺乏针对性的评估基准。

Method: (1) 构建RS-Neg基准：利用LLM自动生成多样化否定查询，并引入动态视觉焦点模块进行验证；(2) 提出NeFo方法：一种测试时学习方法，显式将否定逻辑融入模型优化过程，仅使用约5%无标注测试样本进行学习。

Result: 评估表明现有RS MLLMs在否定理解任务上表现不佳，存在严重幻觉和性能退化；NeFo方法能显著提升模型的否定理解能力，并展现出良好的跨任务泛化性。

Conclusion: 遥感MLLMs在否定理解方面存在显著缺陷，而NeFo作为一种轻量级测试时学习方法，能有效弥补这一能力差距，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in various Remote Sensing (RS) tasks. However, their ability to comprehend negation remains underexplored, limiting deployment in real-world applications where models must explicitly identify what is false or absent, e.g., emergency responders need to locate non-flooded routes for evacuation. To comprehensively study this limitation, we introduce RS-Neg, the first benchmark to evaluate negation understanding across region-level to scene-level tasks. Specifically, we design an automated data generation pipeline for RS imagery, using LLMs to synthesize diverse negation queries, and introduce a dynamic visual focus module for verification. Our evaluation reveals that advanced RS MLLMs struggle with negation, exhibiting hallucinations and substantial performance degradation. To close this gap, we propose NeFo, a novel test-time learning method that explicitly incorporates the logical role of negation into the model optimization. Remarkably, using about 5\% unlabeled test samples, NeFo significantly improves the negation understanding of models and shows strong generalization to unseen tasks. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在各类遥感（RS）任务中取得了显著成功。然而，它们对否定语义的理解能力仍未被充分探索，这限制了模型在实际应用中的部署——在这些场景中，模型需要明确识别什么是错误的或不存在的，例如应急响应人员需要定位非洪水路线以进行疏散。为全面研究这一局限，我们提出了RS-Neg，这是首个评估从区域级到场景级任务中否定理解能力的基准。具体而言，我们设计了一条面向遥感图像的自动化数据生成流水线，利用大语言模型合成多样化的否定查询，并引入一个动态视觉焦点模块进行验证。评估结果表明，先进的遥感MLLMs在否定理解方面存在困难，表现出幻觉和显著的性能退化。为弥补这一差距，我们提出NeFo，一种新颖的测试时学习方法，该方法将否定的逻辑角色显式地纳入模型优化过程。值得注意的是，仅使用约5%的无标签测试样本，NeFo就能显著提升模型的否定理解能力，并展现出对未见任务的良好泛化性。代码和数据将在论文被接收后发布。

</details>


### [68] [HilDA: Hierarchical Distillation with Diffusion for Advancing Self-Supervised LiDAR Pre-trainin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20189)
*Maciej Wozniak,Jesper Ericsson,Hariprasath Govindarajan,Truls Nyberg,Thomas Gustafsson,Patric Jensfelt,Olov Andersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HilDA，一种自监督预训练框架，通过层次化知识蒸馏（多层蒸馏+全局上下文蒸馏）和时间占用扩散目标，更好地从视觉基础模型（VFM）向LiDAR骨干网络传递语义和几何知识，在3D检测、场景流和语义占用预测等任务上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将VFM视为黑盒教师，仅依赖逐帧特征相似性，未能充分利用教师模型的层次化语义结构、全局上下文以及LiDAR序列中丰富的时空信息，导致知识蒸馏效果受限。

Method: 提出HilDA框架，包含：(1) 层次化蒸馏：多层蒸馏实现渐进式语义对齐，全局上下文蒸馏捕获场景级语义；(2) 时间占用扩散目标：促进时空一致性约束，用于自监督预训练LiDAR骨干网络。

Result: 在跨模态蒸馏基准上达到SOTA结果；在3D目标检测、场景流估计和语义占用预测任务上优于先前蒸馏方法训练的模型。

Conclusion: 通过层次化蒸馏和时空一致性约束，更充分地挖掘VFM中的语义和几何知识，有效提升了LiDAR骨干网络的自监督预训练效果。

Original Abstract: Leveraging Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) for camera-to-LiDAR knowledge distillation offers a promising solution to the scarcity of annotated data needed to represent the immense geometric and kinematic diversity of real-world autonomous driving (AD). However, current approaches typically treat VFMs as black-box teachers, relying exclusively on frame-wise feature similarity. Consequently, they do not fully exploit the teacher's layer-wise semantic structure and global context, as well as the rich spatiotemporal information inherent in LiDAR sequences. We propose HilDA, a self-supervised pretraining framework for LiDAR backbones that better captures the semantic what and geometric where needed for driving tasks. HilDA combines hierarchical distillation comprising multi-layer distillation for progressive semantic alignment and global context distillation for scene-level semantics, with a temporal occupancy diffusion objective promoting spatiotemporal consistency. Models pre-trained with HilDA achieve state-of-the-art results on cross-modal distillation benchmarks and outperform models trained via prior distillation approaches on 3D object detection, scene flow, and semantic occupancy prediction. Code available at: https://maxiuw.github.io/hilda.

中文摘要翻译: 利用视觉基础模型（VFM）进行相机到LiDAR的知识蒸馏，为解决标注数据稀缺问题提供了有前景的方案，这些标注数据用于表示真实世界自动驾驶（AD）中巨大的几何和运动学多样性。然而，当前方法通常将VFM视为黑盒教师，仅依赖逐帧特征相似性。因此，它们未能充分利用教师模型的层次化语义结构和全局上下文，以及LiDAR序列中固有的丰富时空信息。我们提出HilDA，一种用于LiDAR骨干网络的自监督预训练框架，能更好地捕捉驾驶任务所需的语义“是什么”和几何“在哪里”。HilDA结合了层次化蒸馏（包括多层蒸馏用于渐进式语义对齐和全局上下文蒸馏用于场景级语义）和时间占用扩散目标以促进时空一致性。使用HilDA预训练的模型在跨模态蒸馏基准上取得了最先进的结果，并在3D目标检测、场景流估计和语义占用预测任务上优于通过先前蒸馏方法训练的模型。代码详见：https://maxiuw.github.io/hilda。

</details>


### [69] [Distill Once, Adapt Life-Long: Exploring Dataset Distillation for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20196)
*Hyun-Kurl Jang,Jihun Kim,Hyeokjun Kweon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DO-ALL框架，通过数据集蒸馏（DD）在部署前生成少量合成锚点来总结源分布，在持续测试时适应（CTTA）中利用这些锚点进行源回放、表示对齐和流形平滑正则化，无需保留原始源数据即可实现长期稳定的在线适应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际部署中常因隐私或许可限制无法保留源数据集，而纯无源CTTA方法在长期分布偏移下会因累积自训练错误和灾难性遗忘而变得不稳定。需要一种既能利用源信息又无需保留原始数据的方法。

Method: DO-ALL框架包含两个阶段：(1) 部署前，通过数据集蒸馏（DD）从源数据中生成一小组合成蒸馏锚点，以紧凑且隐私友好的形式概括源分布；(2) 适应时，每个目标样本与语义最对齐的锚点匹配，通过源回放、表示对齐和流形平滑正则化提供稳定参考，可无缝集成到现有CTTA算法中。

Result: 在CIFAR100-C、ImageNet-C和CCC基准上，DO-ALL一致提升了长期鲁棒性，表明利用DD可实现无需保留原始源数据的稳定持续适应。

Conclusion: DO-ALL通过数据集蒸馏以紧凑、隐私友好的形式重新利用源信息，有效解决了无源CTTA中的长期不稳定问题，可作为插件式框架提升现有CTTA方法的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Continual Test-Time Adaptation (CTTA) aims to maintain model performance under evolving target domains by adapting online without labeled data. However, practical deployments often cannot retain the source dataset due to privacy or licensing constraints, and purely source-free CTTA methods tend to become unstable under long-term distribution shift, suffering from compounding self-training errors and catastrophic forgetting. We introduce DO-ALL (Distill Once, Adapt Life-Long), a plug-and-play framework that revisits source information in a compact and privacy-conscious form via Dataset Distillation (DD). Before deployment, DO-ALL performs DD to produce a small set of synthetic distilled anchors that summarize the source distribution. During adaptation, each target sample is matched with its most semantically aligned anchor, which provides a stable reference for various CTTA via source replay, representation alignment, and manifold-smoothing regularization. DO-ALL can be seamlessly integrated into existing CTTA algorithms, consistently improving long-term robustness across CIFAR100-C, ImageNet-C, and the CCC benchmark. This demonstrates the potential of leveraging DD to enable stable and continuous adaptation without retaining raw source data. The code is available at https://github.com/blue-531/DOALL.

中文摘要翻译: 持续测试时适应（CTTA）旨在通过在线无标签数据适应的方式，在目标域不断演变的情况下维持模型性能。然而，实际部署中由于隐私或许可限制，通常无法保留源数据集，而纯无源的CTTA方法在长期分布偏移下会变得不稳定，遭受累积性的自训练错误和灾难性遗忘。我们提出DO-ALL（一次蒸馏，终身适应），这是一个即插即用的框架，通过数据集蒸馏（DD）以紧凑且保护隐私的形式重新利用源信息。在部署前，DO-ALL执行DD生成一小组合成蒸馏锚点，用于概括源分布。在适应过程中，每个目标样本与其语义最对齐的锚点进行匹配，通过源回放、表示对齐和流形平滑正则化为各种CTTA提供稳定参考。DO-ALL可以无缝集成到现有的CTTA算法中，在CIFAR100-C、ImageNet-C和CCC基准上持续提升长期鲁棒性。这表明利用DD可以在不保留原始源数据的情况下实现稳定且持续的适应。代码可在https://github.com/blue-531/DOALL获取。

</details>


### [70] [Evaluation of Image Matching for Art Skills Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20199)
*Asaad Alghamdi,Michael Poor,Trung-Nghia Le,Tam V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用计算机视觉技术（SIFT特征和Siamese网络）匹配手绘图像与原始模板来评估绘画技能水平的方法，实验表明SIFT特征匹配更有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的绘画技能评估过程繁琐、耗时，缺乏客观高效的自动化评估手段，因此希望借助计算机视觉技术实现人类水平的图像相似度比较。

Method: 利用SIFT特征和Siamese网络两种计算机视觉方法，对手绘图像与原始模板图像进行相似度匹配，通过特征分析来评估绘画技能水平。

Result: 基于SIFT的关键点匹配方法在检测绘画技能方面比Siamese网络更有效。

Conclusion: 利用计算机视觉技术评估绘画技能水平是可行的，其中SIFT特征匹配方法效果更优。

Original Abstract: While some individuals possess a natural talent for drawing, mastering this skill requires dedicated training and practice. Determining one's skill in the art of drawing requires proper comprehensive assessment. In this paper, we propose a method to measure drawing skill by by matching the hand-drawn image with the original template. Existing techniques often involve complex processes. However, advancements in computer vision allow us to train computers to perform these comparisons at a human-like level, thereby resolving the tedious and overwhelming traditional process. Using computer vision applications, determining image similarity involves identifying the level of similarities in an image with a reference image. We have implemented and analyzed the SIFT feature and Siamese network to measure image similarity. Our results indicate that it is feasible to assess art skill levels. Through feature analysis, we found that SIFT-based key point matching provides a more effective means of detecting drawing skills.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然有些人天生具备绘画天赋，但掌握这项技能仍需专门的训练和练习。评估一个人的绘画技能水平需要全面且适当的方法。本文提出了一种通过将手绘图像与原始模板进行匹配来衡量绘画技能的方法。现有技术通常涉及复杂的过程。然而，计算机视觉的进步使我们能够训练计算机以接近人类的水平执行这些比较，从而解决了传统流程中繁琐且耗时的难题。利用计算机视觉应用，判断图像相似度涉及识别图像与参考图像之间的相似程度。我们实现并分析了SIFT特征和Siamese网络来衡量图像相似度。实验结果表明，评估绘画技能水平是可行的。通过特征分析，我们发现基于SIFT的关键点匹配方法在检测绘画技能方面更为有效。

</details>


### [71] [DeepForestVisionV2: Ecology-Driven Taxonomy Expansion for Camera-Trap Monitoring in African Tropical Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20223)
*Hugo Magaldi,Theau d'Audiffret,Etienne Francois Akomo-Okoue,Bala Amarasekaran,Naomi Anderson,Claire Auger,Noemie Cappelle,Daniel Cornelis,Raphael Cornette,Tobias Deschner,Gabriel Dubus,Davy Fonteyn,Rosa M. Garriga,Jennifer Hatlauf,Innocent Kasekendi,Raymond Katumba,Aram Kazandjian,Alfred Ngomanda,Stephan Ntie,Simone Pika,Xavier Rufray,Harold Rugonge,John Justice Tibesigwa,Peter van Lunteren,Hadrien Vanthomme,Joeri A. Zwerts,Sabrina Krief*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepForestVisionV2，将相机陷阱图像分类从35类扩展到64类，以覆盖非洲热带森林中垂直分层、场景开放度和人为界面等部署梯度，在保持离线工作流的同时显著提升了现场可用性和分类鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原有DeepForestVision模型仅针对封闭冠层、地面级森林内部设计，使用35类预测空间，在面对树栖灵长类、鸟类、半水生类群及牲畜等人为干扰物时分类粒度太粗，无法满足多样化的野外部署需求。

Method: 将预测类别从35类扩展至64类（61个动物类+人类、车辆、空白），针对垂直分层、场景开放度和人为界面三种部署梯度进行生态驱动的类别设计，沿用原有离线工作流，使用多国非洲热带森林项目的1,535,010张照片和243,354段视频进行训练。

Result: 在验证集上达到0.86准确率、0.82宏F1和0.81平衡准确率；在森林内部视频中识别的分类单元从22个增至29个，河岸场景从4个增至9个；公园边缘场景准确率从0.62提升至0.86，误报从11次降至0次。

Conclusion: DeepForestVisionV2在保持跨站点、跨栖息地和相机设置鲁棒性的同时，显著提升了现场实用性，证实了生态驱动类别扩展的有效性。

Original Abstract: Camera-trap monitoring in African tropical forests increasingly extends beyond closed-canopy interiors to riverbanks, clearings, and park edges. Among available open tools for African forest camera-trap classification, DeepForestVision is the only one providing a matched offline workflow for both photographs and videos, and previous work showed that it outperformed other available baselines on a comparable benchmark. However, it was designed for closed-canopy, ground-level forest interiors and uses a 35-class prediction space that becomes too coarse when deployments encounter arboreal primates, birds, semi-aquatic taxa, or human-associated confounders such as livestock. We present DeepForestVisionV2, an ecology-driven expansion from 35 to 64 prediction classes (61 animal classes plus human, vehicle, and blank) designed to address three recurrent deployment gradients: vertical stratification, scene openness, and anthropogenic interfaces. DeepForestVisionV2 retains the same offline workflow and is trained on 1,535,010 photographs and 243,354 videos from multi-country African tropical-forest projects. Evaluation combines a cross-country cropped-photo validation set, used to assess robustness across sites and camera-trap settings, with three held-out Uganda video benchmarks spanning the targeted gradients. On the validation set, DeepForestVisionV2 reaches 0.86 accuracy, 0.82 macro-F1, and 0.81 balanced accuracy. On the deployment benchmarks, it preserves or improves baseline accuracy despite its harder classification task, while increasing the number of identified taxa from 22 to 29 in forest-interior videos and from 4 to 9 at riverbanks. In the park-edge use case, it raises accuracy from 0.62 to 0.86 and reduces false alarms from 11 to 0. These results show that DeepForestVisionV2 materially improves field utility while preserving robustness across sites, habitats, and camera-trap settings.

中文摘要翻译: 非洲热带森林中的相机陷阱监测正越来越多地从封闭冠层内部扩展到河岸、空地及公园边缘。在现有的非洲森林相机陷阱分类开源工具中，DeepForestVision是唯一提供照片和视频匹配离线工作流的工具，此前的研究表明其在可比基准上优于其他基线方法。然而，该模型专为封闭冠层、地面级森林内部设计，使用35类预测空间，当部署遇到树栖灵长类、鸟类、半水生类群或牲畜等人为干扰因素时分类粒度变得过于粗糙。我们提出DeepForestVisionV2，这是一种由生态驱动的从35类到64类预测类别（61个动物类别加上人类、车辆和空白）的扩展，旨在应对三种反复出现的部署梯度：垂直分层、场景开放度和人为界面。DeepForestVisionV2保留了相同的离线工作流，并使用来自多国非洲热带森林项目的1,535,010张照片和243,354段视频进行训练。评估结合了一个跨国家裁剪照片验证集（用于评估跨站点和相机陷阱设置的鲁棒性）和三个留出的乌干达视频基准（涵盖目标梯度）。在验证集上，DeepForestVisionV2达到了0.86的准确率、0.82的宏F1和0.81的平衡准确率。在部署基准上，尽管分类任务更具挑战性，它仍然保持或提升了基线准确率，同时将森林内部视频中识别的分类单元从22个增加到29个，河岸场景中从4个增加到9个。在公园边缘用例中，它将准确率从0.62提升到0.86，并将误报从11次减少到0次。这些结果表明，DeepForestVisionV2在保持跨站点、栖息地和相机陷阱设置鲁棒性的同时，显著提升了现场实用性。

</details>


### [72] [Cinematic Compositing Using Character-Environment-Harmonized Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20233)
*Tianyi Xiang,Mingming He,Li Ma,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的视频扩散框架，通过联合建模角色与环境的双向物理交互和光照协调，实现高质量的电影级动态视频合成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法未能捕捉角色与环境之间复杂的双向交互（C2E物理交互和E2C光照协调），导致合成结果缺乏物理和光度真实感。

Method: 采用三掩码引导的RGB-D联合去噪架构，结合先验驱动的数据策展管线和高品质重光照对构建，以及参考条件化机制实现可控环境合成和道具替换。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在电影级动态视频合成任务上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端框架有效解决了视频合成中角色与环境双向交互建模的难题，在合成质量和物理一致性上超越了现有方法。

Original Abstract: Cinematic compositing aims to integrate green-screen characters into novel environments while maintaining physical and photometric realism. Previous methods often fail to capture the complex bidirectional interactions between characters and their surroundings, which we characterize as Character-to-Environment (C2E) physical interaction and Environment-to-Character (E2C) lighting harmonization. To address this, we propose an end-to-end video diffusion framework that jointly models C2E and E2C interactions, specifically handling the challenges of interactive props. Our approach introduces a tri-mask-guided architecture with RGB-D joint denoising to ensure physically consistent interactions among the character, props, and environment. We further develop an efficient prior-driven data curation pipeline to construct high-quality relighting pairs without expensive rendering. Finally, a reference-conditioned mechanism enables controllable environment synthesis and precise prop replacement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing methods in cinematic-quality dynamic video compositing.

中文摘要翻译: 电影级合成旨在将绿幕角色融入新环境，同时保持物理和光度真实感。以往方法往往无法捕捉角色与环境之间复杂的双向交互，我们将之定义为角色到环境（C2E）的物理交互和环境到角色（E2C）的光照协调。为此，我们提出一个端到端的视频扩散框架，联合建模C2E和E2C交互，特别是处理交互道具带来的挑战。我们的方法引入了一种三掩码引导的架构，结合RGB-D联合去噪，确保角色、道具和环境之间物理一致性的交互。我们进一步开发了高效的先验驱动数据策展管线，无需昂贵的渲染即可构建高质量的重光照对。最后，参考条件化机制实现了可控的环境合成和精确的道具替换。大量实验表明，我们的框架在电影级动态视频合成方面显著优于现有方法。

</details>


### [73] [BAFIS: Dataset + Framework to assess occupational Bias and Human Preference in modern Text-to-image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20241)
*Thomas Klassert,Adrian Ulges,Biying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种主流文生图模型（Midjourney v6.1、Stable Diffusion 3 Medium、DALL-E 3、Playground v2.5、FLUX.1-dev）在与职业相关的图像生成中的性别和种族偏见，通过构建BAFIS平台收集人类偏好反馈，发现模型存在系统性偏见，且传统评估指标与主观用户评分仅有部分相关性，强调了纳入人类偏好以开发更公平模型的必要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究表明图像生成模型受到偏见的显著影响，但缺乏对文生图模型中内在偏见和语言诱导偏见的系统评估，尤其是在职业相关图像生成场景下，需要结合人类偏好反馈来补充传统指标。

Method: 开发了"Battle-Arena for Fair Image Synthesis" (BAFIS) 平台用于收集人类对生成图像偏见的反馈；构建了包含21,140张多语言提示生成图像的合成数据集；对五种文生图模型进行全面评估，重点关注性别和种族偏见、图像质量及提示对齐度，并将结果与德国联邦就业局的官方统计数据进行比较。

Result: 文生图模型存在系统性偏见；传统评估指标与主观用户评分仅呈现部分相关性；通过与德国官方统计数据对比，将结果置于更广泛的社会背景中揭示了偏见的存在。

Conclusion: 研究强调了开发更公平、更具包容性的文生图模型需要纳入人类偏好反馈，而非仅依赖自动化评估指标。

Original Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has the potential to improve productivity and transform the production of creative content. However, existing research indicates that image generation models are significantly influenced by biases. This work investigates the inherent biases and language-induced biases present in text-to-image models within the context of occupation-related image generation, complementing established metrics with human preference feedback. We present a comprehensive evaluation of five current text-to-image models: Midjourney v6.1, Stable Diffusion 3 Medium, DALL-E 3, Playground v2.5, and FLUX.1-dev , focusing on gender and ethnicity bias, image quality, and prompt alignment. To facilitate this evaluation, we developed the "Battle-Arena for Fair Image Synthesis" (BAFIS), a platform designed to collect human feedback on bias in generated images. Furthermore, we created a dataset comprising 21,140 synthetic images generated using multilingual prompts, which serves as a basis for our analysis. We further place our results within a broader social context by comparing them to official statistics from the German Federal Employment Agency. Our findings reveal systematic biases in text-to-image models, with established evaluation metrics in partial correlation with subjective user ratings. Thus, our research emphasizes the need for including human preferences to develop fairer and more inclusive text-to-image models.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能具有提升生产力并转变创意内容生产的潜力。然而，现有研究表明图像生成模型受到偏见的显著影响。本文研究了文本到图像模型在与职业相关的图像生成场景中的内在偏见和语言诱导偏见，并通过人类偏好反馈补充了已有的评估指标。我们对五种当前主流的文本到图像模型进行了全面评估：Midjourney v6.1、Stable Diffusion 3 Medium、DALL-E 3、Playground v2.5 和 FLUX.1-dev，重点关注性别和种族偏见、图像质量以及提示对齐度。为促进这一评估，我们开发了"公平图像合成战场"（BAFIS）平台，用于收集关于生成图像偏见的人类反馈。此外，我们构建了一个包含21,140张使用多语言提示生成的合成图像数据集，作为分析的基础。我们进一步将结果与德国联邦就业局的官方统计数据进行比较，将其置于更广泛的社会背景中。我们的研究揭示了文本到图像模型中的系统性偏见，且既有评估指标与主观用户评分仅呈部分相关性。因此，本研究强调了纳入人类偏好以开发更公平、更具包容性的文本到图像模型的必要性。

</details>


### [74] [SPOT-E: Test-Time Entropy Shaping with Visual Spotlights for Frozen VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20244)
*Bo Yin,Xiaobin Hu,Chengming Xu,Ruolin Shen,Mo Yang,Jiangning Zhang,Peng-Tao Jiang,Cheng Tan,Shuicheng YAN*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPOT-E是一种即插即用的测试时方法，通过基于GRPO的轻量调优生成问题条件化的视觉高亮区域，利用低熵锚点和熵塑形目标降低答案不确定性，从而提升视觉语言模型在证据密集型任务上的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在证据密集型任务中表现不佳，因为关键的视觉证据往往微小、局部化且容易被忽略。现有的推理时视觉干预方法缺乏验证高亮证据是否被实际使用的闭环机制。同时，简单的熵最小化存在歧义——低熵可能来自基于证据的置信度，也可能来自捷径坍塌。

Method: 提出SPOT-E方法：研究答案跨度预测熵作为模型内部反馈信号；引入低熵锚点和熵塑形目标，在降低答案不确定性的同时保留基线高置信度token；通过基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的轻量级调优，每实例优化生成问题条件化的视觉高亮区域（spotlights）。

Result: 在多个基准测试和不同VLM家族上，SPOT-E均取得一致性的性能提升，并在视觉损坏条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPOT-E通过熵塑形策略解决了低熵歧义问题，作为一种即插即用的测试时方法，能够有效提升视觉语言模型在证据密集型任务中的证据读取能力，且无需重新训练。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often underperform on evidence intensive tasks because decisive visual evidence are small, localized, and easy to overlook, leading to failures in evidence readout even when high-level reasoning is intact. Prior inference-time visual interventions can improve grounding without retraining, but they are largely open-loop and lack a mechanism to verify whether highlighted evidence is actually used. We study answer-span prediction entropy as a model-internal feedback signal and show that naive entropy minimization is ambiguous, since low entropy may arise from evidence-grounded confidence or shortcut collapse. To resolve this ambiguity, we introduce low-entropy anchors and an entropy-shaping objective that reduces answer uncertainty while preserving baseline high-confidence tokens. We instantiate this principle in SPOT-E, a plug-and-play test-time method that produces question-conditioned spotlights, optimized per instance via light-weight tuning based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Across all benchmarks and different VLM families, SPOT-E yields consistent gains and improved robustness under visual corruptions. Code is publicly available at: \url{https://github.com/YinBo0927/SPOT-E}

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在证据密集型任务上往往表现不佳，因为关键性的视觉证据通常微小、局部化且容易被忽视，导致即使高层推理能力完好，证据读取仍会失败。先前的推理时视觉干预方法可以在不重新训练的情况下改善接地能力，但它们大多是开环的，缺乏验证高亮证据是否被实际使用的机制。我们研究了答案跨度预测熵作为模型内部反馈信号，并表明朴素的熵最小化存在歧义，因为低熵可能来源于基于证据的置信度，也可能来源于捷径坍塌。为解决这一歧义，我们引入了低熵锚点和熵塑形目标，在降低答案不确定性的同时保留基线的、高置信度的token。我们将这一原则实例化为SPOT-E，一种即插即用的测试时方法，能够生成问题条件化的高亮区域（spotlights），并基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）通过轻量级调优对每个实例进行优化。在所有的基准测试和不同VLM家族上，SPOT-E均取得了一致性的性能提升，并在视觉损坏条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性。代码已公开：\url{https://github.com/YinBo0927/SPOT-E}

</details>


### [75] [Single-Stage Hierarchical Rectification for Weakly Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20250)
*Duc T. Nguyen,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Ha DO,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种单阶段层次化纠错框架（SSHR），通过在前向传播中主动净化中间特征表示，无需多阶段流程即可生成高保真激活图，在计算病理学弱监督语义分割任务上超越现有方法并大幅缩短训练时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有计算病理学中的弱监督语义分割方法采用多阶段范式（CAM生成→离线伪掩膜细化→全监督重训练），存在训练成本高、误差传播（浅层CNN局部纹理偏差产生假阳性伪影，后续细化难以纠正）等根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出单阶段层次化纠错（SSHR）框架，核心是层次化特征纠错模块（HFRM），利用深层的全局语义上下文过滤浅层的局部异常，在前向传播中主动净化中间特征表示，直接在一个训练循环内生成高保真激活图。

Result: 在LUAD-HistoSeg和BCSS数据集上，SSHR超越了最先进的多阶段方法；同时训练时间缩短2~5倍，降低了计算开销，加速了大规模组织病理学工作流的临床转化。

Conclusion: 单阶段层次化纠错框架有效解决了多阶段弱监督语义分割中的误差传播和高计算成本问题，为计算病理学提供了一种高效且高性能的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Existing weakly supervised semantic segmentation (WSSS) methods in computational pathology rely on a multi-stage paradigm: class activation map (CAM) generation, offline pseudo-mask refinement, and fully supervised retraining. While established, this decoupled approach presents fundamental limitations. The multi-stage process not only incurs high computational training costs but also suffers from error propagation: local texture biases in shallow CNN layers generate false-positive artifacts that subsequent refinement steps often fail to correct. To address these persistent challenges through a simple yet highly effective approach, we propose the Single-Stage Hierarchical Rectification (SSHR) framework. Rather than passively refining CAMs post-hoc, our method proactively purifies intermediate feature representations during the forward pass. We introduce a Hierarchical Feature Rectification Module (HFRM) that utilizes deep global semantic context to filter out local anomalies in shallow layers. This mechanism generates high-fidelity activation maps directly within a single training loop. Experiments on the LUAD-HistoSeg and BCSS datasets demonstrate that SSHR outperforms state-of-the-art multi-stage methods. Furthermore, SSHR reduces training duration by 2 to 5 times. This efficiency minimizes computational overhead and accelerates clinical translation for large-scale histopathology workflows. The code is available at: https://github.com/trongduc-nguyen/SSHR

中文摘要翻译: 现有的计算病理学弱监督语义分割方法依赖于多阶段范式：类激活图生成、离线伪掩膜细化和全监督重训练。虽然这种方法已被广泛采用，但这种解耦的方式存在根本性缺陷。多阶段过程不仅带来高昂的计算训练成本，还会遭受误差传播：浅层CNN中的局部纹理偏差会产生假阳性伪影，而后续的细化步骤往往无法纠正这些错误。为了通过一种简单且高效的方法来解决这些长期存在的挑战，我们提出了单阶段层次化纠错框架。该方法并非被动地在事后细化CAM，而是在前向传播过程中主动净化中间特征表示。我们引入了层次化特征纠错模块，该模块利用深层的全局语义上下文来过滤浅层的局部异常。这一机制能够直接在一个训练循环内生成高保真激活图。在LUAD-HistoSeg和BCSS数据集上的实验表明，SSHR优于最先进的多阶段方法。此外，SSHR将训练时间缩短了2到5倍。这种高效性降低了计算开销，并加速了大规模组织病理学工作流的临床转化。代码可在https://github.com/trongduc-nguyen/SSHR获取。

</details>


### [76] [U$^2$Mamba: A Two-level Nested U-structure Mamba for Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20282)
*Junhui Li,Jialu Li,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出U²Mamba，一种基于Mamba的U型网络用于显著目标检测，通过多尺度Mamba U块（MMUBs）和嵌套U结构增强局部特征提取与上下文信息整合，并采用分层训练监督方法替代传统深度监督，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Mamba的显著目标检测模型未能充分探索上下文信息和整个架构的深度，导致局部特征提取能力和长距离依赖建模受限。

Method: 1) 提出多尺度Mamba U块（MMUBs）增强模型深度以提升局部特征提取能力；2) 设计嵌套U结构，融入MMUBs，整合浅层和深层的不同感受野以收集更丰富的上下文信息和更长范围的数据，且不受分辨率限制；3) 提出分层训练监督方法，在训练过程中对每一层级计算损失，替代传统的深度监督和顶层监督训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，U²Mamba在多个显著目标检测基准上取得了与最先进方法高度竞争的性能。

Conclusion: U²Mamba通过创新的U型网络结构、多尺度Mamba U块和分层训练监督，有效提升了显著目标检测的性能，验证了Mamba架构在该任务中的潜力。

Original Abstract: Mamba-based models have emerged as a promising alternative for salient object detection (SOD), offering significant advantages in modeling long sequences. However, existing models often fail to explore contextual information and the depth of the entire architecture. This paper introduces U$^2$Mamba, a powerful and innovative U-structured network for salient object detection. We propose multiscale Mamba U-blocks (MMUBs) that enhance the model depth to improve local feature extraction capabilities. Our newly developed nested U-structure, incorporating MMUBs, enables the network to integrate various receptive fields from shallow and deep layers, thereby collecting richer contextual information and longer-range data without being constrained by resolution. Instead of using the traditional deep supervision scheme and top-level supervised training, we propose a hierarchical training supervision method where the loss is computed at each level during the training process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that U$^2$Mamba achieves highly competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/JL021/U2Mamba}.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Mamba的模型已成为显著目标检测（SOD）领域一种有前途的替代方案，在长序列建模方面具有显著优势。然而，现有模型往往未能充分探索上下文信息以及整个架构的深度。本文提出U²Mamba，一种用于显著目标检测的强大且创新的U型结构网络。我们提出了多尺度Mamba U块（MMUBs），通过增强模型深度来提升局部特征提取能力。我们新开发的嵌套U结构融入了MMUBs，使网络能够整合来自浅层和深层的不同感受野，从而无需受分辨率限制即可收集更丰富的上下文信息和更长范围的数据。我们提出了一种分层训练监督方法，在训练过程中对每一层级计算损失，替代了传统的深度监督方案和顶层监督训练。大量实验表明，U²Mamba在多个基准上取得了与最先进方法高度竞争的性能。源代码可在\url{https://github.com/JL021/U2Mamba}获取。

</details>


### [77] [CMDS-AD: Cross-Modal Dual-Stream Decoupling for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20300)
*Junhao Cai,Deyu Zeng,Junhao Pang,Junyu Chen,Qiwei Liang,Xiaopin Zhong,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CMDS-AD提出跨模态双流异常检测框架，通过扩散模型生成正常样本并利用其低频滤波特性构建辅助估计流，结合坐标感知特征映射器和乘性评分机制，在极端的单样本设置下实现多模态异常检测的SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少样本多模态异常检测方法对所有特征进行空间一致处理，混淆了稳定的宏观结构与高频局部缺陷信号，加剧了跨模态错位并导致高误报率。

Method: 1) LoRA引导的扩散模型生成多样化的RGB样本缓解数据稀缺；2) 预训练扩散模型作为正态估计器，其非线性低通滤波特性提取低频正常表示，建立辅助估计流；3) 未压缩的真实流包含高低频分量，用于精确隔离微缺陷；4) 坐标感知层次特征映射器自适应对齐跨模态语义；5) 乘性评分机制过滤模态特定噪声。

Result: 在极端单样本设置下，MVTec 3D-AD上I-AUROC提升5.7%，AUPRO提升2.0%；EyeCandies上分别提升7.7%和5.6%，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 提出的双流框架有效解耦了低频结构信息与高频缺陷信号，显著提升了少样本多模态异常检测精度，验证了扩散模型作为非线性低通滤波器的实用价值。

Original Abstract: Few-shot anomaly detection remains challenging due to limited training data. Multi-modal anomaly detection (MAD) offers a viable solution, leveraging 3D geometric cues to enrich 2D RGB representations and compensate for this scarcity. However, existing MAD methods apply spatially uniform feature processing, conflating stable macroscopic structures with high-frequency localized defect signals, exacerbating cross-modal misalignment and inflating false-positive rates. To overcome this, we present CMDS-AD, a Cross-Modal Dual-Stream Anomaly Detection framework. A LoRA-guided diffusion model generates diverse RGB samples to mitigate extreme data scarcity. For 3D normal augmentation, we employ a pre-trained diffusion model as a normal estimator. Crucially, this estimator inherently acts as a non-linear low-pass filter, directly extracting low-frequency normal representations from RGB inputs. This establishes an auxiliary estimated stream of purely low-frequency information, anchoring robust structural templates and assisting the uncompressed real stream, containing coupled high- and low-frequency components, to precisely isolate micro-defects. A Coordinate-Aware Hierarchical Feature Mapper adaptively aligns cross-modal semantics, while a multiplicative scoring mechanism filters modality-specific noise. Under the extreme 1-shot setting, CMDS-AD achieves absolute performance gains of 5.7% (I-AUROC) and 2.0% (AUPRO) on MVTec 3D-AD, alongside 7.7% and 5.6% improvements on EyeCandies, establishing a new state-of-the-art.

中文摘要翻译: 少样本异常检测因训练数据有限仍具挑战。多模态异常检测（MAD）提供了一种可行方案，利用3D几何线索丰富2D RGB表示以弥补数据稀缺。然而，现有MAD方法对所有特征进行空间一致处理，混淆了稳定的宏观结构与高频局部缺陷信号，加剧了跨模态错位并导致误报率升高。为此，我们提出CMDS-AD，一种跨模态双流异常检测框架。LoRA引导的扩散模型生成多样化的RGB样本以缓解极端数据稀缺。对于3D正常增强，我们采用预训练扩散模型作为正态估计器。关键在于，该估计器本质上充当非线性低通滤波器，直接从RGB输入中提取低频正常表示。这建立了一个纯低频信息的辅助估计流，锚定稳健的结构模板，并辅助包含耦合高低频分量的未压缩真实流精确定位微缺陷。坐标感知层次特征映射器自适应对齐跨模态语义，乘性评分机制过滤模态特定噪声。在极端单样本设置下，CMDS-AD在MVTec 3D-AD上实现了I-AUROC提升5.7%、AUPRO提升2.0%的绝对性能增益，在EyeCandies上分别提升7.7%和5.6%，建立了新的最优水平。

</details>


### [78] [CUPID: Reconstructing UV Texture Maps for Interpretable Person-of-Interest Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20302)
*Giovanni Affatato,Sara Mandelli,Edoardo Daniele Cannas,Paolo Bestagini,Stefano Tubaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CUPID是一个针对特定人物（POI）的深度伪造视频检测器，结合UV纹理图和掩码自编码器（MAE），仅使用真实视频训练，无需伪造样本，在多个数据集上达到最优性能并具备强鲁棒性和可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有针对特定人物的深度伪造检测方法难以同时兼顾对后处理的鲁棒性、检测效率和可解释性，这三点是现代深度伪造检测器的核心需求。

Method: CUPID首先从视频帧中提取UV纹理图（基于3D人脸重建的面部外观表示），然后利用掩码自编码器（MAE）进行上下文引导重建，学习判别性面部特征表示。测试时，将查询视频的嵌入与真实参考视频进行匹配以判断真伪，并通过解码残差图突出显示与身份表示偏差最大的面部区域以实现可解释性。

Result: 在四个深度伪造数据集上，CUPID在多数数据集上超越当前最先进方法，在强下采样和压缩下具有最佳整体鲁棒性，且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: CUPID通过结合UV纹理图和MAE，在无需伪造样本或特定人物训练数据的前提下，实现了高性能、鲁棒、高效且可解释的特定人物深度伪造视频检测。

Original Abstract: Deepfakes targeting a high-profile individual, known as Person-of-Interest (POI), are a threat to modern democracies and societies. Current POI deepfake detection methods still struggle to combine robustness to post-processing, efficiency and interpretability, focal aspects of modern deepfake detectors. In this paper we propose CUPID, a POI video deepfake detector that combines UV texture maps, a facial appearance representation derived from 3D face reconstructions, with the representation learning capabilities of the Masked Autoencoder (MAE). Our method does not require any deepfake videos in its training phase. Moreover, it does not even require to include a specific POI in the training set: the combination of UV texture maps extracted from real video frames and the MAE context-guided reconstruction yields a latent space that captures rich and discriminative facial features also for identities unseen during training. In the testing phase, the embeddings extracted from a query video depicting the POI can be matched against pristine reference videos to assess the video authenticity. Furthermore, operating in the UV space naturally provides an additional layer of interpretability. Specifically, we can extract decoded residual maps that highlight which facial regions of a test video deviate most from the identity representation of the corresponding POI. Experiments on four deepfake datasets show that CUPID outperforms current state of the art on most datasets and achieves the best overall robustness against strong downscaling and compression, providing also substantially faster inference. Our experimental code will be released at https://github.com/polimi-ispl/CUPID.

中文摘要翻译: 针对高知名度人物（即特定人物，POI）的深度伪造对现代民主和社会构成威胁。当前POI深度伪造检测方法仍然难以兼顾对后处理的鲁棒性、效率和可解释性，而这些正是现代深度伪造检测器的核心关注点。本文提出CUPID，一种POI视频深度伪造检测器，它将UV纹理图（一种从3D人脸重建中导出的面部外观表示）与掩码自编码器（MAE）的表征学习能力相结合。我们的方法在训练阶段不需要任何深度伪造视频。此外，它甚至不需要在训练集中包含特定的POI：从真实视频帧中提取的UV纹理图与MAE上下文引导重建相结合，能够产生一个捕捉丰富且具有判别性面部特征的潜在空间，即使对于训练中未见过的身份也是如此。在测试阶段，从描述该POI的查询视频中提取的嵌入可以与真实参考视频进行匹配，以评估视频的真实性。此外，在UV空间中进行操作天然提供了额外的可解释性层。具体来说，我们可以提取解码残差图，突出显示测试视频中哪些面部区域与对应POI的身份表示偏差最大。在四个深度伪造数据集上的实验表明，CUPID在大多数数据集上超越了当前最先进方法，在强下采样和压缩下实现了最佳的整体鲁棒性，同时提供了显著更快的推理速度。我们的实验代码将在https://github.com/polimi-ispl/CUPID上发布。

</details>


### [79] [GEN-Guard: Correcting Generalization Failures for Deployable Federated Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20303)
*Julia Alekseenko,Pietro Mascagni,AI4SafeChole Consortium,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文识别了联邦学习中基于内部验证数据选择“最佳”全局模型会导致对未参与机构泛化失败（称为性能泄漏）的问题，并提出GEN-Guard框架，通过客户端封锁评估检测泛化失败和基于分歧感知蒸馏进行特征级校正，显著提升了跨机构鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准联邦学习评估仅依赖参与医院的验证数据选择全局模型，这会导致模型过拟合内部联邦数据，无法泛化到未见的医疗机构，即性能泄漏问题。该问题在手术视频AI中尚未被充分认识，亟需系统性的检测与纠正方法。

Method: 提出GEN-Guard框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）泛化检测——客户端封锁评估（CBE），通过在隔离的客户端分布上验证性能来防止性能泄漏；（2）泛化校正——分歧感知蒸馏（DAD），学习自适应特征级校正以增强跨机构鲁棒性。两者均在标准联邦学习收敛后运行，并支持对未见环境的零样本自适应。

Result: 在腹腔镜胆囊切除术的手术阶段识别和结肠镜息肉分割两个多中心临床任务上，标准评估下模型选择失败率超过80%。GEN-Guard将联邦内F1分数提升最多2个点，未见机构性能提升最多3个点，最差机构性能提升3-9个点。

Conclusion: 性能泄漏是联邦手术AI中一个系统性且此前未被充分认识的风险。GEN-Guard提供了一种实用的后验解决方案，能够有效检测和纠正此类泛化失败，通过提升跨机构鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力，增强了联邦学习在真实手术部署中的可靠性。

Original Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) in surgical video AI enables collaborative model training without sharing sensitive data. However, standard evaluation practices - selecting the "best" global model based only on validation data from participating hospitals - can lead to suboptimal deployment choices. We identify this critical failure mode as performance leakage, where the selected model overfits internal federation data and fails to generalize to unseen institutions. We propose GEN-Guard, a practical post-hoc framework to detect and correct generalization failures in federated surgical AI. It integrates Generalization Detection via Client-Blocked Evaluation (CBE), which validates performance on isolated client distributions to prevent performance leakage, and Generalization Correction through Disagreement-Aware Distillation (DAD), which learns adaptive feature-level corrections for cross-institutional robustness. Both components operate after standard FL convergence while providing robust support for zero-shot adaptation to unseen environments. We first quantify the severity of performance leakage, observing Model Selection Failures (MSFs) exceeding 80% under standard evaluation. GEN-Guard is evaluated on two multi-center clinical challenges: surgical phase recognition in laparoscopic cholecystectomy and polyp segmentation in colonoscopy. Across both datasets, GEN-Guard consistently corrects these failures, improving in-federation F1 scores by up to 2 points, unseen-institution performance by up to 3 points, and worst-case institutional performance by 3-9 points. Performance leakage represents a systematic and previously under-recognized risk in federated surgical AI. GEN-Guard provides a practical solution for detecting and correcting such failures. By improving cross-institutional robustness and zero-shot generalization, it strengthens the reliability of FL for real-world surgical deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习在手术视频AI中能够在不共享敏感数据的情况下实现协作模型训练。然而，标准的评估实践——仅基于参与医院的验证数据选择“最佳”全局模型——可能导致次优的部署选择。我们将这一关键失效模式识别为**性能泄漏**，即所选模型过拟合内部联邦数据，无法泛化到未见的医疗机构。我们提出**GEN-Guard**，一个实用的后验框架，用于检测和纠正联邦手术AI中的泛化失败。它整合了**基于客户端封锁评估的泛化检测**（CBE），通过在隔离的客户端分布上验证性能来防止性能泄漏，以及**基于分歧感知蒸馏的泛化校正**（DAD），学习自适应的特征级校正以增强跨机构鲁棒性。这两个组件均在标准联邦学习收敛后运行，同时为未见环境的零样本自适应提供稳健支持。我们首先量化了性能泄漏的严重程度，观察到标准评估下模型选择失败率超过80%。GEN-Guard在两个多中心临床任务上进行了评估：腹腔镜胆囊切除术中的手术阶段识别和结肠镜中的息肉分割。在两个数据集上，GEN-Guard均持续纠正这些失败，将联邦内F1分数提升最多2个点，未见机构性能提升最多3个点，最差机构性能提升3-9个点。性能泄漏是联邦手术AI中一个系统性且此前未被充分认识的风险。GEN-Guard提供了一种实用的解决方案，用于检测和纠正此类失败。通过提升跨机构鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力，它增强了联邦学习在真实手术部署中的可靠性。

</details>


### [80] [Through the PRISM: Preference Representation in Intermediate States of Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20310)
*Haoxuan Wu,Lai Man Po,Mengyang Liu,Kun Li,Hongzheng Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM 利用冻结的视频扩散骨干网络和轻量级查询聚合头，直接从噪声潜变量中解码偏好信号，实现了无需像素级奖励模型的视频生成评估，并支持早期最佳N采样以降低计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的基于像素的奖励模型评估视频生成存在两个问题：(1) 与噪声扩散过程脱节；(2) VAE解码成本高昂。本文旨在探索强大的视频生成器是否能够直接从噪声潜变量中判别偏好，从而绕过像素级解码。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，包含：(1) 冻结视频扩散骨干网络；(2) 轻量级查询聚合头（Query-based Aggregation head），用于从噪声潜变量中解码偏好信号。

Result: (1) 在偏好预测准确率上达到SOTA；(2) 具备强噪声鲁棒性，支持早期阶段的最佳N采样（Best-of-N sampling），可在去噪初期过滤次优候选；(3) 发现骨干网络的生成性能与其评估能力之间存在强正相关关系。

Conclusion: PRISM证明了从噪声潜变量中直接进行偏好评估是可行且高效的，该方法能显著降低计算开销、提升视频质量，并揭示了生成骨干网络具有自我改进的潜力。

Original Abstract: Evaluating video generation with clean, pixel-based reward models disconnects evaluation from the noisy diffusion process and incurs massive VAE decoding costs. In this paper, we challenge this paradigm by asking a fundamental question: Can a powerful video generator inherently discriminate preferences directly from noisy latents? To answer this, we introduce \textbf{PRISM} (\textbf{P}reference \textbf{R}epresentation in \textbf{I}ntermediate \textbf{S}tates of Diffusion \textbf{M}odels). PRISM employs a lightweight Query-based Aggregation head with a frozen video diffusion backbone to decode preference signals from noisy latents. Surprisingly, PRISM not only achieves SOTA preference accuracy but also unlocks strong noise-robustness, which enables early-stage Best-of-$N$ sampling. This allows for filtering suboptimal candidates at the very beginning of denoising, drastically reducing computation while boosting video quality. We also reveal a strong positive correlation between a backbone's generative performance and its inherent evaluative power, enabling self-improving video backbones.

中文摘要翻译: 使用基于干净像素的奖励模型评估视频生成，既与噪声扩散过程脱节，又会带来巨大的VAE解码成本。在本文中，我们通过提出一个基本问题来挑战这一范式：一个强大的视频生成器能否直接从噪声潜变量中内在地判别偏好？为了回答这个问题，我们引入了\textbf{PRISM}（扩散模型中间状态中的偏好表示）。PRISM采用一个轻量级的基于查询的聚合头，结合冻结的视频扩散骨干网络，从噪声潜变量中解码偏好信号。令人惊讶的是，PRISM不仅达到了最先进的偏好预测准确率，还解锁了强大的噪声鲁棒性，从而支持早期阶段的最佳N采样。这使得可以在去噪的最初阶段过滤掉次优候选，显著减少计算量同时提升视频质量。我们还揭示了骨干网络的生成性能与其内在评估能力之间存在强正相关关系，这为视频骨干网络的自我改进提供了可能。

</details>


### [81] [Reliability-Aware Prototype Calibration for Frozen Pose-Flow Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20312)
*Ning Dong,Yingna Su,Xin Dong,Ziyun Jiao,Xinnian Guo,Zhuangzhuang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对姿态流视频异常检测中单一似然分数可能掩盖多模态正常行为且对姿态观测噪声敏感的问题，提出了一种后验分数校准方法RPC（可靠性感知原型校准），在冻结检测器设置下，通过标准化最近原型偏差与流分数结合，并利用关键点置信度门控几何证据，提升了8个骨干-数据集组合上的帧级AUROC，平均提升2.03个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 姿态流视频异常检测器虽能提供基于似然排名的骨架窗口评分，但单一似然分数可能隐藏多模态正常行为模式，且对姿态观测噪声敏感。现有方法在冻结检测器设置下（姿态流骨干、缓存骨架轨迹和评估流水线固定）缺乏有效的后验校准手段。

Method: 提出可靠性感知原型校准（RPC），一种后验分数校准方法：在冻结的潜在空间中，将标准化最近原型偏差与标准化流分数相加，并仅使用关键点置信度来门控添加的几何证据。该方法在保留原始密度信号的同时，利用经验常态模式结构在姿态可靠性下校正排序。

Result: 在两个冻结姿态流骨干和四个数据集上，RPC在所有八个骨干-数据集对上都提升了帧级AUROC，提升幅度从0.34到4.49个百分点不等，平均提升2.03个百分点。消融和可靠性分析表明，原型偏差是主要的校正信号，而可靠性门控在姿态观测可信度较低时最为有用。

Conclusion: 轻量级后验校准可以在无法重训练或复现完整姿态流水线的情况下，有效增强缓存的姿态流系统，验证了在冻结检测器设置下通过原型偏差和可靠性门控进行分数校正的可行性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Pose-flow video anomaly detectors are attractive for one-class surveillance because they provide likelihood-based rankings for tracked skeleton windows. However, a single likelihood score may hide multimodal normal behavior and be sensitive to pose-observation noise. We study a frozen-detector setting in which the pose-flow backbone, cached skeleton tracks, and evaluation pipeline are fixed. Reliability-Aware Prototype Calibration (RPC) is a post-hoc score calibration method for this setting. It adds a standardized nearest-prototype deviation in the frozen latent space to the standardized flow score, and uses keypoint confidence only to gate this added geometric evidence. Thus, RPC preserves the original density signal while correcting the ranking with empirical normal-mode structure under pose reliability. Across two frozen pose-flow backbones and four datasets, RPC improves frame-level AUROC in all eight backbone-dataset pairs, with gains ranging from 0.34 to 4.49 percentage points and averaging 2.03 points. Ablation and reliability analyses show that prototype deviation is the main corrective signal, while reliability gating is most useful when pose observations are less trustworthy. These results suggest that lightweight post-hoc calibration can strengthen cached pose-flow systems when retraining or reproducing the full pose pipeline is impractical.

中文摘要翻译: 姿态流视频异常检测器因其能为跟踪的骨架窗口提供基于似然的排名，对于单类监控具有吸引力。然而，单一似然分数可能隐藏多模态正常行为，并且对姿态观测噪声敏感。我们研究了一种冻结检测器设置，其中姿态流骨干、缓存的骨架轨迹和评估流水线固定不变。可靠性感知原型校准（RPC）是适用于该设置的一种后验分数校准方法。它在冻结的潜在空间中将标准化的最近原型偏差与标准化的流分数相加，并仅使用关键点置信度来门控这一额外添加的几何证据。因此，RPC保留了原始的密度信号，同时利用姿态可靠性下的经验常态模式结构校正排序。在两个冻结的姿态流骨干和四个数据集上，RPC在所有八个骨干-数据集对上都提升了帧级AUROC，提升幅度从0.34到4.49个百分点不等，平均提升2.03个百分点。消融和可靠性分析表明，原型偏差是主要的校正信号，而可靠性门控在姿态观测可信度较低时最为有用。这些结果表明，当无法重新训练或复现完整姿态流水线时，轻量级后验校准能够增强缓存的姿态流系统。

</details>


### [82] [Geometry-Aware Superpixel Graph Transformer with Metadata for Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20390)
*Muhammad Azeem,Tanveer Hussain,Amr Ahmed,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于区域的图学习框架，将皮损建模为超像素区域图，通过边感知图变换器和元数据上下文节点实现多模态融合，在皮肤癌分类中超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CNN/ViT方法依赖全局或块级特征，且通常通过晚期融合方式整合患者元数据，缺乏空间层面的多模态推理能力，难以处理异质性病灶结构和微妙的类间差异。

Method: 将皮损图像分割为空间连贯的超像素区域，提取冻结CNN特征作为节点；编码区域间几何关系作为边属性；引入元数据上下文节点与所有区域节点相连，实现人口学/临床变量的结构化融合；使用边感知图变换器更新节点表示，通过注意力驱动传播得到图级嵌入用于分类。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上，显式的区域级关系建模和图原生多模态融合一致性地优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 建立了一种新的以图为中心的视角，将CNN特征建模为关系节点并通过上下文集成进行改进，从而获得更具表达力和鲁棒性的分类结果。

Original Abstract: Automated skin cancer classification from dermoscopic images remains challenging due to heterogeneous lesion structure, strong intra-class variability, and subtle visual differences between benign and malignant cases. Existing CNN/ViT pipelines typically rely on global or patch-level features and often combine patient metadata via late fusion, which limits spatially grounded multimodal reasoning. We present a novel region-based graph learning framework that explicitly models lesions as graphs of spatially coherent superpixel regions represented as frozen CNN features. To capture fine-grained lesion arrangements, we encode inter-regional geometry as edge attributes and introduce a dedicated metadata context node connected to all regions, providing structured integration of demographic/clinical variables within the same relational space. Node representations are updated using our edge-aware graph transformer followed by attention-driven propagation, and a final graph-level embedding for benign-malignant classification. Experiments on four public benchmarks demonstrate that explicit region-level relational modeling and graph-native multimodal fusion yield consistent gains over the state-of-the-art. Consequently, we establish a new graph-centric perspective in which CNN features are modeled as relational nodes and improved through contextual integration, yielding more expressive and robust classifications.

中文摘要翻译: 由于病灶结构的异质性、强烈的类内变异以及良恶性病例之间细微的视觉差异，基于皮肤镜图像的自动化皮肤癌分类仍然具有挑战性。现有的CNN/ViT流程通常依赖全局或块级特征，并通常通过晚期融合方式整合患者元数据，这限制了空间层面的多模态推理能力。我们提出了一种新颖的基于区域的图学习框架，该框架将病灶显式建模为以冻结CNN特征表示的空间连贯超像素区域图。为捕捉细粒度的病灶排列，我们将区域间几何关系编码为边属性，并引入一个专门的元数据上下文节点连接到所有区域节点，从而在同一关系空间内实现人口学/临床变量的结构化整合。节点表示通过我们的边感知图变换器进行更新，随后进行注意力驱动的传播，最终得到用于良恶性分类的图级嵌入。在四个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，显式的区域级关系建模和图原生多模态融合一致性地优于现有最先进方法。因此，我们建立了一种新的以图为中心的视角，其中CNN特征被建模为关系节点并通过上下文集成得到改进，从而产生更具表达力和鲁棒性的分类结果。

</details>


### [83] [FlowBender: Feedback-Aware Training for Self-Correcting Conditional Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20404)
*Daniel Gilo,Sven Elflein,Ido Sobol,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowBender 提出一种闭环框架，将条件扩散/流模型在推理时产生的对齐误差作为一等输入，训练网络学习基于反馈的校正策略，从而在不牺牲生成质量的前提下显著提升条件约束的满足度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有条件扩散/流模型在训练时仅将条件信号作为静态线索，推理时忽略对齐信息；而基于引导的方法使用手工设计的线性更新，往往在保真度与生成合理性之间权衡。核心问题是模型从未被训练去利用自身的对齐误差。

Method: 提出 FlowBender 闭环框架：每步先进行一次无引导的 look-ahead 前向传递估计干净信号，通过前向算子计算任务特定的偏差，然后由精炼传递消费该偏差信号以产生校正后的速度。包含基于梯度的变体（适用于可微算子）和零阶变体（适用于 JPEG 压缩等非可微场景）。还引入 prior-step shortcut 以最小额外计算开销实现闭环校正。

Result: 在图像到图像翻译、图像恢复和3D网格纹理映射等任务上，FlowBender 一致优于标准监督基线、对齐损失增强训练以及最先进的推理时引导方法，同时提升了保真度和生成合理性，而非两者之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 将推理时的对齐误差显式引入训练过程可以同时改善条件约束的满足度和生成样本的质量，为条件生成模型的训练范式提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Conditional diffusion and flow models routinely fail to satisfy the very constraints that define their task. For instance, a depth-conditioned model often produces images whose re-extracted depth disagrees with the input, even though the forward operator--the depth predictor defining the constraint--is available during both training and inference. Existing approaches generally fall into two categories: supervised models that treat the conditioning signal as a static cue and ignore alignment information at inference, and guidance-based methods that consult it through hand-tuned linear updates, typically trading fidelity to the condition against the plausibility of the generated sample. We argue that the fundamental gap in both paradigms is that the model is never trained to utilize its own alignment error. We introduce FlowBender, a closed-loop framework that treats this error as a first-class input, training the network to learn a correction policy conditioned on inference-time feedback. At each step, an unguided look-ahead pass estimates the clean signal, a task-specific deviation is computed via the forward operator, and a refinement pass consumes this signal to produce a corrected velocity. We propose several variants of FlowBender, including a gradient-based formulation for differentiable operators and a zero-order variant for non-differentiable settings such as JPEG compression. For efficient sampling, we introduce a prior-step shortcut that enables closed-loop correction at a minimal additional computational cost. Across image-to-image translation, restoration, and 3D mesh texturing, FlowBender consistently outperforms standard supervised baselines, alignment-loss-augmented training, and state-of-the-art inference-time guidance, improving fidelity and plausibility simultaneously rather than trading them against each other. Project page: https://flow-bender.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 条件扩散模型和流模型通常无法满足定义其任务的那些约束条件。例如，一个深度条件模型生成的图像，其重新提取的深度往往与输入不一致，尽管前向算子——定义约束条件的深度预测器——在训练和推理过程中都是可用的。现有方法通常分为两类：一类是监督模型，将条件信号视为静态线索并在推理时忽略对齐信息；另一类是基于引导的方法，通过手工调整的线性更新来使用该信息，通常需要在条件保真度和生成样本的合理性之间进行权衡。我们认为这两种范式的根本差距在于，模型从未被训练去利用自身的对齐误差。我们提出了 FlowBender，一个闭环框架，将该误差作为一等输入，训练网络学习一种基于推理时反馈的校正策略。在每一步，一个无引导的前瞻传递估计出干净信号，通过前向算子计算任务特定的偏差，然后一个精炼传递消费该信号以产生校正后的速度。我们提出了 FlowBender 的几种变体，包括适用于可微算子的基于梯度的公式，以及适用于 JPEG 压缩等非可微场景的零阶变体。为了实现高效采样，我们引入了一种前步捷径，使得闭环校正的额外计算成本极低。在图像到图像翻译、图像恢复和3D网格纹理映射任务上，FlowBender 一致优于标准监督基线、对齐损失增强训练以及最先进的推理时引导方法，同时提升了保真度和生成合理性，而非在两者之间进行权衡。项目页面：https://flow-bender.github.io/

</details>


### [84] [Spectral Query-Key Product Weight Steering for Training-Free VLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20419)
*Karn Tiwari,Varnith Chordia,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QK Product Steering方法，通过直接编辑注意力头中的查询-键（QK）乘积来抑制物体幻觉，无需数据、训练或推理额外开销，在多个VLM上平均降低4.0% CHAIR$_s$指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）经常生成流畅但视觉上无依据的描述，尤其是提及图像中不存在的物体（物体幻觉）。现有缓解方法通常需要额外数据、微调或推理时开销，缺乏轻量级解决方案。

Method: 通过编辑每头查询-键（QK）乘积，抑制选定中间层中少数主导奇异模态，再将编辑后的乘积通过闭式查询-only更新映射回查询权重（保持共享键权重不变），从而兼容分组查询注意力。进一步将QK乘积分解为对称分量（互内容相似性模式）和反对称分量（方向性注意力模式），定位幻觉信号主要存在于对称互注意力通道中。

Result: 在三个基于GQA的VLM上，QK Product Steering实现平均相对CHAIR$_s$降低4.0%，而随机模态控制实验显示几乎无变化。消融实验表明幻觉信号特异于主导QK模态，且主要定位在对称互注意力通道中。

Conclusion: QK Product Steering提供了一种简单的替代解码时缓解方法的方案，无需额外数据、微调或推理时开销，同时基本保持多模态能力。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often generate fluent but visually unsupported descriptions, especially by mentioning objects absent from the image. We propose QK Product Steering, a data-free, training-free, and zero-inference-cost weight edit for reducing object hallucination. The method directly edits the per-head query-key product, the operator that produces pre-softmax attention logits, by suppressing a small number of dominant singular modes in selected middle layers. The edited product is then mapped back to the query weights through a closed-form query-only update while keeping shared key weights fixed, making the edit compatible with grouped-query attention. We further decompose the QK product into symmetric and antisymmetric components to distinguish mutual content-similarity patterns from directional attention patterns. Across three GQA-based VLMs, QK Product Steering achieves an average relative CHAIR$_s$ reduction of $4.0\%$, while matched random-mode controls show negligible change. Interpretability ablations show that the hallucination signal is specific to dominant QK modes and is primarily localized to the symmetric mutual-attention channel. Overall, QK Product Steering offers a simple alternative to decoding-time mitigation, requiring no additional data, fine-tuning, or inference-time overhead while largely preserving general multimodal capability.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）常常生成流畅但缺乏视觉支持的描述，特别是提及图像中不存在的物体（物体幻觉）。我们提出QK乘积引导（QK Product Steering），一种无数据、无训练、零推理成本的权重编辑方法，用于减少物体幻觉。该方法直接编辑每头查询-键乘积（即产生softmax之前注意力逻辑分数的算子），通过抑制选定中间层中少数主导奇异模态来实现。随后，将编辑后的乘积通过闭式查询-only更新映射回查询权重，同时保持共享的键权重固定，从而使该编辑兼容分组查询注意力。我们进一步将QK乘积分解为对称分量和反对称分量，以区分互内容相似性模式与方向性注意力模式。在三个基于GQA的VLM上，QK Product Steering实现了平均相对CHAIR$_s$降低4.0%，而匹配的随机模态控制实验显示出可忽略的变化。可解释性消融实验表明，幻觉信号特异于主导QK模态，且主要定位于对称互注意力通道中。总体而言，QK Product Steering提供了一种简单的替代解码时缓解方法的方案，无需额外数据、微调或推理时开销，同时基本保持通用多模态能力。

</details>


### [85] [InfantFace: Detecting infant faces in neonatal clinical environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20449)
*Abdullah Bin-Obaid,Maria M. Cobo,Rebeccah Slater,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLOv11m的单阶段新生儿面部检测模型，通过多数据集预训练和临床域微调，在新生儿临床环境中实现了高精度面部检测（AP50达0.96），并指出了缺乏公开新生儿数据集对领域发展的制约。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 新生儿临床环境中存在背景杂乱、光照变化、遮挡（医疗设备、监测仪器等）等挑战，导致现有通用面部检测模型精度下降。可靠的新生儿面部定位是基于视频的非接触式评估（如疼痛表情分析、心肺信号提取、呼吸停止警报）的第一步，因此需要针对新生儿临床环境专门优化的面部检测模型。

Method: 采用单阶段YOLOv11m模型，先在多个公开数据集（VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB、WIDER FACE）上预训练，再使用包含228个视频、114次录制、113名独立婴儿的新生儿研究数据集进行微调（临床域自适应）。

Result: 微调前模型在AP50指标上达到0.87，超越三种最先进的通用面部检测器；经过临床域自适应微调后，AP50进一步提升至0.96。

Conclusion: 所提出的新生儿面部检测模型在临床环境中表现优异，但跨数据集评估仍受限于缺乏公开的新生儿数据集。优先创建此类数据集（同时保障隐私和伦理标准）将极大推动该领域进展。

Original Abstract: Reliable localisation of the neonatal face is the first step for several video-camera based non-contact assessments such as pain and distress related facial expression analysis, pain scoring, cardiorespiratory signal extraction and cessation of breathing alerts. However, major challenges persist in neonatal clinical environments. Cluttered backgrounds, illumination changes and poor lighting conditions can reduce the accuracy of face detection models. Clinical interventions, monitoring equipment and, in some cases, medical devices can obstruct the face, making visual assessment difficult. We propose a one-stage YOLOv11m-based model tailored for face detection of infants in neonatal clinical environments. We combined multiple publicly available datasets (VGGFace2, CelebA, FDDB, WIDER FACE) to train and evaluate our proposed model. We then fine-tuned our model on a neonatal research dataset involving 228 videos from 114 recording sessions of 113 independent infants. Before fine-tuning, our model achieved an AP50 of 0.87, surpassing the performance of three state-of-the-art general face detectors. Performance improved further to an AP50 of 0.96 after clinical-domain adaptation. Evaluating face detection performance across different datasets remains a challenge due to the lack of publicly available neonatal datasets. Prioritising the creation of such datasets, while upholding appropriate privacy safeguards and ethical standards in their creation and use, would greatly support further progress in this field.

中文摘要翻译: 新生儿面部的可靠定位是基于视频摄像的非接触式评估的第一步，这些评估包括疼痛与痛苦相关的面部表情分析、疼痛评分、心肺信号提取以及呼吸停止警报。然而，新生儿临床环境中存在重大挑战。杂乱的背景、光照变化和不良照明条件会降低面部检测模型的准确性。临床干预、监测设备以及在某些情况下的医疗器械可能遮挡面部，使视觉评估变得困难。我们提出了一种基于YOLOv11m的单阶段模型，专门用于新生儿临床环境中的婴儿面部检测。我们结合了多个公开数据集（VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB、WIDER FACE）来训练和评估所提出的模型。然后，我们在一个包含114次录制会话中228个视频（涉及113名独立婴儿）的新生儿研究数据集上对模型进行微调。在微调之前，我们的模型达到了0.87的AP50，超越了三种最先进的通用面部检测器的性能。经过临床域自适应后，性能进一步提升至0.96的AP50。由于缺乏公开的新生儿数据集，跨数据集评估面部检测性能仍然具有挑战性。优先创建此类数据集，同时在其创建和使用中维护适当的隐私保护措施和伦理标准，将极大地支持该领域的进一步进展。

</details>


### [86] [PCFootprint: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Vectorized Building Footprint Extraction from Aerial LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20455)
*Haoyuan Shen,Kuihao Wang,Ruisheng Wang,Yujun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCFootprint，首个大规模航空激光雷达点云建筑足迹提取公共数据集，包含33,000个瓦片，并建立了全面的基准测试，揭示了高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于光学影像的方法易受遮挡、透视畸变和残余地形位移影响，导致足迹提取不完整或不对齐，且缺乏高程信息，限制了其在建筑物精细建模中的应用。因此需要从激光雷达点云中提取建筑足迹的数据集和方法。

Method: 构建了包含33,000个瓦片的大规模航空激光雷达点云数据集PCFootprint，每个瓦片128×128米，并配准了矢量化建筑足迹。设立了包含3,000个瓦片的跨域测试集，并评估了主流方法作为基准。

Result: 实验结果表明，在复杂地理环境中存在高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等显著挑战。

Conclusion: PCFootprint将推动建筑建模、城市场景理解和地理空间分析领域的未来研究。

Original Abstract: Building footprint extraction is a fundamental task in photogrammetry, remote sensing, and computer vision. Recent image-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in extracting vectorized footprints from high-resolution optical imagery. However, optical imagery inherently susceptible to occlusions, perspective distortions, and residual relief displacement, yielding incomplete or misaligned footprint extraction. Furthermore, the lack of explicit elevation information limits its direct applicability to Level of Detail building modeling. In this paper, we present PCFootprint, the first large-scale public dataset for footprint extraction from airborne laser scanning point clouds. PCFootprint comprises \num{33000} tiles derived from the Estonian Land and Spatial Development Board, covering diverse urban and rural landscapes. Each tile spans \qtyproduct{128 x 128}{\m} with systematically aligned vectorized footprints aligned to point clouds. The dataset includes a \num{3000} tiles cross-domain test set for evaluating generalization across geographic regions. We establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating mainstream methods. Experimental results reveal significant challenges including high intra-class variance, data imbalance, and noise across complex geospatial environments. We believe PCFootprint will advance future research in building modeling, urban scene understanding, and geospatial analysis. The PCFootprint dataset is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Haoyuan-Shen/PCFootprint}.

中文摘要翻译: 建筑足迹提取是摄影测量、遥感与计算机视觉中的一项基础任务。近期基于图像的方法在高分辨率光学影像的矢量化足迹提取方面取得了显著进展。然而，光学影像固有地易受遮挡、透视畸变和残余地形位移的影响，导致足迹提取不完整或不对齐。此外，缺乏显式高程信息限制了其在建筑物细节层次建模中的直接适用性。本文提出了PCFootprint，这是首个面向机载激光扫描点云建筑足迹提取的大规模公共数据集。PCFootprint包含来自爱沙尼亚土地与空间发展委员会的33,000个瓦片，覆盖多样的城市与乡村景观。每个瓦片面积为128×128米，并配有与点云系统对齐的矢量化建筑足迹。该数据集包含3,000个瓦片的跨域测试集，用于评估跨地理区域的泛化能力。我们通过评估主流方法建立了全面的基准测试。实验结果表明，在复杂地理环境中存在高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等显著挑战。我们相信PCFootprint将推动建筑建模、城市场景理解和地理空间分析领域的未来研究。PCFootprint数据集已在https://huggingface.co/datasets/Haoyuan-Shen/PCFootprint公开提供。

</details>


### [87] [Scalable Training of Spatially Grounded 2D Vision-Language Models for Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20477)
*Yusuf Salcan,Simon Ging,Robin Schirrmeister,Philipp Arnold,Elmar Kotter,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Thomas Brox*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于放射学视觉语言模型的多任务框架，通过自动构建的大规模双语数据集 RefRad2D（包含120万CT/MR图像-文本对），训练模型 RadGrounder 同时完成报告生成、视觉问答和空间定位，并证明空间定位监督不会降低语言生成质量，且临床数据具有良好的迁移性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有放射学视觉语言模型通常需要人工空间标注，成本高昂且难以规模化。本文旨在探索无需手动空间标注即可训练具备空间定位能力的放射学VLM，同时验证空间定位与语言生成任务之间是否存在性能权衡。

Method: 1) 构建 RefRad2D 双语数据集，包含120万对CT/MR图像-文本，通过LLM自动策展和自动分割生成任务特定的VQA和空间定位子集；2) 提出 RadGrounder 模型，联合训练报告生成、视觉问答和空间定位（边界框检测或分割）三个任务。

Result: 1) 在外部VQA基准（Slake, VQA-RAD）上，RadGrounder 取得了与专用医学VLM相竞争的结果；2) 将临床数据加入训练混合能提升开放域VQA性能，优于仅在下游数据集上微调；3) 加入空间定位监督不会降低语言生成质量，可在不牺牲VQA性能的情况下获得可空间验证的输出。

Conclusion: 无需手动空间标注即可训练具备空间定位能力的放射学VLM，空间定位与语言生成任务可以兼容而非冲突，且大规模临床预训练数据具有良好的跨任务迁移能力。

Original Abstract: We study how to train visually grounded vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology without manual spatial annotations. We introduce RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual (German/English) dataset of 1.2M CT and MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with task-specific VQA and spatial grounding subsets generated automatically via LLM-based curation and automated segmentation. Trained on this data, our model RadGrounder jointly performs report generation, visual question answering, and spatial grounding via bounding-box detection or segmentation. On external VQA benchmarks (Slake, VQA-RAD), RadGrounder achieves competitive results with specialized medical VLMs. Adding our clinical data to the training mixture improves open-ended VQA over fine-tuning on the downstream datasets alone, showing the transferability of our dataset. Crucially, adding grounding supervision does not degrade language quality, enabling spatially verifiable outputs at no cost to VQA performance.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究如何在不依赖手动空间标注的情况下训练用于放射学的视觉语言模型（VLM）。我们引入了 RefRad2D，这是一个大规模双语（德语/英语）数据集，包含来自临床实践的120万对CT和MR图像-文本对，并通过基于LLM的自动策展和自动分割生成任务特定的VQA和空间定位子集。在该数据上训练的模型 RadGrounder 联合执行报告生成、视觉问答以及通过边界框检测或分割进行的空间定位。在外部VQA基准（Slake, VQA-RAD）上，RadGrounder 取得了与专用医学VLM相竞争的结果。将我们的临床数据加入训练混合中，相比仅在下游数据集上微调，能提升开放域VQA性能，证明了我们数据集的可迁移性。关键在于，添加空间定位监督并不会降低语言质量，从而在不牺牲VQA性能的前提下实现了可空间验证的输出。

</details>


### [88] [How Fragile Are Training-Free AI-Generated Image Detectors? A Controlled Audit of Score Direction, Preprocessing, and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20488)
*Jingwen Zhou,Mingzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了两种无训练AI生成图像检测方法（自编码器重建分数和噪声扰动特征相似度分数），发现其性能差异主要源于实现细节而非方法本质，分数方向依赖于超参数选择，且数据集格式偏差会虚增鲁棒性声明。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无训练AI生成图像检测器虽宣称可免训练部署，但不同方法的性能报告缺乏统一的受控对比协议，导致难以评估其真实有效性。

Method: 在统一的GenImage衍生基准（1500张图像，覆盖7个生成器及JPEG压缩质量70/50）上，审计两种代表性无训练分数（自编码器重建分数AEROBLADE风格、噪声扰动特征相似度分数RIGID风格）以及朴素特征kNN基线，系统控制实现细节、超参数和数据集格式等变量。

Result: (i) 实现细节（LPIPS骨干网络、预处理方式）可造成高达0.38 AUROC的性能翻转；(ii) RIGID分数的方向随噪声水平sigma变化，在不同生成器和sigma值下AUROC可从<0.5逆转为>0.5乃至崩溃至0.15；(iii) 数据集格式偏差（未统一重编码）使JPEG-50下的AUROC优于干净条件，偏差校正后异常仅位于BigGAN；各分数失败集互补但简单z-score融合不及最优单分数。

Conclusion: 无训练检测器的性能高度依赖于实现细节、超参数和数据集预处理，分数互补性的利用需要方向感知的组合策略，现有报告的鲁棒性声明可能被格式偏差夸大。

Original Abstract: Training-free detectors of AI-generated images promise generator-agnostic deployment without classifier training, yet their reported numbers are rarely compared under a single controlled protocol. We audit two representative training-free scores -- an autoencoder-reconstruction score (AEROBLADE-style) and a noise-perturbation feature-similarity score (RIGID-style) -- plus a naive feature-kNN control, on a common 1,500-image GenImage-derived benchmark spanning seven generators and JPEG compression at quality 70 and 50. The audit yields three cautionary findings. (i) Implementation details masquerade as method differences: replacing the LPIPS backbone (AlexNet -> VGG-16) changes overall AUROC by +0.085, and switching between resize-to-512 and native-resolution preprocessing flips per-generator conclusions by up to 0.38 AUROC. (ii) Score direction is not a property of the method but of its hyperparameters: the RIGID-style score is inverted (AUROC < 0.5) on SD1.5 and Wukong at noise level sigma=0.05, recovers to >0.5 for every generator at sigma=0.01, and collapses to 0.15 at sigma=0.3. (iii) Dataset format bias inflates robustness claims: without unified re-encoding, AUROC under JPEG-50 exceeds the clean condition for the AlexNet-backbone reconstruction score; after bias correction the residual anomaly localizes to a single generator (BigGAN). The audited scores have complementary per-generator failure sets, but naive z-score fusion does not beat the best single score, indicating that exploiting complementarity requires direction-aware combination.

中文摘要翻译: 无训练的人工智能生成图像检测器承诺无需分类器训练即可实现生成器无关的部署，然而其报告的性能指标很少在统一的受控协议下进行比较。我们在一个统一的GenImage衍生基准（包含1500张图像，涵盖七个生成器以及质量70和50的JPEG压缩）上，审计了两种代表性的无训练分数——一种自编码器重建分数（AEROBLADE风格）和一种噪声扰动特征相似度分数（RIGID风格）——外加一个朴素的特征kNN对照。该审计得出了三点警示性发现。(i) 实现细节伪装成方法差异：将LPIPS骨干网络（从AlexNet更换为VGG-16）使整体AUROC变化+0.085，而切换为resize到512与原始分辨率预处理则使各生成器结论翻转高达0.38 AUROC。(ii) 分数方向并非方法的固有属性，而是其超参数的特性：RIGID风格分数在噪声水平sigma=0.05时在SD1.5和Wukong上出现反转（AUROC < 0.5），在sigma=0.01时对每个生成器恢复至>0.5，并在sigma=0.3时崩溃至0.15。(iii) 数据集格式偏差虚增了鲁棒性声明：未经统一重编码时，JPEG-50下的AUROC在AlexNet骨干重建分数上超过了干净条件；偏差校正后残差异常仅定位于单个生成器（BigGAN）。被审计的分数具有互补的各生成器失败集，但朴素z-score融合未能超越最佳单一分数，表明利用互补性需要方向感知的组合策略。

</details>


### [89] [FreeStyle: Free Control of Style-Content Dual-Reference Generation from Community LoRA Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20506)
*Jinghong Lan,Wei Cheng,Yunuo Chen,Ziqi Ye,Peng Xing,Yixiao Fang,Rui Wang,Yufeng Yang,Xuanyang Zhang,Xianfang Zeng,Difan Zou,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FreeStyle，一种基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考生成框架，通过两阶段课程学习（注意力富集约束+频率感知RoPE调制）解决内容泄漏问题，并在多维度基准上实现风格对齐、内容保持与泄漏抑制的强平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前风格-内容双参考生成任务面临三大瓶颈：缺乏大规模、内容-风格干净分离的长尾三元组数据；模型难以平衡内容保真度、风格对齐与指令遵循；风格参考中语义泄漏问题严重。

Method: 1) 基于社区LoRA挖掘构建大规模风格-内容参考三元组；2) 两阶段课程学习：风格迁移阶段采用注意力富集约束抑制风格参考泄漏，双参考阶段采用频率感知RoPE调制策略抑制基于位置对应的泄漏；3) 提出新基准，包含风格不变内容对齐分数(CAS)和基于VLM的校准拒绝分数。

Result: 实验表明模型在风格对齐、内容保持和泄漏抑制之间达到了强平衡，并且所提出的基准和评估指标有效衡量了生成可靠性与泄漏抑制能力。

Conclusion: FreeStyle通过社区LoRA挖掘和大规模三元组构建解决了数据瓶颈，两阶段解耦机制有效抑制了不同类型的语义泄漏，为双参考生成提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Style-content dual-reference generation aims to synthesize an image that preserves the structure and semantics of a content reference while adopting the style of a separate style reference.Despite recent progress, this setting remains challenging because models must balance content fidelity, style alignment, and instruction following avoiding semantic leakage from the style reference.A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale triplet data with clean content-style separation and broad long-tail style coverage.In this work, we propose FreeStyle, a scalable dual-reference generation framework based on community LoRA mining.We treat community LoRAs as compositional anchors for style and content, and design a rigorous generation and filtering pipeline to construct large-scale Style-Reference and Content-Reference triplets across multiple base models.To address content leakage, we adopt a two-stage curriculum with stage-specific disentanglement mechanisms: an attention-level enrichment constraint that suppresses style-reference leakage in the style-transfer stage, and a frequency-aware RoPE modulation strategy that targets positional-correspondence-based leakage in the harder dual-reference stage.We also introduce a benchmark covering both style-reference and dual-reference generation, with evaluations on style similarity, content preservation, aesthetics, instruction following, and leakage rejection.
