The benchmark incorporates a style-invariant Content Alignment Score (CAS) and introduces a calibrated VLM-based Rejection Score for evaluating generation reliability and leakage suppression.Extensive experiments show that our model achieves a strong balance among style alignment, content preservation, and leakage suppression.

中文摘要翻译: 风格-内容双参考生成旨在合成一张图像，使其保留内容参考图的结构和语义，同时采用另一张风格参考图的风格。尽管近期取得了一定进展，但该设置仍具挑战性，因为模型必须在内容保真度、风格对齐和指令遵循之间取得平衡，同时避免风格参考中的语义泄漏。一个关键瓶颈是缺乏大规模、内容-风格干净分离且覆盖广泛长尾风格的三元组数据。本文提出FreeStyle，一种基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考生成框架。我们将社区LoRA视为风格和内容的组合锚点，并设计了一套严格的生成与过滤流水线，在多个基座模型上构建大规模风格参考和内容参考三元组。为解决内容泄漏问题，我们采用两阶段课程学习及阶段特定的解耦机制：在风格迁移阶段引入注意力富集约束以抑制风格参考泄漏，在难度更大的双参考阶段引入频率感知RoPE调制策略以抑制基于位置对应的泄漏。我们还引入了一个覆盖风格参考和双参考生成的新基准，评估风格相似度、内容保持度、美学质量、指令遵循度和泄漏抑制能力。该基准包含风格不变内容对齐分数(CAS)，并引入了经过校准的基于VLM的拒绝分数，用于评估生成可靠性和泄漏抑制效果。大量实验表明，我们的模型在风格对齐、内容保持和泄漏抑制之间达到了强平衡。

</details>


### [90] [S-Agent: Spatial Tool-Use Elicits Reasoning for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20515)
*Yalun Dai,Hao Li,Shulin Tian,Runmao Yao,Yuhao Dong,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Fangfu Liu,Baoliang Tian,Dingwen Zhang,Tao Wang,Kim-Hui Yap,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S-Agent，一种空间工具使用智能体范式，通过将空间推理建模为时空证据累积过程，结合层级化空间工具集和时序记忆机制，以无需训练的方式提升开源/闭源VLM的多视图和视频空间推理能力，并基于生成的S-300K轨迹微调出紧凑的S-Agent-8B模型，性能媲美GPT-5.4等闭源模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM和工具增强智能体大多局限于静态、无状态的孤立视觉观测推理，缺乏对连续、演化的3D世界的空间智能理解能力，无法进行跨帧的时空证据累积。

Method: 将VLM作为语义规划器决定所需证据，层级化空间工具集负责2D目标 grounding、3D几何证据提升以及高级空间知识聚合；同时引入时序记忆机制（Scene Memory维护场景状态，Agent Memory累积推理上下文），实现跨帧和跨推理步骤的证据整合。

Result: 在多视图和视频空间推理基准上，S-Agent以无需训练的方式一致提升开源和闭源VLM性能；基于S-Agent生成的S-300K空间轨迹进行SFT得到的S-Agent-8B显著超越同规模基线（如Qwen3-VL-8B），性能媲美GPT-5.4和Gemini 3等先进闭源模型。

Conclusion: 将空间推理从帧级预测重塑为场景中心理解的时空证据累积范式是有效的，层级化工具集和时序记忆机制是实现这一范式的关键组件，且生成的推理轨迹可用于训练更紧凑且高性能的空间智能体。

Original Abstract: Real-world spatial intelligence requires reasoning over a continuous and evolving 3D world, yet existing VLMs and tool-augmented agents largely remain tied to static, stateless inference from isolated visual observations. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}, a spatial tool-use agentic paradigm for understanding and reasoning over continuous multi-view images and videos. By formulating spatial reasoning as spatio-temporal evidence accumulation rather than isolated frame-level prediction, \textsc{S-Agent} reshapes spatial perception into scene-centric understanding beyond frame-centric recognition. Specifically, \textsc{S-Agent} casts the VLM as a semantic planner that decides what evidence is needed, while a hierarchy of spatial tools and experts grounds objects in 2D, lifts them into 3D geometric evidence, and aggregates this evidence into high-level spatial knowledge (\textit{e.g.}, counting, measurement, orientation, and relative position). Additionally, a temporal memory mechanism, including Scene Memory for maintaining the evolving scene state and Agent Memory for accumulating reasoning context, enables evidence integration across frames and reasoning steps. Comprehensive experiments on multi-view and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show that \textsc{S-Agent} consistently improves both open-source and closed-source VLMs in a training-free manner. Beyond inference-time augmentation, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on \textsc{S-Agent}-generated spatial trajectories \textsc{S-300K} yields \textsc{S-Agent-8B}, a compact spatial agent that significantly surpasses similar-scale baselines (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B) and performs comparably to advanced closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3).

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界中的空间智能需要对连续且演化的3D世界进行推理，然而现有的视觉语言模型和工具增强型智能体大多仍局限于从孤立的视觉观测中进行静态、无状态的推理。我们提出\textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}，一种空间工具使用智能体范式，用于理解并推理连续的多视图图像和视频。通过将空间推理构建为时空证据累积过程而非孤立的帧级预测，\textsc{S-Agent}将空间感知从帧中心识别重塑为场景中心理解。具体而言，\textsc{S-Agent}将VLM作为语义规划器，决定需要哪些证据；同时，层级化的空间工具和专家负责在2D中定位物体，将其提升为3D几何证据，并将这些证据聚合成高级空间知识（如计数、测量、方向和相对位置）。此外，时序记忆机制（包括用于维护演化场景状态的场景记忆和用于累积推理上下文的智能体记忆）实现了跨帧和跨推理步骤的证据整合。在多视图和视频空间推理基准上的全面实验表明，\textsc{S-Agent}以无需训练的方式一致提升了开源和闭源VLM的性能。超越推理时增强，基于\textsc{S-Agent}生成的空间轨迹\textsc{S-300K}进行监督微调得到的\textsc{S-Agent-8B}，一个紧凑的空间智能体，显著超越了同规模基线（如Qwen3-VL-8B），性能与先进闭源模型（如GPT-5.4和Gemini 3）相当。

</details>


### [91] [HumanScale: Egocentric Human Video Can Outperform Real-Robot Data for Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20521)
*Juncheng Ma,Jianxin Bi,Yufan Deng,Xuanran Zhai,Kewei Zhang,Ye Huang,Bo Liang,Shukai Gong,Jiankai Tu,Xiaotian Tang,Jiaxin Li,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Yuqi Wang,Bingyi Kang,Eric Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Wojciech Matusik,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了自我中心人类视频与远程操控真实机器人轨迹作为具身基础模型预训练数据源的效果，发现经过精心设计的过滤和标注流程后，自我中心视频不仅可作为替代方案，甚至能带来更优性能：在真实机器人动作预测上验证损失降低24%，分布内和分布外任务成功率分别提升52.5%和90%，验证了“以自我中心视频预训练+少量机器人数据微调”的可扩展范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 远程操控真实机器人轨迹因精确动作监督和具身对齐成为主要预训练数据源，但存在采集成本高、获取难度大、行为和環境多样性低等 scalability 瓶颈；自我中心人类视频虽成本低、规模大、多样性高，但其相比于远程操控数据的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 系统比较研究：在固定后训练和验证协议下，对比使用自我中心人类视频（经精心设计的过滤与标注流程处理）与远程操控真实机器人轨迹作为预训练数据源，训练具身基础模型，并通过真实机器人动作预测损失和任务执行成功率进行评估。

Result: 在相同预训练数据量下，使用自我中心视频预训练的模型：1）真实机器人动作预测验证损失降低24%；2）分布内任务成功率提升52.5%；3）分布外任务成功率提升90%。

Conclusion: 验证了一种可扩展的具身基础模型范式：先在大规模自我中心人类视频上预训练以学习多样的世界表征，再使用少量标注的真实机器人数据进行动作空间对齐的适配。

Original Abstract: Embodied foundation models are expected to benefit from data scaling like large language models, but face a much tighter data bottleneck. Teleoperated real-robot trajectories remain the dominant pretraining source due to their precise action supervision and embodiment alignment, yet their scalability is limited by high collection cost, acquisition difficulty, and low behavioral and environmental diversity. These limitations have sparked interest in egocentric human video as a scalable, substantially lower-cost, and more diverse alternative for embodied model pretraining. However, its effectiveness compared to teleoperated real-robot data remains underexplored. To address this question, we conduct a systematic study comparing egocentric human video and teleoperated real-robot trajectories as pretraining data sources for embodied foundation models, under fixed post-training and validation protocols. Surprisingly, we find that egocentric data, when processed through a carefully designed filtering and labeling pipeline, is not merely a viable substitute for model pretraining but can lead to superior performance. With the same amount of pretraining data, models pretrained on egocentric data achieve a 24% lower validation loss on real-robot action prediction, as well as 52.5% and 90% higher success rates on in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-robot task execution, respectively. This finding verifies a scalable paradigm for embodied foundation models: pretrain on egocentric human video to learn diverse world representations, then adapt with a small amount of labeled real-robot data for action-space alignment. We hope this study encourages broader exploration of egocentric data and offers guidance for data quality assessment before costly robot data collection.

中文摘要翻译: 具身基础模型有望像大语言模型一样受益于数据规模扩展，但面临严重的数据瓶颈。远程操控的真实机器人轨迹因具有精确的动作监督和具身对齐能力，仍然是主要的预训练数据源，但其可扩展性受到采集成本高、获取难度大以及行为和環境多样性低的限制。这些局限性激发了人们对自我中心人类视频的兴趣，将其作为具身模型预训练的一种可扩展、成本显著更低且更多样化的替代方案。然而，与远程操控真实机器人数据相比，其有效性尚未得到充分探索。为解决这一问题，我们在固定后训练和验证协议下进行了一项系统研究，比较了自我中心人类视频和远程操控真实机器人轨迹作为具身基础模型预训练数据源的效果。令人惊讶的是，我们发现经过精心设计的过滤和标注流程处理后，自我中心数据不仅是模型预训练的可行替代方案，还能带来更优的性能。在相同的预训练数据量下，使用自我中心数据预训练的模型在真实机器人动作预测上的验证损失降低了24%，在分布内和分布外真实机器人任务执行上的成功率分别提升了52.5%和90%。这一发现验证了一种可扩展的具身基础模型范式：在自我中心人类视频上预训练以学习多样的世界表征，然后通过少量标注的真实机器人数据进行适配以实现动作空间对齐。我们希望这项研究能够鼓励对自我中心数据的更广泛探索，并为在昂贵的机器人数据采集之前进行数据质量评估提供指导。

</details>


### [92] [SARLO-80: Worldwide Slant SAR Language Optic Dataset 80cm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20523)
*Solène Debuysère,Nicolas Trouvé,Nathan Letheule,Elise Colin,Georgia Channing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并公开了一个大规模甚高分辨率（VHR）SAR-光学-文本多模态数据集（SARLO-80），包含约12万组SLC级SAR图像、对齐光学图像和自然语言描述，填补了该领域公开数据集的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SAR-光学数据集大多基于低分辨率、仅强度的GRD产品，未保留复数SAR测量值和原生采集几何结构，限制了基于物理的多模态学习，且缺少结合VHR SAR SLC、对齐光学图像和自然语言描述的大规模公开数据集。

Method: 从约2500景Umbra聚束模式SICD数据出发，将所有SAR数据通过带限FFT重采样标准化至80cm斜距网格，切分为1024×1024瓦片；为每块SAR瓦片检索高分辨率光学图，利用局部坐标对应将其扭曲至SAR网格实现像素级对齐；并为每个样本生成三种文本描述（短/中/长）。

Result: 构建了包含119,566组三元组（复数及振幅斜距SAR图、对齐光学图、自然语言描述）的数据集，覆盖72个国家257个地点，包含多种土地类型和基础设施，并发布了固定数据划分及预处理与基线代码。

Conclusion: 该数据集为多模态对齐、跨模态检索和条件生成提供了可在原生SAR几何下进行可重复基准测试的公开资源，推动了SAR领域多模态基础模型的发展。

Original Abstract: Multimodal foundation models have advanced rapidly thanks to large optical benchmarks, but comparable resources for synthetic aperture radar (SAR) remain limited. Existing SAR--optical datasets largely rely on low-resolution, intensity-only Ground Range Detected~(GRD) products and do not preserve complex-valued SAR measurements or native acquisition geometry, which restricts physically grounded multimodal learning. In particular, large-scale public datasets combining very-high-resolution (VHR) SAR SLC, aligned optical imagery, and natural-language descriptions are still lacking. We present a VHR SAR--optical--text dataset built from open-access Umbra spotlight acquisitions distributed as Sensor Independent Complex Data (SICD). From around 2,500 worldwide scenes (VV/HH, 20cm--2m native resolution), we standardize all SAR data to an 80cm slant-range grid via band-limited FFT resampling and tile the imagery into 1024 by 1024 patches. For each SAR patch, we retrieve a high-resolution optical tile and warp it into the SAR grid using local coordinate correspondences for local pixel-level alignment. We further generate three caption variants (SHORT/MID/LONG) per sample to support vision--language training and evaluation. Our dataset contains 119,566 triplets (complex and amplitude slant-range SAR patch, aligned optical patch, natural-language description) covering 257 locations across 72 countries and a broad range of land types and infrastructures. We release fixed train/validation/test splits and the full preprocessing and baseline code to enable reproducible benchmarks for multimodal alignment on cross-modal retrieval and conditional generation in native SAR geometry. The dataset is publicly available on the Hugging Face Hub at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态基础模型得益于大型光学基准数据集而快速发展，但合成孔径雷达（SAR）领域的类似资源仍然有限。现有的SAR-光学数据集主要依赖低分辨率、仅强度的地距探测（GRD）产品，未保留复数SAR测量值或原生采集几何结构，这限制了基于物理的多模态学习。特别是，结合甚高分辨率（VHR）SAR单视复数（SLC）、对齐光学图像和自然语言描述的大规模公开数据集仍然缺乏。我们提出了一个VHR SAR-光学-文本数据集，该数据集基于以传感器独立复数数据（SICD）格式分发的开放存取Umbra聚束模式图像构建。从约2500景全球场景（VV/HH极化，20cm–2m原生分辨率）出发，我们通过带限FFT重采样将所有SAR数据标准化至80cm斜距网格，并将图像切分为1024×1024的瓦片。对于每块SAR瓦片，我们检索高分辨率光学图，并利用局部坐标对应将其扭曲至SAR网格以实现逐像素级对齐。此外，我们为每个样本生成三种描述文本变体（短/中/长）以支持视觉-语言训练与评估。我们的数据集包含119,566组三元组（复数及振幅斜距SAR图、对齐光学图、自然语言描述），覆盖72个国家257个地点以及多种土地类型和基础设施。我们发布了固定的训练/验证/测试划分以及完整的预处理和基线代码，以便在原生SAR几何下对跨模态检索和条件生成任务中的多模态对齐进行可重复的基准测试。该数据集可在Hugging Face Hub上公开获取，地址为https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80。

</details>


### [93] [VisDom: Sparse Novel View Synthesis with Visible Domain Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20531)
*Mariia Gladkova*,Tarun Yenamandra*,Edmond Boyer,Robert Maier,Tony Tung,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisDom，一种无学习的几何约束，通过最小多视角可见性要求增强经典视觉外壳重建，在稀疏视角新视角合成中提供更强的空间先验，适用于NeRF和GS管道，仅需四张输入图像即可实现高质量重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 稀疏视角下的新视角合成因从少量输入视角恢复3D几何存在歧义而具有挑战性。现有基于NeRF和高斯泼溅的方法在密集监督下表现良好，但在稀疏设置下容易过拟合，产生漂浮伪影和不一致的几何结构。轮廓一致性作为正则化器仍不足以解决该问题，因为轮廓一致区域可能超出真实物体几何范围。

Method: VisDom定义了一个可见域（visible domain），即被至少K个视角观测到的3D空间子集，将其作为在标准轮廓重建之上的额外过滤标准。通过限制体积采样和引导高斯点优化，将VisDom集成到隐式（NeRF）和显式（GS）管道中。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的数据集上，稀疏视角NVS取得了一致改进，仅需四张输入图像即可实现高质量物体中心重建。将VisDom应用于GaussianObject可在Omni3D和MipNeRF360上进一步提升性能，同时以22倍更低的训练成本达到或超越原有水平。

Conclusion: VisDom是一种领域无关、仅需轮廓、无学习参数的方法，可作为现有稀疏视角NVS方法的简单补充，有效提升重建质量并降低训练成本。

Original Abstract: Sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging due to the ambiguity of recovering 3D geometry from few input views. While NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting (GS)-based methods perform well with dense supervision, they often overfit in sparse settings, producing floating artifacts and inconsistent geometry. Silhouette consistency is commonly used as a regularizer, but it remains insufficient, as silhouette-consistent regions can extend beyond the true object geometry. We introduce VisDom, a learning-free geometric constraint that augments classical carving-based visual hull reconstruction by enforcing a minimum multi-view visibility requirement. Specifically, we define a visible domain as the subset of 3D space observed by at least $K$ views and use it as an additional filtering criterion on top of standard silhouette-based reconstruction. This provides a stronger spatial prior in sparse-view settings. We integrate VisDom into both implicit (NeRF) and explicit (GS) pipelines by restricting volumetric sampling and guiding Gaussian placement during optimization. Experiments on three challenging datasets show consistent improvements in sparse-view NVS, enabling high-quality object-centric reconstruction from as few as four input images. Our method is domain-agnostic, requires only silhouettes, and introduces no learned parameters, making it a simple complement to existing approaches. Applying VisDom on top of GaussianObject further improves performance on Omni3D and MipNeRF360, while matching or surpassing it at 22 $\times$ lower training cost.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏视角新视角合成（NVS）由于从少量输入视角恢复3D几何存在的歧义性，仍然具有挑战性。虽然基于NeRF和高斯泼溅（GS）的方法在密集监督下表现良好，但它们在稀疏设置中容易过拟合，产生漂浮伪影和不一致的几何结构。轮廓一致性常被用作正则化器，但这仍然不足，因为轮廓一致区域可能超出真实物体几何范围。我们提出VisDom，一种无学习的几何约束，它通过强制最小多视角可见性要求来增强经典的基于雕刻的视觉外壳重建。具体而言，我们将可见域定义为被至少K个视角观测到的3D空间子集，并将其作为标准轮廓重建之上的额外过滤标准。这为稀疏视角设置提供了更强的空间先验。我们通过限制体积采样和引导优化过程中的高斯点放置，将VisDom集成到隐式（NeRF）和显式（GS）管道中。在三个具有挑战性数据集上的实验表明，稀疏视角NVS取得了一致改进，仅需四张输入图像即可实现高质量物体中心重建。我们的方法是领域无关的，仅需轮廓，且不引入任何学习参数，使其成为现有方法的简单补充。将VisDom应用于GaussianObject可在Omni3D和MipNeRF360上进一步提升性能，同时以22倍更低的训练成本达到或超越原有水平。

</details>


### [94] [The FID Lottery: Quantifying Hidden Randomness in Generative-Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20536)
*Nicolas Dufour,Alexei A. Efros,Patrick Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了FID作为图像生成评价指标的随机性与可重复性，发现训练种子（而非采样种子）是FID波动的主要来源，并提出新的评估协议：报告多个训练种子的误差棒，将低于约1.3%变异系数的FID差距视为不确定。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前图像生成领域普遍将FID作为标准评价指标，但大多数论文仅报告单个模型在单次采样下的单一数值，忽略了其统计随机性。本文旨在系统量化FID在不同训练种子和采样种子下的方差，揭示其可重复性问题。

Method: 将FID视为训练种子和生成种子二维面板上的随机变量，在数百个类条件ImageNet 256x256的SiT网络上直接测量其方差，分析随机初始化、数据顺序、流匹配损失的逐步高斯噪声等因素的影响，并研究计算量、模型大小、无分类器引导（CFG）调优对FID变异系数的影响。

Result: (a) 更换训练种子导致的FID波动是更换采样种子的3.2倍；(b) 该差距由随机初始化、数据顺序和流匹配损失中的高斯噪声驱动；(c) 增加计算量或模型大小几乎不缩小FID的变异系数（维持在1-2%）；(d) 每单元CFG调优可将波动减半，但会重新排序最优种子，幸运的训练种子可用最多2倍少的计算达到相同FID。

Conclusion: 建议新的FID评估协议：在每单元最优引导下评估，将低于约1.3%变异系数的FID差距视为不确定，报告多个训练种子的误差棒而非单一FID数值。

Original Abstract: The Frechet Inception Distance (FID) is the de facto arbiter of image generation, yet most papers report just a single number from a single trained model using a single sampling seed. How reproducible is that number if we retrain the model, or merely resample from it? In this paper, we treat FID as a random variable on a two-axis panel of training and generation seeds, and measure its variance directly on several hundred SiT networks trained on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256. We report surprising findings: (a) Retraining the model using the same recipe with a different seed moves FID 3.2x more (in Inception feature space) than redrawing samples from a fixed network. (b) That gap is driven by three factors: random initialisation, data ordering, and the per-step Gaussian noise of the flow-matching loss. (c) Increasing compute or model size barely tightens the spread, holding the FID coefficient of variation (CoV) inside a 1-2% band. (d) Per-cell classifier-free-guidance tuning halves the spread but reshuffles which seeds work best, and a lucky training seed reaches the same FID with up to 2x less compute than an unlucky one. Based on these findings, we recommend a new FID evaluation protocol: evaluate under per-cell optimal guidance, treat any FID gap below the empirically measured ~1.3% CoV as inconclusive, and report an error bar over several training seeds rather than a single FID number.

中文摘要翻译: Frechet Inception Distance (FID) 是图像生成领域的事实仲裁标准，然而大多数论文仅报告来自单个训练模型、使用单个采样种子的单一数值。如果重新训练模型，或仅从中重新采样，这个数字的可重复性如何？在本文中，我们将 FID 视为训练种子和生成种子二维面板上的随机变量，并直接在数百个在类条件 ImageNet 256x256 上训练的 SiT 网络上测量其方差。我们报告了令人惊讶的发现：(a) 使用不同种子、相同配方重新训练模型带来的 FID 变化（在 Inception 特征空间中）比从固定网络中重新采样高出 3.2 倍。(b) 这一差距由三个因素驱动：随机初始化、数据顺序以及流匹配损失的逐步高斯噪声。(c) 增加计算量或模型大小几乎不会缩小波动范围，使 FID 变异系数保持在 1-2% 的区间内。(d) 每单元的无分类器引导调优可将波动减半，但会重新排列哪些种子效果最佳，并且幸运的训练种子相比不幸的种子可以用多达 2 倍少的计算量达到相同的 FID。基于这些发现，我们推荐一种新的 FID 评估协议：在每单元最优引导下进行评估，将任何低于经验测量的约 1.3% 变异系数的 FID 差距视为不确定，并报告多个训练种子的误差棒，而非单一的 FID 数值。

</details>


### [95] [CalTennis: Large Multi-View Tennis Video Dataset and Benchmark of Monocular-to-3D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20542)
*Ilona Demler,Xinran Xie,Blake Werner,Anna Szczuka,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalTennis是一个包含超过1100万帧、51小时网球比赛视频的大规模基准数据集，用于评估野外单目到3D姿态估计。它比现有数据集大10倍，首次提供同步多视角专业运动记录。实验发现当前方法在深度估计和足部接触方面存在困难，并提出了两个新指标——步法和稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有野外人体运动视频数据集规模有限，缺乏大规模同步多视角专业运动数据集，难以有效评估单目到3D姿态估计算法在真实复杂运动场景中的表现，特别是深度估计、足部接触等关键问题。

Method: 构建了CalTennis数据集，使用2-6台同步摄像机以60Hz频率采集40名网球运动员的练习和比赛视频（共1100万帧/51小时），设计了标准化的数据采集协议和全自动视频标定与同步流程。

Result: 在CalTennis上测试了最先进的单目到3D姿态方法，发现3D关节角度恢复已相当准确，但所有模型在深度估计和足部接触一致性上表现不佳。此外提出了步法和稳定性两个新指标，揭示了之前未被充分探索的失败模式。

Conclusion: 当前姿态估计方法在深度和足部接触方面存在系统性缺陷；提出的步法和稳定性指标能有效暴露这些失败模式，为姿态估计和动作分析指明了具体的改进方向。

Original Abstract: The Caltech Tennis Dataset (CalTennis) is a large-scale video benchmark for evaluating monocular-to-3D pose estimation in the wild. CalTennis comprises over 11 million frames (51 hours) of tennis practice and match play from 40 players, captured with 2-6 synchronized cameras at 60 Hz. It is 10 times larger than existing in-the-wild human motion video datasets and 3 times larger than existing MOCAP-ground-truthed datasets, and it is the first large-scale benchmark to provide synchronized multi-view recordings of expert athletic motion. The multi-view setup enables inexpensive, label-free evaluation of monocular-to-3D pose estimation algorithms. We describe a simple, standardized protocol that enables data collection without specialized equipment or expertise, along with fully automated video calibration and synchronization. Benchmarking state-of-the-art monocular-to-3D pose methods on CalTennis, we find that while 3D joint angle recovery is now quite accurate, all models struggle to estimate depth and foot contact consistently. We further propose two novel performance metrics, footwork and stability, as well as qualitatively study body shape inconsistency. These metrics expose previously underexplored failure modes and point to concrete opportunities for improvement in pose estimation and action analysis.

中文摘要翻译: Caltech网球数据集（CalTennis）是一个大规模视频基准，用于评估野外场景下单目到3D姿态估计。CalTennis包含超过1100万帧（51小时）的网球练习和比赛视频，涵盖40名运动员，使用2至6台同步摄像机以60Hz频率采集。该数据集规模是现有野外人体运动视频数据集的10倍，是现有MOCAP真值数据集的3倍，并且是首个提供同步多视角专业运动记录的大规模基准。多视角设置使得单目到3D姿态估计算法能够以低成本、无需标注的方式进行评估。我们描述了一种简单的标准化协议，无需专业设备或专业知识即可进行数据采集，并实现了全自动视频标定与同步。在CalTennis上对最先进的单目到3D姿态方法进行基准测试后发现，尽管3D关节角度恢复已相当准确，但所有模型在深度估计和足部接触一致性方面仍存在困难。我们进一步提出了两个新颖的性能指标——步法和稳定性，并对身体形状不一致性进行了定性研究。这些指标揭示了之前未被充分探索的失败模式，为姿态估计和动作分析指明了具体的改进方向。

</details>


### [96] [SSD: Spatially Speculative Decoding Accelerates Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20543)
*Shilong Xiang,Zirui Zhang,Lijun Yu,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间推测解码（SSD）框架，利用二维空间相关性同时预测相邻水平和垂直令牌，将自回归图像生成速度提升最高13.3倍，同时保持高保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归模型将图像展平为一维序列处理，丢弃了图像固有的二维空间局部性，导致推理过程中产生严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出空间推测解码（SSD）框架，模型不是仅预测一维序列中的下一个令牌，而是同时预测水平相邻令牌和正下方垂直令牌，利用二维空间相关性来克服视觉推理中的内存墙问题。

Result: 在DPG-Bench和GenEval基准上，自回归图像生成速度提升高达13.3倍，同时保持高保真度。

Conclusion: 尊重视觉的底层几何结构可以释放巨大的计算效率，为实时、高分辨率自回归生成模型铺平道路。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive models excel in visual generation by treating images as 1D sequences of discrete tokens, mirroring language modeling. However, this flattening discards the intrinsic 2D spatial locality of visual signals, creating severe computational bottlenecks during inference. We introduce Spatially Speculative Decoding (SSD), a framework that aligns the predictive objective with the natural geometry of images. Rather than predicting only the immediate next token in a 1D sequence, our model simultaneously predicts the adjacent horizontal token and the token directly below it. By capitalizing on this 2D spatial correlation, spatially speculative decoding overcomes the memory wall in visual inference. Our approach accelerates autoregressive image generation by up to 13.3x while maintaining high fidelity on DPG-Bench and GenEval. Our results suggest that respecting the underlying geometry of vision unlocks massive computational efficiencies, paving the way for real-time, high-resolution autoregressive generative models.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归模型通过将图像视为离散令牌的一维序列，在视觉生成方面表现出色，这与语言建模相呼应。然而，这种展平处理丢弃了视觉信号固有的二维空间局部性，在推理过程中造成了严重的计算瓶颈。我们提出了空间推测解码（SSD）框架，该框架将预测目标与图像的自然几何结构对齐。我们的模型不是仅预测一维序列中的下一个直接令牌，而是同时预测相邻的水平令牌及其正下方的垂直令牌。通过利用这种二维空间相关性，空间推测解码克服了视觉推理中的内存墙问题。我们的方法在DPG-Bench和GenEval上实现了高达13.3倍的自回归图像生成加速，同时保持高保真度。我们的结果表明，尊重视觉的底层几何结构可以释放巨大的计算效率，为实时、高分辨率的自回归生成模型铺平道路。

</details>


### [97] [Current World Models Lack a Persistent State Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20545)
*Jinpeng Lu,Dexu Zhu,Haoyuan Shi,Linghan Cai,Guo Tang,Yinda Chen,Jie Cao,Duyu Tang,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WRBench基准，系统评估世界模型在观测中断后物理状态的持续演化能力。发现现有模型在摄像机移开时无法让世界事件继续推进，而是停留在最后观测状态，表明当前世界模型本质上是"跟踪镜头"而非真正的世界演化引擎。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准仅奖励生成画面的表面质量（保真度、运动、可控性），从未评估世界在未被观测时是否持续演化。物理世界建模要求内部状态独立于观测而随时间推进，这是当前研究的盲点。

Method: 设计WRBench诊断基准，将摄像机运动视为对可观测性的干预，构建人工校准的评估链：(1) 摄像机是否执行了请求的交互；(2) 场景在视野中是否连续且可识别；(3) 返回的目标是否与之前启动的事件状态一致。

Result: 在9600个视频、23个模型、4种控制范式上发现：模型在摄像机离开后不会推进事件，而是让返回的目标停留在被放弃时的状态。该失败跨控制范式、模型家族和规模尺度普遍存在，不能通过更清晰的图像、更紧的控制、更丰富的几何先验或更多参数解决。

Conclusion: 物理状态核的稳定性和视角干预下世界线的一致性应成为世界模型设计的一类目标，使世界模型捕捉世界如何展开而非下一帧如何呈现。

Original Abstract: World models are increasingly regarded as a decisive step toward artificial general intelligence, yet modeling the physical world demands more than rendering convincing frames on demand: it requires an internal world state that keeps evolving over time, decoupled from observation, so that objects endure and events run to their conclusions whether or not a camera is watching, much as the moon holds to its orbit when no one is looking. This requirement is a blind spot of existing benchmarks, which reward surface properties such as fidelity, motion, and camera controllability while never asking whether a generated world keeps evolving once it is unobserved. We introduce \textbf{WRBench}, the first systematic diagnostic benchmark that treats camera motion as an intervention on observability and resolves evaluation into a human-calibrated chain that asks whether the camera executes the requested interaction, whether the scene stays continuous and identifiable while in view, and whether a returning target remains consistent with the event that was set in motion. Across 9{,}600 videos from 23 models spanning four control paradigms, one finding proves stubborn: current systems maintain the observed world as a tracking shot, resuming a returning target in the state at which it was abandoned rather than advancing the event while it went unseen. Because this failure recurs across control paradigms, model families, and increments of scale, robust world-state evolution does not follow from cleaner imagery, tighter control, richer geometric priors, or sheer parameter count We therefore argue that the stability of the physical state kernel and the consistency of worldlines under viewpoint intervention should become first-class objectives of world-model design, so that a world model captures how the world will unfold rather than how the next frame appears.

中文摘要翻译: 世界模型日益被视为通向通用人工智能的关键一步，然而对物理世界进行建模要求的不仅仅是按需生成逼真的画面：它需要一个随时间持续演化的内部世界状态，该状态与观测解耦，使得即使在无人观看时物体仍保持存在、事件仍继续推进——正如月亮在无人注视时仍沿轨道运行。这一要求是现有基准的盲点，它们奖励画面的表面属性（如保真度、运动、摄像头可控性），却从未考察已生成的世界在无人观测时是否持续演化。我们提出了\textbf{WRBench}，这是首个将摄像机运动视为对可观测性的干预，并将评估分解为人工校准的评测链的系统性诊断基准。该评测链依次考察：摄像机是否执行了请求的交互；场景在视野内时是否保持连续且可识别；以及返回目标是否与之前启动的事件状态保持一致。在涵盖4种控制范式的23个模型、共9600个视频的评估中，一个发现被反复证实：当前系统仅将所观测的世界维持为一个跟踪镜头——当摄像机返回时，目标被恢复至其被弃置时的状态，而非在未被观测期间事件持续推进后的状态。由于这一失败跨越控制范式、模型家族和规模尺度，稳健的世界状态演化并不能从更清晰的图像、更紧致的控制、更丰富的几何先验或单纯的参数量增长中获得。因此我们认为，物理状态核的稳定性以及视角干预下世界线的一致性应成为世界模型设计的一类核心目标，从而使世界模型能够捕捉世界将如何展开，而非下一帧看起来怎样。

</details>


### [98] [Thinking in Boxes: 3D Editing in Real Images Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20556)
*Pradhaan S Bhat,Naveen Chandra R,Rishubh Parihar,Vaibhav Vavilala,R. Venkatesh Babu,D. A. Forsyth,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D框（"Thinking in Boxes"）的图像编辑方法，用户通过指定输入和输出3D框来精确控制物体在图像中的平移、旋转、缩放和视角变换，结合深度对齐的地面参考帧，使生成器在保持场景和物体身份的同时，实现大范围3D变换下的图像编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于文本或2D条件的图像编辑方法在处理大物体运动和相机视角变化时，控制能力弱且模糊。此前的工作虽使用3D框作为条件信号，但仅用于指示物体大致位置，无法精确指定变换参数。

Method: 用户提供输入和输出3D框，每个框面用颜色编码表示3D朝向。引入深度对齐的平面地板作为全局参考坐标系，并添加深度感知线索。采用两阶段训练：先在合成多物体场景上训练，再在Objectron真实视频小数据集上微调，使模型能泛化到复杂真实图像。

Result: 该方法直接处理真实照片，在大范围3D编辑任务上显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 将3D框作为结构化规范（而非松散条件信号）来解决图像编辑中的空间变换问题，结合深度对齐参考帧，能够在大物体运动和视角变化下实现精准、一致的编辑效果。

Original Abstract: Text and 2D-conditioning interfaces provide weak, ambiguous control over spatial transformations in image editing -- particularly under large object motions and camera changes. Prior work has used 3D primitives such as boxes, but only as loose conditioning signals indicating approximate object location rather than specifying the transformation. We instead use 3D boxes as structured specifications: the user provides the input and output boxes of the edit, casting editing as a well-posed geometry problem. This ``thinking in boxes'' interface, where each box face is color-coded to convey 3D orientation, gives precise control over translation, rotation, scaling, and viewpoint changes in real images while preserving scene and object identity, and recovering previously unseen object regions. To ground transformations in scene appearance, we introduce a depth-aligned planar floor as a global reference frame, shaded with depth-aware cues. Conditioned on this structure, an image generator produces consistent results under large transformations. Trained in two stages -- on synthetic multi-object scenes and a small set of real-world videos from Objectron -- the system generalizes to complex, in-the-wild real images. Our method operates directly on real photographs and substantially outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods on large 3D edits.

中文摘要翻译: 文本和2D条件接口在图像编辑中，尤其是面对大物体运动和相机视角变化时，对空间变换的控制是微弱且模糊的。先前的工作使用了诸如3D框之类的3D基元，但仅作为指示物体大致位置的松散条件信号，而非精确指定变换。我们则将3D框用作结构化规范：用户提供编辑的输入框和输出框，将编辑任务转化成一个适定的几何问题。这种"在框中思考"的界面设计下，每个框面通过颜色编码来传达3D朝向，从而在保持场景和物体身份、恢复先前不可见物体区域的同时，对真实图像中的平移、旋转、缩放和视角变化提供精确控制。为了将变换与场景外观对齐，我们引入了一个深度对齐的平面地板作为全局参考框架，并辅以深度感知线索进行着色。在这样的结构条件下，图像生成器能够在大幅变换下产生一致的结果。该方法通过两阶段训练——先在合成多物体场景上训练，再在来自Objectron的小规模真实世界视频集上训练——泛化到复杂的野外真实图像。我们的方法直接对真实照片进行操作，在大范围3D编辑任务上大幅优于当前最先进的 methods。

</details>


### [99] [UNIEGO: Proxies as Mediators for Unified Egocentric Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20559)
*Wenhao Chi,Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNIEGO：一种层级化多教师蒸馏框架，通过代理模型将异构教师知识转化为统一的第一人称视角表征，并利用选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）自适应筛选可靠监督信号，在三个自中心视频理解任务上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 第一人称视频理解受限于可穿戴摄像头的单一视角、单一模态和单一模型，无法捕捉人类动作的丰富语义。现有方法难以融合来自不同视角（自我/他人）、不同模态（RGB/深度/骨架）和不同基础模型的多源互补知识。

Method: 提出层级化多教师蒸馏框架UNIEGO：第一层，引入代理模型（Proxy models）将九种异构教师（涵盖自我/他人视角、RGB/深度/骨架模态、四种基础模型）的知识转化为统一的同质第一人称空间；第二层，选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）对每个训练样本自适应选择正确且置信的代理进行蒸馏，抑制错误信号；此外，通过凸组合初始化UNIEGO以优化损失景观。

Result: 在三个挑战性数据集上，UNIEGO在动作识别、视频检索和动作分割三个任务上均达到最先进水平，显著优于朴素的多教师蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的、代理中介的知识迁移能够产生更丰富、更具判别力的第一人称表征，证明了统一多源知识并仅从第一人称视频部署的有效性。

Original Abstract: Egocentric video understanding is inherently limited by the narrow perspective of wearable cameras: a single viewpoint, a single modality, a single model cannot capture the full richness of human action. We argue that a truly expressive egocentric representation must subsume complementary knowledge across viewpoints, modalities, and foundation model representations, yet remain deployable from egocentric video alone. To this end, we introduce a hierarchical multi-teacher distillation framework that produces UNIEGO, a unified egocentric encoder trained with nine teachers spanning ego-exo viewpoints, RGB, depth, and skeleton modalities, and four foundation models. Rather than distilling directly from heterogeneous teachers whose incompatible architectures and feature geometries induce conflicting gradients, our framework interposes a layer of representation-specific Proxy models that translate diverse teacher knowledge into a homogeneous egocentric space. A second distillation stage, Selective Proxy Distillation (SPD), then adaptively selects, for each training sample, the subset of proxies that are both correct and confident, distilling exclusively from reliable supervision and suppressing erroneous signals. SPD is further stabilized by initializing UNIEGO as a learned convex combination of proxy parameters, placing the unified model in a well-conditioned region of the loss landscape before distillation begins. UNIEGO achieves state-of-the-art performance across three egocentric video understanding tasks - action recognition, video retrieval, and action segmentation on three challenging ego-exo benchmarks, outperforming naive multi-teacher distillation baselines and demonstrating that structured, proxy-mediated knowledge transfer yields richer and more discriminative egocentric representations.

中文摘要翻译: 自中心视频理解本质上面临可穿戴摄像头窄视角的限制：单一视角、单一模态、单一模型无法捕捉人类动作的全部丰富性。我们认为，一个真正富有表现力的自中心表征必须融合跨视角、跨模态和跨基础模型的互补知识，同时仍能从自中心视频中独立部署。为此，我们引入了一个层级化多教师蒸馏框架，生成了UNIEGO——一个统一的自中心编码器。该编码器由九位教师训练而成，涵盖自我-他人视角、RGB、深度和骨架模态，以及四种基础模型。我们的框架并非直接从不兼容架构和特征几何导致梯度冲突的异构教师进行蒸馏，而是在表示层插入了特定于表征的代理模型，将多样化的教师知识转换为同质的自中心空间。第二蒸馏阶段——选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）——随后针对每个训练样本自适应地选择那些既正确又置信的代理子集，仅从可靠的监督信号中蒸馏，并抑制错误信号。SPD还通过将UNIEGO初始化为代理参数的学习凸组合来进一步稳定，使统一模型在蒸馏开始前处于损失景观的良好条件区域。UNIEGO在三个自中心视频理解任务——动作识别、视频检索和动作分割——以及三个具有挑战性的自我-他人基准上达到了最先进的性能，优于朴素的多教师蒸馏基线，并证明了结构化的、代理中介的知识迁移能够产生更丰富且更具判别力的自中心表征。

</details>


### [100] [TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20561)
*Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Siddharth Krishnan,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeProVe，一种面向长视频问答的高效混合框架，先通过轻量模块生成基于动作的证据假设，再调用昂贵的视觉语言模型进行目标验证，在显著降低计算成本的同时取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频问答方法要么密集处理整个视频，计算成本过高；要么依赖稀疏字幕推理，容易丢失时间定位和运动相关的证据。需要一种既能定位时间敏感证据、又能控制计算开销的方法。

Method: TimeProVe采用两阶段混合框架：1) 使用轻量模块（ACE模块）将时间定位的动作转化为查询条件下的候选答案和支持证据窗口，通过轻量LLM推理完成；2) 仅对生成的候选证据调用昂贵的VLM进行针对性验证，避免对整个视频进行密集处理。

Result: 在OTB基准上比最强基线提升7.3%，同时减少75%的VLM调用和93%的推理成本；在Charades-STA上无需显式时间定位训练即取得竞争性能，结合定位VLM后达到SOTA。

Conclusion: TimeProVe证明了"轻量假设生成+目标验证"的混合范式能够在长视频问答中实现计算效率与时间定位性能的双赢，是一种适用于日常活动场景的实用方案。

Original Abstract: Long Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires identifying sparse, query-relevant evidence within hours-long untrimmed videos. Existing approaches either process videos densely with large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring prohibitive computational cost, or rely on sparse caption-based reasoning, which often misses temporally localized and motion-centric evidence. We introduce TimeProVe, a cost-efficient hybrid framework for temporally grounded reasoning in long videos. TimeProVe first employs lightweight modules to generate action-grounded answer--evidence hypotheses and subsequently invokes an expensive VLM only for targeted verification. The core of our framework lies in the Action-based Candidate Evidence (ACE) module, which converts temporally localized actions into query-conditioned candidate answers and supporting evidence windows through lightweight LLM reasoning. We further introduce OpenTSUBench (OTB), an open-ended benchmark designed to evaluate temporally grounded reasoning in real-world Activities of Daily Living (ADL) scenarios. Experiments show that TimeProVe outperforms the strongest baseline on OTB by 7.3%, while reducing VLM calls by 75% and inference cost by 93%. Furthermore, without explicit temporal grounding training, TimeProVe achieves competitive performance on Charades-STA, and reaches state-of-the-art results when enhanced with grounding VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 长视频问答（LVQA）需要在长达数小时的未剪辑视频中识别与查询相关的稀疏证据。现有方法要么使用大型视觉语言模型（VLM）对视频进行密集处理，导致计算成本过高；要么依赖基于稀疏字幕的推理，但往往会遗漏时间定位和运动中心的证据。本文提出TimeProVe，一种面向长视频中时间定位推理的高效混合框架。TimeProVe首先使用轻量模块生成基于动作的答案-证据假设，随后仅调用昂贵的VLM进行针对性验证。我们框架的核心在于基于动作的候选证据（ACE）模块，该模块通过轻量LLM推理，将时间定位的动作转化为查询条件下的候选答案和支持证据窗口。我们进一步提出了OpenTSUBench（OTB），一个面向真实世界日常活动（ADL）场景的开放基准，用于评估时间定位推理能力。实验表明，TimeProVe在OTB上比最强基线提升7.3%，同时减少75%的VLM调用和93%的推理成本。此外，无需显式时间定位训练，TimeProVe在Charades-STA上取得了具有竞争力的性能，结合定位VLM后更达到了最先进水平。

</details>


### [101] [JanusMesh: Fast and Zero-Shot 3D Visual Illusion Generation via Cross-Space Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20563)
*Siang-Ling Zhang,Huai-Hsun Cheng,Tsung-Ju Yang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速、无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架，通过跨空间双分支去噪过程和视图条件纹理合成，在3-5分钟内生成高真实感、几何无缝融合的双语义3D错觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D视觉错觉生成方法存在两大问题：基于优化的方法速度慢且颜色过饱和；朴素拼接方法无法生成几何一致的对象，出现明显接缝和语义泄露。亟需一种高效、几何无缝的生成方法。

Method: 将生成过程解耦为两阶段：1）跨空间双分支去噪过程，动态将3D隐变量解码到体素空间进行CLIP引导的朝向对齐和SDF融合，实现无缝几何融合；2）视图条件纹理合成模块，将特定视图的2D扩散先验投影并聚合到融合后的几何上。

Result: 该方法仅需3-5分钟即可生成高真实感的双语义3D错觉，在几何完整性、语义可识别性和效率上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种快速、训练免费的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架，有效解决了现有方法的几何不一致、速度和颜色问题，实现了高质量的双语义3D错觉生成。

Original Abstract: Creating 3D visual illusions, a single 3D mesh that reveals entirely different semantics from various viewing angles, is a fascinating but tough challenge. Existing optimization-based methods are slow and can produce oversaturated colors. In contrast, naive stitching approaches fail to produce geometrically coherent objects. This results in visible unnatural seams and semantic leaks. In this paper, we present a fast and training-free framework for generating text-driven 3D visual illusions. Our approach decouples the generation into two stages. First, we propose a cross-space dual-branch denoising process. This process dynamically decodes 3D latents into voxel space for CLIP-guided orientation alignment and Signed Distance Field (SDF) blending, which ensures seamless geometric fusion. Second, we introduce a view-conditioned texture synthesis module that projects and aggregates view-specific 2D diffusion priors onto the fused geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method generates highly realistic, dual-semantic 3D illusions in just 3-5 minutes. It significantly outperforms existing methods in geometric integrity, semantic recognizability, and efficiency. Project page: https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 创建3D视觉错觉——即一个从不同视角展现出完全不同语义的单一3D网格——是一项迷人但极具挑战性的任务。现有的基于优化的方法速度慢且会产生过饱和的颜色。相比之下，朴素的拼接方法无法生成几何一致的对象，导致出现可见的不自然接缝和语义泄露。在本文中，我们提出了一种快速、无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架。我们的方法将生成过程解耦为两个阶段。首先，我们提出了一种跨空间双分支去噪过程。该过程动态地将3D隐变量解码到体素空间，用于CLIP引导的朝向对齐和有符号距离场（SDF）融合，从而确保无缝的几何融合。其次，我们引入了一个视图条件纹理合成模块，将特定视图的2D扩散先验投影并聚合到融合后的几何上。大量实验证明，我们的方法仅需3-5分钟即可生成高真实感、双语义的3D错觉，在几何完整性、语义可识别性和效率上显著优于现有方法。项目页面：https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Full-Self Diagnostics (FSD): Physics-Grounded Visual Biomarker Inference from Smartphone Video via Inverse Problems and Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19372)
*Jonathan Thomas,Harsh Thaker*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出全自诊断（FSD）框架，从智能手机拍摄的9秒无约束面部视频中恢复潜在生理状态，通过物理模型、信息论、正则化逆问题、算子学习与贝叶斯推理五部分协同，在59名受试者38812次配对扫描中验证了无创血糖监测的临床可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有非侵入式生理状态监测通常需要专用设备或受控环境，智能手机面部视频包含的生理信息未被充分挖掘，缺乏统一的理论框架来定量恢复生物标志物浓度。

Method: 五个相互强化组件：(1)基于辐射传输方程和发色团吸收的物理前向模型；(2)信息论可观测性理论证明多通道视觉信号与生理状态的互信息递增性；(3)Tikhonov正则化逆问题求解；(4)算子学习方法实现跨设备、分辨率和人群泛化；(5)可解释为随机变分推断的监督学习，性能随配对观测数平方根倒数比例提升。

Result: 38812次真实配对扫描验证：第一作者自身数据（血糖35-550 mg/dL）MARD为29.86%，97.57%预测落在Clarke误差网格A+B区，仅0.27%在危险E区；管理良好的糖尿病患者在70-180 mg/dL窄带内MARD达17%。

Conclusion: 消费级面部视频在完全无约束条件下编码了足够的结构化信息，可用于临床相关的非侵入性生物标志物推断，且性能随配对数据增加可预测地提升。

Original Abstract: We present Full-Self Diagnostics (FSD), a unified mathematical framework for recovering latent physiological states from unconstrained 9-second facial videos captured by consumer smartphones. The approach integrates five mutually reinforcing components: (1) a physics-based forward model derived from the radiative transfer equation and chromophore absorption that maps camera observables to biomarker concentrations; (2) an information-theoretic observability theory proving that multi-channel visual signals (spectral, pulse, respiratory, micro-expression, and oculomotor) contain strictly increasing mutual information with physiological state; (3) a stable, Tikhonov-regularized inverse problem with domain-uniform identifiability guarantees; (4) an operator-learning formulation that enables generalization across devices, resolutions, and populations; and (5) a supervised learning procedure, interpretable as stochastic variational inference, that continuously refines the model from paired biosensor ground truth with performance improving proportionally to one over the square root of the number of paired observations.
  Empirical validation on 38812 real-world paired scans across 59 subjects demonstrates practical performance. Self-collected data from the lead author (glucose range 35-550 mg/dL) yields MARD of 29.86 percent with 97.57 percent of predictions in Clarke Error Grid Zones A+B and only 0.27 percent in the dangerous Zone E. A well-managed diabetic participant achieves MARD of 17 percent in the narrower 70-180 mg/dL band. These results confirm that consumer-grade facial video encodes sufficient structured information for clinically relevant, non-invasive biomarker inference under fully unconstrained conditions, with performance scaling predictably as more paired data becomes available.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出全自诊断（FSD）框架，这是一个统一的数学框架，用于从消费级智能手机拍摄的9秒无约束面部视频中恢复潜在生理状态。该方法整合了五个相互强化的组成部分：(1)基于辐射传输方程和发色团吸收的物理前向模型，将相机可观测信号映射到生物标志物浓度；(2)信息论可观测性理论，证明多通道视觉信号（光谱、脉搏、呼吸、微表情和眼动）与生理状态之间存在严格递增的互信息；(3)具有域均匀可辨识性保证的稳定Tikhonov正则化逆问题；(4)算子学习形式化方法，实现跨设备、分辨率和人群的泛化；(5)可解释为随机变分推断的监督学习过程，通过配对的生物传感器真实值持续优化模型，性能随配对观测数量的平方根倒数成比例提升。在59名受试者共38812次真实世界配对扫描上的实证验证展示了实际性能。第一作者自采集数据（血糖范围35-550 mg/dL）的MARD为29.86%，97.57%的预测落在Clarke误差网格A+B区，仅0.27%落在危险E区。一名管理良好的糖尿病参与者在较窄的70-180 mg/dL范围内MARD达到17%。这些结果证实，消费级面部视频在完全无约束条件下编码了足够的结构化信息，可用于临床相关的非侵入性生物标志物推断，且性能随更多配对数据的可用性呈可预测地提升。

</details>


### [103] [FrequencyFormer: A Co-Designed Sensor-to-Processor Pipeline for Frequency-Domain Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19574)
*Chengwei Zhou,Ovishake Sen,Xuming Chen,Rishith Paramasivam,Shaahin Angizi,Swarup Bhunia,Baibhab Chatterjee,Gourav Datta*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: FrequencyFormer提出了一种基于频域标记化的传感器-处理器协同流水线，通过多尺度DCT压缩和近传感器硬件实现，大幅减少视觉Transformer在边缘设备上的数据传输能耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在传感器边缘系统部署ViT时，不仅受限于设备端计算能力，高维图像数据从传感器到处理器的传输也带来巨大的能耗和带宽开销。现有近传感器计算方法仅提供有限的压缩效果。

Method: (1) 多尺度DCT分词器：将224x224图像压缩为紧凑的频域token，实现高达128倍的数据量缩减；(2) 基于查找表(LUT)的近传感器硬件实现，利用固定DCT系数实现无乘法的节能高面积效能的token化；(3) 基于MIPI的低功耗通信架构，进一步降低传输能耗。该设计可作为标准ViT patch embedding的即插即用替代方案。

Result: 达到28.8 TOPS/W的能效，通信能耗降低230倍，传感器侧总能耗降低2.22倍，在分类、检测和分割任务中与预训练骨干网络兼容。

Conclusion: 频域token化可作为一种可扩展的基础方案，用于在传感器端高效部署ViT模型。

Original Abstract: Deploying vision transformers (ViTs) on sensor-edge systems is limited not only by on-device compute, but also by the energy and bandwidth required to transmit high-dimensional image data from the sensor to the processor. While in-sensor and near-sensor computing reduce this cost through early feature extraction, existing methods often provide only modest compression. We observe that the frequency domain provides a naturally compact representation of visual information and can be exploited at the sensor level to reduce sensor-to-processor data movement. Building on this insight, we present FrequencyFormer, a co-designed sensor-to-processor pipeline for efficient ViT inference. FrequencyFormer includes: (1) a multi-scale DCT tokenizer that compresses a 224x224 image into compact frequency-domain tokens, achieving up to 128x reduction in off-chip data volume with modest accuracy loss; (2) a LUT-based near-sensor hardware implementation that leverages fixed DCT coefficients for multiplier-free, energy- and area-efficient tokenization; and (3) a modified MIPI-based low-power communication architecture that further reduces transfer energy. FrequencyFormer serves as a drop-in replacement for standard ViT patch embedding and remains compatible with pretrained backbones across classification, detection, and segmentation tasks. The pipeline achieves 28.8 TOPS/W, reduces communication energy by 230x, and lowers total sensor-side energy by 2.22x, demonstrating frequency-domain tokenization as a scalable foundation for in-sensor ViT deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 在传感器边缘系统上部署视觉Transformer（ViT）不仅受限于设备端计算能力，还受限于将高维图像数据从传感器传输到处理器所需的能量和带宽。虽然传感器内和近传感器计算通过早期特征提取降低了这一成本，但现有方法通常仅提供适度的压缩。我们观察到，频域为视觉信息提供了天然的紧凑表示，并且可以在传感器层面加以利用以减少传感器到处理器的数据移动。基于这一洞察，我们提出了FrequencyFormer，一种面向高效ViT推理的传感器-处理器协同设计流水线。FrequencyFormer包括：(1) 多尺度DCT分词器，将224x224图像压缩为紧凑的频域token，在精度适度损失的情况下实现高达128倍的片外数据量缩减；(2) 基于查找表（LUT）的近传感器硬件实现，利用固定DCT系数实现无乘法的节能高面积效能的token化；(3) 一种改进的基于MIPI的低功耗通信架构，进一步降低传输能耗。FrequencyFormer可作为标准ViT patch embedding的即插即用替代方案，并与分类、检测和分割任务中的预训练骨干网络保持兼容。该流水线实现了28.8 TOPS/W的能效，通信能耗降低了230倍，传感器侧总能耗降低了2.22倍，证明了频域token化可作为传感器端ViT部署的可扩展基础方案。

</details>


### [104] [Contour-Constrained Deformable Registration with Parameter Characterization for Head and Neck Surgical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19767)
*Qingyun Yang,Jon S. Heiselman,Ayberk Acar,Morgan J. Ringel,Michael I. Miga,Matthieu Chabanas,Michael C. Topf,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于力学生物学的可变形配准框架，通过正则化Kelvinlet基函数和边界轮廓约束，将术后3D标本网格与术中切除床点云对齐，显著降低了头颈鳞癌术中切缘定位的配准误差（平均目标配准误差从刚性配准的11.11 mm降至5.62 mm，降幅49.41%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 头颈部鳞状细胞癌术后复发率高，术中冰冻切片分析虽为标准方法，但因切除组织与切除床之间难以精确对齐（组织切除后黏膜收缩、形变），导致阳性切缘定位不准确，亟需一种能够校正术后组织变形的术中引导方法。

Method: 提出基于力学生物学的可变形配准框架，使用正则化Kelvinlet基函数对3D标本网格与术中切除床点云进行可变形配准，同时匹配表面点云、基准标记和边界轮廓约束（直接惩罚标本与切除床边界间的垂直距离偏差）。

Result: 在9个标本（皮肤、颊黏膜、舌）上，刚性配准的平均目标配准误差为11.11±4.07 mm；无轮廓约束的可变形配准降至8.20±2.68 mm（降幅26.19%）；所提轮廓约束可变形配准进一步降至5.62±2.28 mm（降幅49.41%），其中舌标本改善最显著。两阶段参数搜索表明，对于具有较大侧向形变的组织类型，轮廓约束权重主导配准精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的轮廓约束可变形配准框架能有效校正术后组织变形，显著提升术中切缘定位精度，尤其适用于临床最具挑战性的舌部标本，且算法在较宽参数范围内鲁棒稳定。

Original Abstract: With 890,000 annual new cases globally, head and neck squamous cell carcinoma has one of the highest recurrence rates among solid malignancies. Although frozen section analysis is the standard of care for intraoperative margin assessment, accurately relocating detected positive margins on the resection bed remains challenging due to imprecise alignment between resected specimens and their resection bed, compounded by post-resection mucosal tissue shrinkage. We present a biomechanics-driven deformable registration framework that corrects post-resection tissue deformation to provide intraoperative guidance. Our approach registers 3D specimen meshes to intraoperative resection bed point clouds using a deformable registration approach based on regularized Kelvinlet basis functions. The registration matches surface point clouds, fiducial landmarks, and boundary contour constraints that directly penalize perpendicular distance-to-agreement between specimen and resection bed boundaries. Across nine specimens from skin, buccal mucosa, and tongue sites, the overall mean target registration error was $11.11 \pm 4.07$ mm using rigid registration, which decreased to $8.20 \pm 2.68$ mm (26.19\% reduction) using deformable registration without contour constraint. The proposed contour-constrained deformable registration further reduced the error to $5.62 \pm 2.28$ mm, a 49.41\% reduction relative to rigid registration. We observed the largest reduction in the most clinically challenging tongue specimens. We also performed a systematic two-stage parameter search to characterize the relative importance of surface alignment, fiducial correspondences, contour constraint, and strain energy regularization. This search revealed that contour weighting dominates registration accuracy for tissue types with large lateral deformation, while the algorithm operates over a broad range of parameter combinations.

中文摘要翻译: 全球每年新发头颈部鳞状细胞癌约89万例，其复发率居实体恶性肿瘤前列。尽管冰冻切片分析是术中切缘评估的标准方法，但由于切除标本与其切除床之间对齐不精确（加之切除后黏膜组织收缩），准确定位检测到的阳性切缘仍具挑战性。我们提出了一种基于力学生物学的可变形配准框架，通过校正术后组织变形来提供术中引导。该方法采用基于正则化Kelvinlet基函数的可变形配准，将3D标本网格与术中切除床点云进行配准。配准过程同时匹配表面点云、基准标记以及边界轮廓约束——该约束直接惩罚标本与切除床边界之间的垂直距离偏差。在皮肤、颊黏膜和舌部位共9个标本上，刚性配准的总体平均目标配准误差为11.11±4.07 mm，无轮廓约束的可变形配准将其降至8.20±2.68 mm（降幅26.19%），而所提出的轮廓约束可变形配准则进一步将误差降至5.62±2.28 mm，较刚性配准降低了49.41%。在临床最具挑战性的舌标本中观察到了最大的降幅。我们还进行了系统的两阶段参数搜索，以表征表面对齐、基准对应、轮廓约束和应变能正则化的相对重要性。该搜索揭示，对于具有较大侧向形变的组织类型，轮廓约束权重主导配准精度，而算法可在较宽参数组合范围内稳定运行。

</details>


### [105] [SIMBA: ABidirectional Retrieval Forward Simulation Framework for Modeling FY-4A GIIRS Hyperspectral Infrared Radiances Toward NWP Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19943)
*Jingdong Shen,Fu Wang*,Qifeng Lu,Hao Huang,Chunqiang Wu,Chi Yang,Xiaofang Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SIMBA统一双向检索-前向仿真框架，联合进行大气廓线反演与辐射重建，通过循环一致性约束和双向Mamba状态空间模块，在FY-4A GIIRS高光谱红外辐射建模任务上优于多种深度学习基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法主要关注从辐射到大气廓线的单向反演，忽略了反向辐射模拟过程以及大气状态空间与辐射观测空间之间的一致性。

Method: 提出SIMBA框架，联合执行大气廓线反演与辐射重建；引入循环一致性约束以增强两个过程的耦合；采用双向Mamba状态空间模块沿气压层级捕获长程依赖。

Result: 在温度反演、比湿反演、长波辐射重建和中波辐射重建任务上，SIMBA优于多个代表性深度学习基线；消融实验验证了双向设计和循环一致性机制的贡献。

Conclusion: SIMBA框架能够有效进行联合大气廓线反演与高光谱红外辐射建模，并具有用于雅可比相关分析和面向数值天气预报扩展的潜力。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral infrared observations are an important data source for numerical weather prediction (NWP) because they provide rich information on the vertical structure of atmospheric temperature and humidity. However, most existing deep learning methods mainly focus on one-way retrieval from radiances to atmospheric profiles, while the reverse radiance simulation process and the consistency between atmospheric state space and radiance observation space are insufficiently considered. In this study, we propose SIMBA, a unified bidirectional retrieval-forward simulation framework for FY-4A GIIRS hyperspectral infrared radiance modeling toward NWP applications. The framework jointly performs atmospheric profile retrieval and radiance reconstruction, introduces a cycle-consistency constraint to strengthen the coupling between the two processes, and employs a bidirectional Mamba state-space module to capture long-range dependencies along pressure levels. Using collocated FY-4A GIIRS observations and ERA5 reanalysis data, the proposed method is evaluated for temperature retrieval, specific humidity retrieval, long-wave radiance reconstruction, and medium-wave radiance reconstruction. Experimental results show that SIMBA outperforms several representative deep learning baselines across both retrieval and reconstruction tasks, while ablation experiments confirm the contribution of the bidirectional design and cycle-consistency mechanism. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework is effective for joint atmospheric profile retrieval and hyperspectral infrared radiance modeling, and suggest potential for future Jacobian-related analysis and NWP-oriented extensions.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱红外观测由于能够提供大气温度和湿度垂直结构的丰富信息，是数值天气预报的重要数据来源。然而，现有深度学习方法主要聚焦于从辐射到大气廓线的单向反演，而反向辐射模拟过程以及大气状态空间与辐射观测空间之间的一致性尚未得到充分考虑。在本研究中，我们提出SIMBA——一种面向数值天气预报应用、针对FY-4A GIIRS高光谱红外辐射建模的统一双向反演-前向仿真框架。该框架联合执行大气廓线反演与辐射重建，引入循环一致性约束以增强两个过程之间的耦合，并采用双向Mamba状态空间模块沿气压层级捕获长程依赖。利用配准的FY-4A GIIRS观测数据与ERA5再分析数据，该方法在温度反演、比湿反演、长波辐射重建和中波辐射重建任务上进行了评估。实验结果表明，SIMBA在反演与重建两类任务上均优于多个代表性深度学习基线；消融实验进一步证实了双向设计与循环一致性机制的贡献。这些结果表明，所提出框架对于联合大气廓线反演与高光谱红外辐射建模是有效的，并预示着其在未来雅可比相关分析和面向数值天气预报扩展方面的潜力。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“计算可识别性”框架，区别于传统的理论可识别性，通过有限计算搜索过程寻找经验估计量，从而在有限样本、模糊图标准则、混合观测-干预数据等实际条件下实现因果效应的可识别。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统理论可识别性假设渐近性质、无限数据等理想化条件，无法回答细粒度、实际场景下的识别问题（如小样本、模糊图标准则等），因此需要一种计算约束下的可识别性框架。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”框架，定义一个有限的计算搜索过程来寻找经验估计量。若该过程能在指定误差容限内找到经验估计量，则满足可识别性，该识别性依赖于搜索的假设（如参数先验分布）和搜索过程本身。

Result: 通过多个实验展示了该框架能够回答实际中的精细识别问题，包括：小有限样本下的识别、模糊图标准则下的识别、混合观测-干预数据下的识别，以及跨反事实数据和估计量的识别。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性框架为因果效应识别提供了更贴近实际应用的理论工具，弥补了传统理论可识别性在真实场景中的不足。

Original Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

中文摘要翻译: 识别条件描述的是目标查询或感兴趣参数作为可用信息类型和数量的函数在计算上的可实现性。在因果识别中，这些信息通常以因果图的形式表达，数据是图中某个变量子集的观测或收集结果。目标查询可以是单一效应，也可以是给定模型中的一类效应。识别算法的推导从数学上定义了期望中理论上唯一确定所需因果效应的过程。期望中的可识别性，即"理论可识别性"，通常假设渐近性质、无限数据或其他数学上的理想化条件。本文探讨了这种理论化的理想化可识别性概念与一种基于计算约束的替代方案之间的根本区别。我们提出的框架——"计算可识别性"——将其定义为寻找经验估计量的有限计算搜索过程。如果该过程能在期望的误差容限内找到经验估计量，则可识别性得以满足，该结论依赖于搜索的指定假设（即参数的先验分布）以及搜索过程本身。通过多个实验，我们展示了该框架如何回答细粒度、实际场景下的识别问题，例如小有限样本下的识别、模糊图标准则下的识别、混合观测-干预数据下的识别，以及跨反事实数据和估计量的识别。代码可在 https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify 获取。

</details>


### [107] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GUARD框架，通过不确定性感知的门控路由机制，从领域不对齐的基础模型中蒸馏轻量化时序预测模型，在四个气候关键领域取得显著效果，支持边缘部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时序基础模型（TSFMs）在物理科学中零样本部署时存在严重的分布偏移问题，且计算成本高昂无法用于边缘传感器网络。需要一种方法能从这些不对齐的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识，训练轻量级专业化预测器。

Method: 提出GUARD（Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation）框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器（Contextual Router），基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature），当教师置信度与领域现实偏离时自动减弱蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源网格四个气候关键领域上，相比固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE；在28.5%的最难实例上，领域不对齐的教师表现优于全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: 领域不对齐的教师模型仍可作为关键校正信号，通过GUARD框架能够从预训练基础模型中成功蒸馏知识，实现适合资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。

Original Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

中文摘要翻译: 时序基础模型（TSFMs）在物理科学中的部署面临一个关键权衡：尽管这些模型编码了丰富、通用的时序动态特性，但当零样本应用于特定科学领域时，它们会遭受严重的分布性错位问题，同时其计算成本也阻碍了在边缘计算传感器网络中的部署。我们解决一个根本性挑战：如何从不对齐的基础模型（FMs）中提取潜在结构知识，以训练轻量级、专业化的预测器？我们提出GUARD（门控不确定性感知蒸馏路由），这是一个新颖框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为一个实例级决策过程，包含两种自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器，基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师，利用不同基础模型之间的互补性；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制，充当"断路器"，当教师置信度与领域现实偏离时自动减弱蒸馏强度。我们在四个气候关键领域评估了该轻量级框架：气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源网格。相比固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线，我们的方法显著降低了均方根误差（RMSE），成功从预训练基础模型（教师）中蒸馏知识——即使这些教师因原始数据与目标数据域之间的分布偏移而表现出次优的零样本精度。我们证明，这些领域不对齐的教师仍然可以作为关键的校正信号，在28.5%的最难实例上优于全局最优的基础模型。最终，这使得适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测成为可能。代码见 https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026。

</details>


### [108] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）方法，通过在边缘设备上使用4位量化的小语言模型压缩用户提示词中的社交冗余信息（如礼貌用语、铺垫性语言等），再将精简后的提示词传输给云端大语言模型进行推理，从而显著降低输入token数量（平均节省99.9 token），并在非劣效性评估框架下保持响应质量。该方法在248条提示词语料上验证了能源节省（每调用节省70-270 µWh）与响应质量的可接受性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型推理的预填充阶段已成为云规模能源成本的重要来源；用户提示词中包含大量社交性冗余（如礼貌用语、道歉铺垫、重复、建立 rapport 的语言），这些语言对人类沟通重要但对机器推理携带的低边际信息，造成了"社会-语义差距"（Social-Semantic Gap）。因此，作者希望在不显著降低推理质量的前提下，通过剔除低信息量的社交冗余来降低云端LLM的输入token成本，从而节约能源。

Method: SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）是一种部署在边缘设备上的流水线方法：使用4位量化的小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M）在用户设备端对用户提示词进行压缩，去除社交性冗余的同时保留语义和情感信息，再将压缩后的提示词传输给云端大语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）进行推理。对安全关键领域采用基于规则的直通路由（passthrough），确保不压缩敏感内容。

Result: 在248条提示词语料上，每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，所有146次蒸馏调用均为正向节省。在121对盲评中，LLM作为评判者的评分结果显示：43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出，蒸馏路径在预设的15分制1分非劣效性边界内不劣于原始路径。余弦相似度均值为0.682，中位数为0.712，54.1%的样本对超过0.70参考阈值。每次调用净能源节省估计为70-270 µWh。

Conclusion: SPSD证明，在边缘设备上进行提示词蒸馏能够有效降低云端LLM的输入token成本，同时在实际可行的非劣效性边界内保持响应质量。该方法为减少大规模LLM推理的云端能源开销提供了一种实用途径。

Original Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）推理的预填充阶段日益成为云规模能源成本的重要来源。许多面向消费者支持和对话的提示词中包含社交性脚手架：礼貌标记、道歉性铺垫、重复以及建立 rapport 的语言——这些对人类沟通至关重要，但对机器推理而言携带的低边际信息。我们将这种差异称为"社会-语义差距"（Social-Semantic Gap）。本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏），一种基于边缘设备的流水线，在传输到云端LLM之前，使用4位量化的小语言模型对用户提示词进行压缩。在248条提示词语料上的评估中，以Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M)作为SLM、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct作为云端评估模型，每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，所有146次蒸馏调用均实现正向节省。响应质量方面，通过盲LLM-as-judge在121对样本上的评分显示，在15分制中预设的1分非劣效性边界内，蒸馏路径不劣于原始路径；评判者给出43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出。余弦相似度结果参差不齐：均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%的样本对超过0.70参考阈值。安全关键领域通过基于规则的门控保守路由至直通模式。在给定假设下，每次调用的净能源节省估计为70-270 µWh。SPSD证明，在设备端进行提示词蒸馏能够在实际可行的非劣效性边界内降低云端LLM的输入token成本，同时保持响应质量。

</details>


### [109] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三种NVIDIA架构下进行了全面的性能分析，发现训练中cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核占主导，存在内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率低等问题。通过TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种优化，将SM周期和动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%，且不降低合成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型在高质量3D MRI合成中表现优异，但每样本需数百次U-Net评估，GPU资源消耗巨大，且内核行为高度异构，现有研究缺乏对医学扩散模型在GPU架构上的系统性性能分析。

Method: 对Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和profiler优先级分数估计的综合性能分析，并基于分析结果提出TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化。

Result: 训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，存在内存访问模式低效、张量布局转换开销大和Tensor Core利用率有限等问题。优化后SM周期和动态指令减少高达100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍，IPC在A100上提升7%，且合成质量无损。

Conclusion: 架构感知的优化策略能显著提升医学扩散模型在GPU上的运行效率，大幅降低计算开销而不牺牲合成质量，为医疗影像生成模型的部署提供了实用指导。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型已成为高质量3D MRI合成的关键工具，然而由于每样本需要数百次U-Net评估且内核行为高度异构，其部署受到大量GPU资源需求的严重制约。本文对最先进的医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行了全面的性能分析，研究了内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动以及profiler优先级分数估计。我们发现训练过程主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，效率低下源于内存访问模式、张量布局转换以及有限的Tensor Core利用率。基于这些分析洞察，我们评估了两种架构感知的优化——TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局——并证明它们能将SM周期减少高达100倍，动态指令削减100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，并在A100上将IPC提高7%，且完全不降低合成质量。

</details>


### [110] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）之间的深层联系，证明ILL学习的概率规则可解释为基于商变量的约束因子图，其中L2均匀性准则近似最大熵原理，从而将ILL定位为一种面向可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 阐明ILL与概率图模型（PGM）之间的形式化关系，为ILL提供更坚实的概率理论基础，并为其与图模型、最大熵模型的交叉应用开辟新方向。

Method: 通过将ILL中的划分视为确定性的商变量、将规则视为商变量的边际分布，建立规则集作为可解释抽象上的边际约束集合的框架；提出通用提升（一般联合分布族）和特殊提升（最大无知重建，基于L2均匀性准则）两种视角，并与香农熵提升下的对数线性因子图建立等价关系。

Result: 证明ILL规则具有自然的概率图模型解释；L2均匀性准则与最大熵原理紧密相关；在香农熵提升下，ILL规则等价于一个因子由学习到的抽象索引的对数线性因子图；信息格的边编码的是抽象的精化与粗化关系，而非条件依赖关系。

Conclusion: ILL应被视为面向可解释的、基于商变量的约束因子图的结构学习方法，这为推理、可辨识性以及混合符号-概率学习提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

中文摘要翻译: 信息格学习（ILL）通过将信号交替投影到编码抽象层次结构的分割格上，并将选定的规则提升回信号域，从而学习信号的可解释规则。当信号为概率质量函数时，我们证明ILL学习的概率规则具有自然的概率图模型（PGM）解释，并详细阐述这一解释。ILL中的划分诱导出一个确定性的商变量，一条规则即该商变量的边际分布。因此，规则集是在可解释抽象上的一组边际约束。通用提升是满足这些约束的所有联合分布的可行族，而特殊提升则选择最大无知重建，在ILL中通过L2均匀性准则实现，该准则与最大熵密切相关。在香农熵提升下，相同的约束产生一个对数线性因子图，其因子由学习到的抽象索引。然而，信息格本身并非贝叶斯网络：其边编码的是抽象的精化与粗化关系，而非条件依赖关系。因此，ILL应被视为面向可解释的、基于商变量的约束因子图的结构学习方法。这一观点阐明了ILL如何与图模型和最大熵模型相关联，同时为推理、可辨识性以及混合符号-概率学习提出了新方向。

</details>


### [111] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文基于双参数Weibull框架诊断Transformer权重分布，研究了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ的增长、过冲和松弛现象。通过推导AdamW更新的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力），发现对齐力主导上升阶段（占88-94%），并与衰减力接近平衡时导致λ从增长转向松弛。并提出了从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的样条位移方法（准确率约92-94%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解AdamW优化器训练Transformer时权重尺度参数λ为何会出现增长、过冲然后松弛的动态行为，并量化各因素（权重与自适应更新方向的对齐、自适应步长大小、权重衰减）的贡献。

Method: 1. 从AdamW更新中推导出权重平方范数的三力分解：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长大小）、衰减力（解耦权重衰减）。2. 在自训练的Pythia-70M模型上使用真实优化器矩进行验证。3. 提出样条位移方法，从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力主导上升阶段，贡献88-94%的绝对力预算，且对超权重移除鲁棒。饱和期对齐力与衰减力接近平衡，解释λ从增长到松弛的转变。RMS到Weibull重构偏移约5-6%。样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的准确率约92-94%，约为朴素两基点基线的两倍。λ的峰值随训练数据一致性变化。

Conclusion: AdamW训练中权重尺度的动态行为可被三力分解准确解释，其中对齐力是增长阶段的主导因素。样条位移方法能在实际模型中有效恢复对齐力，为理解优化过程提供了新的分析框架。

Original Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

中文摘要翻译: 基于诊断Transformer权重分布的双参数Weibull框架，我们研究了为何在AdamW训练过程中Weibull权重尺度参数λ会增长、过冲然后松弛。我们从AdamW更新中推导出权重平方范数的领先阶三力分解：对齐力衡量权重与自适应更新方向之间的相关性，注入力来自自适应步长大小，衰减力来自解耦权重衰减。在具有真实优化器矩的自训练Pythia-70M模型上，对齐力主导上升阶段，在四个随机种子下贡献了绝对力预算的88-94%，并且对超权重移除保持鲁棒。接近饱和时，对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡，这解释了从权重尺度增长到松弛的转变。这些力动态直接支配着λ(t)背后的平方范数成分；剩余的RMS到Weibull重构偏移是可测量的，可分解为桥接和积分成分，在密集采样区域总计约5-6%。为了将分析扩展到无法获取优化器矩的真实模型，我们引入了样条位移方法，该方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力，准确率约为92-94%，约为朴素两基点基线的两倍。我们进一步观察到，在我们的实验中λ(t)的峰值随训练数据一致性变化，这提示了权重尺度增长中可能存在数据依赖成分，我们将此留待后续受控研究。代码和数据可在https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public获取。

</details>


### [112] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布的估计分布算法（ZIG-EDA），用于稀疏黑盒优化，无需手工设计稀疏算子，能联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值，在Lunar Lander基准上优于稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的估计分布算法（EDA）在连续参数空间上表现良好，但无法推广到稀疏参数空间（即解的大部分系数恰好为零）。现有的稀疏黑盒优化器重新引入了手工设计的稀疏算子、双层交替优化方案等EDA原本想要避免的假设和偏见。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样律。该分布通过潜高斯模型，用独立的指示维度和数值维度分别表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及两者之间的交互，从而无层次地联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值。并提出了实用的基于摊销逆变换的估计器来估计潜参数。

Result: 所提估计器能准确恢复潜相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，优于稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA，且能找到只有很小比例参数活跃的控制器。

Conclusion: 所提的ZIG-EDA有效填补了EDA在稀疏参数空间上的空白，避免了手工设计稀疏算子，实现了稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化，在黑盒稀疏优化任务上展现出更优的性能和效率。

Original Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

中文摘要翻译: 估计分布算法（EDA）是一类强大的黑盒优化进化方法，尤其适用于对目标函数结构知之甚少的情况。传统进化算法依赖手工设计的变异和交叉算子——这些算子对于未知问题结构难以设计且带有偏见——而EDA完全绕过了算子设计：它将概率分布拟合到最优个体，并从该分布中采样下一代。EDA在连续参数空间上已得到充分发展，但此前尚未被推广到稀疏参数空间（即最优解的大多数系数恰好为零）。现有的稀疏黑盒优化器因此重新引入了EDA原本旨在避免的东西：手工设计的稀疏算子、在支撑集与活跃值之间交替的双层方案、归零阈值以及其他内嵌的假设。我们通过提出多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样律来填补这一空白。该潜高斯模型通过独立的指示维度和数值维度分别表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及两者之间的交互，从而无需层次结构，实现稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化。我们证明，该模型的潜参数可以从观测样本中识别（这与该构造所源自的缺失数据设置不同），并引入了实用的基于摊销逆变换的估计器。这些估计器能准确恢复潜相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，所得到的ZIG-EDA收敛更快且最终回报更高，优于稠密高斯EDA、手工设计的稀疏进化算法以及临时性的稀疏EDA，同时找到的控制器仅有很小一部分参数处于活跃状态。

</details>


### [113] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合少量人类驾驶数据（仅30分钟）作为正则化项的自我对弈强化学习方法，在保持与人类驾驶行为兼容的同时，实现了高效（15小时单GPU训练）且数据高效（比模仿学习少2500倍数据）的自动驾驶策略训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纯自我对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略虽然有效，但会学习到与人类驾驶习惯不兼容的"异类"驾驶行为；现有的奖励工程和域随机化方法脆弱且劳动密集，需要大量人工调参。

Method: 在最小安全目标到达奖励的基础上，将少量人类演示数据（30分钟）作为正则化目标，与自我对弈强化学习联合训练，既保留自我对弈的可扩展性，又通过人类数据引导策略行为与人类兼容。

Result: 仅需30分钟人类演示数据（比模仿学习少2500倍），在单张消费级GPU上15小时完成训练，学得的策略能够与未见过的（held-out）人类轨迹协调共驾。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据作为正则化项能有效解决自我对弈策略与人类行为不兼容的问题，实现了数据效率和训练效率的双重提升，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

中文摘要翻译: 自我对弈强化学习近期成为一种无需任何人类数据即可训练驾驶策略的方法。它利用廉价、大规模的仿真来替代昂贵、大规模的人类驾驶示范。该方法的一个关键局限性在于，通过纯自我对弈训练出的策略可能学习到高效但异于常人的、与人类不相容的驾驶习惯。以往的工作试图通过广泛的奖励工程和域随机化来缓解此类行为偏差，但这些方法脆弱且劳动密集。我们的方法并未完全抛弃人类示范，而是将其作为基于最小安全目标到达奖励之上的正则化目标。正如好汤中的调味料，我们发现少量人类数据就能起到重要作用：我们的方法仅使用30分钟的人类示范数据，比可比的模仿学习方法少2500倍。由此得到的策略能够与未见过的（held-out）人类轨迹协调共存，并在单张消费级GPU上15小时内完成训练。视频和完整源代码可在 https://spiced-self-play.com/ 获取。

</details>


### [114] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE提出一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过在低风险时仅使用低成本临床数据、不确定性高时逐步引入MRI/PET影像，在保持诊断精度的同时减少50-90%的昂贵影像使用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AD早期诊断依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET采集成本高且非普遍可用，全模态推理在实际临床中不可行。因此需要一种能够自适应决定何时需要额外模态、在降低数据采集成本的同时保持诊断准确性的方法。

Method: ProMUSE首先使用低成本临床数据进行证据分类，通过基于狄利克雷的主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，并通过Dempster-Shafer理论融合各模态的置信度和不确定性，获得校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD三个任务中，ProMUSE达到与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时减少50-90%的MRI/PET使用，显著降低了成本。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的AD筛查方案，能够在不牺牲准确性的前提下大幅降低对昂贵影像数据的依赖，适合真实临床工作流程。

Original Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

中文摘要翻译: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）是一种致命的神经系统疾病，会破坏老年人群的记忆和认知能力。大多数AD治疗在早期阶段有效，因此对早期AD诊断的需求日益增长。AD诊断日益依赖多模态数据，如临床评估、结构性磁共振成像（MRI）和正电子发射断层扫描（PET）影像。然而，MRI和PET的采集仍然成本高昂且并非普遍可及，这使得全模态推理在真实临床工作流程中不切实际。我们提出ProMUSE——一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，它能够自适应地确定何时需要额外模态，从而在保持准确性的同时降低数据采集的整体成本。ProMUSE首先使用低成本的临床数据进行证据分类，并通过基于狄利克雷分布的主观逻辑模型量化不确定性。当不确定性超过学习阈值时，ProMUSE逐步引入MRI或PET特征，通过Dempster-Shafer理论融合各模态的置信度和不确定性，从而获得校准的多模态预测。这种分阶段采集策略能够在最小化对昂贵影像依赖的同时实现准确诊断。在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE达到了与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时减少50-90%的MRI/PET使用，带来了显著的成本节约。这些结果突显了ProMUSE作为一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的方案，适用于真实世界的AD筛查。

</details>


### [115] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cAPM，一种持续主动学习AI模型，用于指导室性心动过速导管消融中的起搏标测，通过跨任务传递知识显著减少所需的起搏位点数量，在仿真测试中实现了81%的定位成功率（5mm精度）仅需4.5个起搏位点，远超基线方法的38%和13.7个位点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主动学习AI模型在起搏标测中需要为每个靶点重新训练，无法在同一患者多个室速病灶间或跨患者间传递知识，导致起搏效率仍有提升空间。本文旨在通过持续学习方法积累并迁移历史起搏标测数据中的知识，进一步减少未来靶点所需的数据量。

Method: cAPM包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务无关的代理神经网络，学习从起搏位点到12导联心电图形态的映射；(2) 主动学习策略，通过选择最具信息量的起搏位点来优化代理模型；(3) 持续学习策略，在顺序处理多个靶点任务时保留先前任务的知识，并支持数据回放以缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形状的序列定位任务的仿真测试平台上，cAPM（带或不带数据回放）使用平均4.5个起搏位点即可实现81%的临床耐受定位成功率（5mm精度），而现有最优主动学习方法需要13.7个起搏位点，成功率仅38%。

Conclusion: cAPM通过持续学习和主动学习相结合，显著提升了起搏标测的效率，为后续体内临床前和临床研究提供了有力基础，有望在临床实践中用于指导起搏标测流程。

Original Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

中文摘要翻译: 室性心动过速是一种危及生命的节律紊乱，也是心脏性猝死的主要原因。起搏标测是在室速导管消融过程中识别干预靶点的临床操作。它要求临床医生在不同心室位点进行起搏，并快速解读由此产生的心电图，以决定下一步起搏位置或是否已找到靶点。已有研究提出主动学习AI模型来指导临床医生选择下一个起搏位点，显示出减少起搏位点数量、提高起搏标测效率的潜力。现有方法需要为每个靶点重新训练模型，无法在同一患者的不同室速病灶之间或跨患者之间传递知识。我们提出了cAPM——连续AI辅助起搏标测，用于捕获和迁移从历史起搏标测数据中积累的知识，从而减少未来靶点室速所需的起搏标测数据量。这一目标通过以下设计实现：一个任务无关的代理神经网络，学习从起搏位点到12导联心电图形态的映射；一种主动学习策略，通过为每个靶点选择最具信息量的起搏位点来优化代理模型；以及一种持续学习策略，在顺序处理任务过程中保留先前靶点的知识。在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形状的序列定位任务的仿真测试平台上，cAPM（带或不带历史数据回放）使用平均4.5个起搏位点即可实现81%的临床耐受定位成功率（5mm精度），而现有最优主动学习方法需要13.7个起搏位点，成功率仅为38%。这些结果为将cAPM应用于体内临床前和临床研究提供了坚实基础，使其能够用于指导起搏标测流程。

</details>


### [116] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种二级结构感知的图神经网络（SSProNet），通过引入二级结构分配增强残基层面的节点表示，并基于氢键相互作用及其能量强度构建图边，从而在蛋白质表示学习中捕获局部结构上下文和长程耦合，在多个蛋白质基准上取得了一致的性能提升，并提供了更好的生物学可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于图的蛋白质建模方法大多依赖序列邻接或几何邻近性来构建图结构，但这仅部分反映了蛋白质折叠的原理。蛋白质实际采用以二级结构元素（如α-螺旋和β-折叠）组织的复杂三维构象，这些元素编码了重复出现的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用，而现有方法未能有效利用这些信息。

Method: 提出一个二级结构感知的图神经网络（SSProNet），核心设计包括：(1) 残基层级的节点表示通过二级结构分配进行增强；(2) 图边基于氢键相互作用并根据其能量强度进行过滤后构建。这种设计使模型能够同时捕获局部结构上下文和对蛋白质稳定性与功能至关重要的长程耦合。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准上评估，所提方法相比现有基于图的方法取得了一致且显著的性能提升。此外，生成的图表示具有更强的生物学可解释性，学习到的连接性与已建立的结构基序相吻合。

Conclusion: 引入二级结构信息和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置，该方法不仅提升了预测性能，还增强了模型的可解释性，为蛋白质结构建模提供了新的思路。

Original Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning.
