Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

中文摘要翻译: 基于图的表示被广泛用于蛋白质建模，然而许多现有方法主要依赖序列邻接或几何邻近性，这仅部分反映了指导蛋白质折叠的原理。蛋白质实际上采用围绕二级结构元素（如α-螺旋和β-折叠）组织的复杂三维构象，这些元素编码了重复出现的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种二级结构感知的图神经网络用于蛋白质表示学习。残基层面的节点表示通过二级结构分配得到增强，图边则基于按能量强度筛选的氢键相互作用构建。这种设计使模型能够捕获对蛋白质稳定性和功能至关重要的局部结构上下文和长程耦合。我们在常用蛋白质基准上评估了所提方法，观察到其相较于现有基于图的方法取得了一致的改进。此外，生成的图表示提供了更强的生物学可解释性，学习到的连接性与已建立的结构基序相吻合。这些发现表明，引入二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置。代码已在 https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet 开源。

</details>


### [117] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，用于从全场位移和反作用力数据中识别各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化屈服函数。该框架通过可微应力更新和凸神经网络表示屈服函数，在弹塑性应力积分内部进行力学约束学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服现象无法在全场力学测量中直接观测、方向性标定需要大量加载方向、选择合适的解析形式非常困难。现有方法依赖应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化形式，限制了其适用性。

Method: 采用凸神经网络表示屈服函数，强制满足凸性和一阶正齐次性，并施加拉压对称性假设；将该神经屈服函数嵌入可微的弹塑性应力积分框架中；通过多个加载工况下的物理信息力平衡损失进行训练，无需直接的应力空间监督。

Result: 在von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准测试中验证了框架的有效性，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性活跃应力状态的可辨识性、认知不确定性以及多项式代理部署效果。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条力学约束的路径，能够从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，同时将识别结果保持在弹塑性应力积分的结构之内。

Original Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

中文摘要翻译: 识别各向异性屈服函数仍然具有挑战性，因为屈服现象无法在全场力学测量中直接观测，方向性标定可能需要大量加载方向，且选择合适的解析形式并非易事。本研究提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，用于从全场位移数据和反作用力数据中发现屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量、直接的屈服面数据或预设的参数化屈服函数。该框架将屈服函数识别为弹塑性应力积分内部的一个受力学约束的本构组件，而非通过直接的应力空间监督。屈服函数由一个凸神经网络表示，该网络强制满足凸性和一阶正齐次性，并施加了假设的拉压对称性。该神经屈服函数通过可微的应力更新和跨多个加载工况的物理信息力平衡损失进行训练。所提出的框架采用von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准研究进行验证，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、通过塑性活跃应力状态的可辨识性、认知不确定性以及多项式代理部署。本研究提供了一条力学约束的路径，用于从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，同时将识别出的组件保持在弹塑性应力积分的结构之内。

</details>


### [118] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter是一种在线路由算法，利用稀疏、单侧的用户反馈学习成本最优策略，在保证SLA严格合规的前提下，将LLM推理成本降低高达2.2倍，且无需针对每个负载进行调优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM推理成本快速增长，用户对高质量响应的需求与SLA中规定的成本约束之间存在根本性矛盾。现有成本感知路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练、大量调参，且大多缺乏SLA保证和推理时自适应性，无法满足实际生产系统的需求。

Method: 提出SLARouter在线路由算法，从生产系统中可获取的稀疏、单侧用户反馈中学习成本最优策略，同时提供成本最优性和严格SLA合规性的理论保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准测试上，SLARouter无需针对每个基准进行调优即可满足SLA约束，相比现有基线方法降低运营成本高达2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter在无需大量人工调参的前提下，实现了成本与质量的良好平衡，为实际生产环境中LLM请求路由提供了高效且具有理论保证的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）应用的推理成本正在快速增长，这得益于需求的激增和基础设施成本的上升。用户期望获得高质量响应，在商业环境中，这一点通过服务等级协议（SLA）正式规范化，从而在成本与质量之间形成了根本性矛盾。近年来，成本感知的LLM请求路由研究显示出解决这一矛盾的潜力，但现有方法依赖于完整的反馈信号、离线训练、大量针对每个负载的调优，且大多缺乏SLA保证或推理时的自适应性。我们提出了SLARouter，一种在线路由算法，能够从生产系统中可获取的稀疏、单侧用户反馈中学习成本最优策略。SLARouter为成本最优性和严格的SLA合规性提供了理论保证。在多种LLM基准测试上的实验表明，SLARouter无需针对每个基准进行调优即可满足SLA约束，相比现有基线方法降低了高达2.2倍的运营成本。

</details>


### [119] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个140M参数的条件流匹配模型，通过轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代传统复杂架构，在计算酶设计任务中以更低的训练成本（682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4）取得了更优的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现存的全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中昂贵的架构设计，导致训练成本高且样本多样性有限。作者认为这些复杂性对于生成器而言是不必要的，因为生成器仅需以稀疏几何约束为条件，而非丰富的共进化信号。

Method: Emyx是一个140M参数的条件流匹配模型，其核心创新包括：(1) 将模型容量集中于标准Transformer模块，用轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代厚重嵌入堆叠；(2) 推导了流匹配插值到EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，在不重新训练的情况下将流匹配的训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法相结合。

Result: Emyx在AME酶设计基准上，在全局折叠恢复与催化几何精度、结构新颖性、骨架多样性和几何有效性等严格评估标准下，全面优于Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3。训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: 通过简化架构设计并引入流匹配与扩散模型之间的理论桥接，Emyx证明了小模型可以在计算酶设计中以更低的训练成本取得更优性能，为蛋白质设计中的生成模型提供了更高效的新范式。

Original Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

中文摘要翻译: 计算酶设计需要生成能够支撑催化残基和配体的蛋白质，这要求底层生成模型兼具几何精确性和结构多样性。当前的全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中昂贵的架构设计，导致训练成本高昂且样本多样性有限。我们认为，对于生成器而言，这些复杂性在很大程度上是不必要的——生成器以稀疏几何约束为条件，而非丰富的共进化信号。Emyx是一个140M参数的条件流匹配模型，将模型容量集中于标准Transformer模块内部，用轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代厚重的嵌入堆叠。我们进一步推导了流匹配插值到EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，在不重新训练的情况下将流匹配的训练效率与针对扩散模型设计的最先进采样方法相桥接。尽管Emyx是最小的模型，但在AME酶设计基准上，它在需要全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度的严格评估、结构新颖性、骨架多样性以及几何有效性方面，均优于Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，而训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

</details>


### [120] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合GNN-FEM框架，用于高效且可泛化的相场断裂模拟，通过在交错的有限元过程中用图神经网络替代相场更新，同时保留位移求解器，显著降低了计算成本并保持了物理一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 相场断裂模拟虽然提供鲁棒的变分框架，但求解耦合、非线性且历史依赖的系统计算成本极高，限制了实际应用。现有代理模型难以实现物理一致且可泛化的预测。

Method: 将GNN代理模型集成到传统交错方案中，在每个载荷增量步用GNN替代相场更新，同时保留基于FEM的位移求解器以强制执行力学平衡和边界条件。采用无量纲特征设计、基于网格的图表示以及源自控制相场方程的物理信息损失函数。

Result: 数值实验表明，该混合方法在保持与常规FEM相当精度的同时降低了计算成本，并在不同几何形状、载荷条件、材料属性和离散化设置下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

Conclusion: 通过保留增量求解结构，框架保持了与历史依赖断裂演化的一致性，无需代理模型逼近完整解轨迹。这种选择性代理策略强调识别物理上有意义且增量结构化的学习目标，而非依赖暴力数据生成来学习完整断裂过程。

Original Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

中文摘要翻译: 科学机器学习已成为加速复杂物理系统模拟的一种有前景的方法，然而，针对非线性、历史依赖问题实现物理一致且可泛化的预测仍然是一个核心挑战。在本研究中，我们提出了一种混合图神经网络-有限元方法框架，用于高效且可泛化的相场断裂模拟。尽管相场方法为模拟复杂裂纹演化提供了鲁棒的变分框架，但由于需要在增量有限元过程中求解耦合、非线性且历史依赖的系统，其高计算成本限制了实际应用。为应对这一挑战，我们将图神经网络代理模型集成到传统的交错方案中，在每个载荷增量步中替代相场更新，同时保留基于有限元方法的位移求解器以强制执行力学平衡和边界条件。通过保留增量求解结构，该框架保持了与历史依赖断裂演化的一致性，无需代理模型逼近完整解轨迹。这种选择性代理策略强调识别物理上有意义且增量结构化的学习目标，而非依赖暴力数据生成来学习完整断裂过程。通过无量纲特征设计、基于网格域的图表示以及源自控制相场方程的物理信息损失函数，所提框架在变化的几何形状、载荷条件、材料属性和离散化设置下实现了强泛化能力。数值实验表明，与传统有限元方法相比，该混合方法在保持精度的同时降低了计算成本，并在多种问题场景下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

</details>


### [121] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性可恢复性（R²_lin）指标，用于衡量Transformer中FFN层的实际非线性程度。实验发现不同块之间的线性度差异极大且非单调，不受激活函数决定，而是训练后的习得属性。该指标还可用于指导模型压缩，并揭示了线性基线训练中可能存在的收敛问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer中的FFN层常被视为非线性计算模块，但训练后其实际非线性程度从未被系统测量。作者希望量化每个FFN块的真实线性程度，以更好地理解其计算特性。

Method: 将每个FFN视为位置级输入-输出映射，将其分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上残差。用线性映射解释的留出方差定义块的线性可恢复性（R²_lin），这是一种无需优化器的线性度度量。

Result: 1) R²_lin在GPT-2、Pythia-160m、llama-160m的所有12个块中高度异质且非单调，范围从近线性(>0.99)到强非线性(<0.3)；2) 相同宽度的GELU模型（GPT-2与Pythia-160m）具有截然不同的轮廓，表明可恢复性是习得属性而非架构属性；3) 低秩双线性探针仅能恢复少量R²，且增益与残差非线性不相关；4) 高可恢复性块可用更大单层替代实现压缩，低可恢复性块则不适合。

Conclusion: 线性可恢复性测量可作为针对性的压缩信号，并为方法论提供了重要警示：训练的线性基线在病态条件的Transformer激活上可能严重欠收敛，需使用精确闭式最小二乘上限。

Original Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer的前馈网络（FFN）常被视作非线性计算存储单元，然而训练后的FFN块实际非线性程度鲜有被量化测量。本文将每个FFN视为位置级输入-输出映射，将其分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上残差。该线性映射解释的留出方差定义了一个块的线性可恢复性（R²_lin），这是一种无需优化器的线性度度量。在GPT-2、Pythia-160m和llama-160m的全部十二个块中，R²_lin高度异质且随深度非单调变化，相邻块之间范围从近线性（>0.99）到强非线性（<0.3），且并非由激活函数决定：相同宽度的GELU模型GPT-2与Pythia-160m具有截然不同的轮廓，因此可恢复性是单个训练块的习得属性，而非架构属性。对残差进行低秩双线性探针仅能恢复少量R²，且增益与残差非线性不相关：未能恢复的计算并非单一位置级乘积，而是高阶或分布式结构。该测量还可用作针对性的压缩信号：可恢复块可接受大的单层替换（GPT-2早期FFN以8倍参数减少换取+0.77困惑度），而低可恢复性块则标记了不安全区域。该方法还暴露了一个方法论陷阱：训练的线性基线在病态条件的Transformer激活上可能严重欠收敛，因此本文全程报告精确的闭式最小二乘上限。

</details>


### [122] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS是一个零样本形式化证明框架，通过两阶段协议（Best-of-N采样 + 批评引导的MCTS）将验证器信号反馈到证明搜索中，在miniF2F上达到40.6%，并发布了包含55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LLM的形式化证明器通常将丰富的验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元的通过/失败标志，丢失了大量可用于指导搜索过程的有用信息。

Method: 提出两阶段零样本协议：第一阶段进行Best-of-N采样；第二阶段采用批评引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），将第一阶段的失败案例作为显式负样本输入，从而利用反馈驱动探索。该协议保留第一阶段已解决的定理，第二阶段额外解决的定理可归因于反馈驱动的探索。

Result: 1) 在miniF2F上达到40.6%（对比独立运行的Best-of-5的36.9%和Portfolio的26.2%）；2) 在VERITAS-CombiBench（55个定理的组合学基准）上达到7.3%，而Best-of-5仅1.8%、Portfolio为3.6%，表明当需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名称时，无引导采样效果反而更差。

Conclusion: VERITAS通过将验证器信号有效回传至证明搜索过程，显著提升了形式化证明的成功率，尤其在需要从反馈中迭代修正的组合学问题上优势明显。

Original Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

中文摘要翻译: 基于LLM的形式化证明器通常将丰富的验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元的通过/失败标志。我们提出VERITAS，一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议将每个验证器信号回传至证明搜索过程：首先进行Best-of-N采样，然后进行批评引导的MCTS阶段，将第一阶段的失败案例作为显式负样本输入。该协议保留了第一阶段已解决的所有定理，因此第二阶段额外解决的定理可归因于反馈驱动的探索。VERITAS在miniF2F上达到40.6%（对比独立运行的Best-of-5的36.9%，Portfolio的26.2%），并在我们发布的包含55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench上达到7.3%，而Best-of-5（1.8%）低于Portfolio（3.6%），这表明当需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确的引理名称时，无引导采样反而有害。相关工件可在GitHub上获取。

</details>


### [123] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自由能签名（Fes）作为新的注意力谱描述符，将注意力拉普拉斯视为哈密顿量并提取热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）及随机矩阵理论谱形状因子，用于检测大语言模型幻觉。理论上证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达丰富性和PAC可学习性；实验上在六个模型和六个基准上达到最优AUROC。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于注意力谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少量特征值或手工标量，忽略了拉普拉斯谱的大部分结构信息，导致检测能力受限。

Method: 将每层注意力拉普拉斯视为哈密顿量，提取热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）和随机矩阵理论的谱形状因子，构建自由能签名（Fes）描述符。理论证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达丰富性和PAC可学习性；实验构建轻量级探测器和无训练RMT偏差检测器。

Result: 在六个开源LLM和六个基准上，Fes轻量探测器平均AUROC比LapEig高+6.5，比GoR-4高+2.4；无监督RMT偏差检测器平均AUROC达0.71；RMT分析表明正确生成呈现Wigner-Dyson谱统计，幻觉呈现Poisson谱统计。

Conclusion: Fes作为一种全面的注意力谱描述符，能够有效检测LLM幻觉，在监督和无监督场景均表现优异，且无需修改底层模型。谱统计差异为理解幻觉机制提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor. We prove three results: (i)~Lipschitz stability of Fes under attention perturbation; (ii)~an expressiveness result showing that Fes enriches finite spectral summaries and approximates moment-derived spectral functionals under explicit regularity and grid-resolution assumptions; and (iii)~a finite-sample PAC bound on the AUROC of a training-free detector built from Fes. Empirically, across six open-weight LLMs and six benchmarks, a lightweight probe on Fes descriptors achieves the strongest aggregate AUROC among attention-spectral baselines, improving over LapEig by $+6.5$ AUROC points and over GoR-4 by $+2.4$ points on average, while requiring no update to the underlying LLM. In the fully unsupervised setting, an RMT-deviation score achieves mean AUROC $0.71$, providing a label-free but weaker detector. A complementary RMT analysis shows that correct generations exhibit more Wigner-Dyson like spectral statistics, whereas hallucinations exhibit more Poisson-like statistics. The anonymized code and config are provided in the supplementary material.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中的幻觉检测对部署至关重要，近期研究表明注意力导出的图拉普拉斯谱携带关于推理质量的强烈信号。然而，现有谱诊断方法仅通过少量特征值或手工选取的标量来概括拉普拉斯谱，导致其大部分结构未被利用。我们提出自由能签名（Free-Energy Signatures, Fes），一种将每一层的注意力拉普拉斯视为哈密顿量并提取其热力学势——配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容——以及随机矩阵理论（RMT）谱形状因子的谱描述符。我们证明了三个结果：(i) Fes在注意力扰动下的Lipschitz稳定性；(ii) 一个表达性结果，表明Fes丰富了有限谱摘要，并在明确的规则性和网格分辨率假设下逼近矩导出的谱泛函；(iii) 基于Fes构建的无训练检测器AUROC的有限样本PAC界。实验方面，在六个开放权重LLM和六个基准上，基于Fes描述符的轻量级探测器在注意力谱基线方法中达到了最强的聚合AUROC，平均比LapEig提高+6.5个AUROC点，比GoR-4提高+2.4个AUROC点，同时无需更新底层LLM。在完全无监督的设置下，RMT偏差得分实现了平均AUROC 0.71，提供了一个无标签但较弱的检测器。补充的RMT分析表明，正确生成呈现更多的Wigner-Dyson谱统计特征，而幻觉呈现更多的Poisson谱统计特征。匿名代码和配置在补充材料中提供。

</details>


### [124] [FlexLAM: Resolving the Bottleneck Trade-off in Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19408)
*Takanori Yoshimoto,Yang Hu,Naruya Kondo,Tatsuya Matsushima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexLAM提出变长潜动作替代固定容量瓶颈，通过嵌套 dropout 生成前缀有效编码，在相同 token 预算下匹配或超越固定容量 LAM，支持推理时调整 token 预算，无需新架构或损失函数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜动作模型（LAM）使用固定容量瓶颈，面临权衡：过于紧凑的编码丢弃动作对齐所需的过渡线索，过于宽松的编码保留额外过渡变异性，在标签稀缺或分布狭窄时难以分辨。

Method: 提出 FlexLAM，用变长潜动作替代固定容量瓶颈，通过嵌套 dropout 训练，生成前缀有效（prefix-valid）编码，先捕获紧凑的过渡结构，仅在需要时添加细节，无需引入新架构或损失函数。

Result: 单个 FlexLAM 在每种评估的 token 预算下，在标准稀缺标签监督和低回报单任务对齐压力测试中，匹配或超越分别训练的固定容量 LAM；支持推理时调整 token 预算而无需重新训练；在 Ego4D 过渡重建任务上取得改进。

Conclusion: 变长潜动作是对潜动作模型、潜动作世界模型和视频预训练动作接口中固定容量瓶颈的一种无需新架构、即插即用的升级方案。

Original Abstract: Latent actions provide a compact interface between action-free video and downstream decision-making, yet existing Latent Action Models (LAMs) force every transition through a fixed-capacity bottleneck. We identify a bottleneck trade-off: overly tight codes can discard transition cues needed for action alignment, while overly loose codes preserve additional transition variation that must be resolved when alignment labels are scarce or narrowly distributed. FlexLAM replaces this fixed capacity with variable-length latent actions trained by nested dropout, yielding prefix-valid codes that capture compact transition structure first and add detail only when needed, without new architectures or losses. A single FlexLAM matches or surpasses separately trained fixed-capacity LAMs at every evaluated token budget under standard scarce-label supervision and under a low-return single-task alignment stress test, indicating that FlexLAM is not merely adjustable at inference time but learns a better latent-action interface at the same token budgets. The same model supports inference-time token-budget adjustment without retraining, and FlexLAM improves Ego4D transition reconstruction. These results suggest that variable-length latent actions are an architecture-free, drop-in upgrade to the fixed-capacity bottleneck in latent action models, latent-action world models, and video-pretrained action interfaces.

中文摘要翻译: 潜动作为无动作视频与下游决策之间提供了紧凑的接口，然而现有潜动作模型（LAM）迫使每一段过渡都通过一个固定容量的瓶颈。我们识别出一种瓶颈权衡：过于紧凑的编码会丢弃动作对齐所需的过渡线索，而过于宽松的编码保留了额外的过渡变异性，在动作对齐标签稀缺或分布狭窄时必须加以分辨。FlexLAM 用变长潜动作取代这一固定容量，通过嵌套 dropout 训练得到前缀有效的编码——先捕获紧凑的过渡结构，仅在需要时增加细节，无需引入新架构或损失函数。在标准稀缺标签监督下以及低回报单任务对齐压力测试中，单个 FlexLAM 在每种评估的 token 预算下均匹配或超越了分别训练的固定容量 LAM。这表明 FlexLAM 不仅能在推理时调整参数，而且在相同的 token 预算下学习到了更好的潜动作接口。同一模型支持推理时调整 token 预算而无需重新训练，FlexLAM 还改进了 Ego4D 过渡重建。这些结果表明，变长潜动作是对潜动作模型、潜动作世界模型和视频预训练动作接口中固定容量瓶颈的一种无需新架构、即插即用的升级方案。

</details>


### [125] [Spectral DPPs via NEPv: A Scalable Continuous Relaxation of Determinantal MAP for Diversity-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19411)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将DPP的MAP子集选择问题重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化，并转化为一种新型非线性特征值问题（NEPv），通过自洽场迭代求解，得到仅需矩阵-向量乘积、近线性时间复杂度的算法，适用于大规模数据策展场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DPP-MAP问题（选取规模为k、行列式最大的子集）是NP-hard的，现有贪心算法和采样算法在候选集规模n（数百万到数十亿）时开销过大，无法满足数据中心时代大规模多样性子集选择的需求。

Method: 将DPP-MAP重铸为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，推导出其最优性条件构成一类新型NEPv（特征向量依赖的非线性特征值问题），并提出基于谱隙局部收缩保证的自洽场（SCF）迭代算法（OurMethod），仅需核矩阵-向量乘积。

Result: 算法时间复杂度为O((ndk+nk²)t)，其中t为迭代次数（较小），在n上呈近线性缩放，可直接兼容低秩核和特征图核，理论证明了基于谱隙的局部收缩保证。

Conclusion: 本文聚焦于理论松弛、求解器设计与可扩展性分析，为大规模DPP-MAP问题提供了一种理论严谨且计算高效的迭代求解框架，实证研究留待后续工作。

Original Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse, high-quality subset from a massive pool of candidates is a recurring primitive in modern machine learning -- data curation and coreset selection for training and fine-tuning large models, active-learning batch acquisition, prompt and exemplar selection for in-context learning, retrieval diversification, and experimental design. Determinantal Point Processes (\DPP s) give a principled, well-calibrated notion of diversity for this task, but their \emph{MAP} objective -- pick a size-$k$ subset $S$ maximizing $\logdet(L_S)$ -- is NP-hard, and the standard greedy and sampling algorithms scale superlinearly in the ground-set size $n$. This cost is prohibitive precisely in the data-centric regime where diversity matters most, where $n$ ranges over millions to billions of candidate examples, features, or embeddings. We recast \DPP-MAP as a continuous optimization problem over the Stiefel manifold, and show that its first-order optimality conditions form a \emph{Nonlinear Eigenvalue Problem with eigenvector dependency} (\NEPv) of a previously unstudied form. This \NEPv\ admits a self-consistent field (\SCF) iteration with a spectral-gap-based local contraction guarantee, giving a principled iterative solver where the diversity objective drives an eigenvector-dependent operator. The resulting algorithm, \OurMethod, requires only matrix-vector products with the kernel and runs in time $O\!\big((ndk+nk^2)\,t\big)$ for a small number of iterations $t$, scaling near-linearly in $n$ and integrating directly with low-rank and feature-map kernels common in ML. This paper focuses on the relaxation, solver, and scaling analysis; full real-data benchmarking is left to a planned empirical study.

中文摘要翻译: 从海量候选中选择一个小型、多样且高质量的子集，是现代机器学习中反复出现的基本操作——包括用于训练和微调大模型的数据策展与核心集选择、主动学习的批次获取、上下文学习的提示与示例选择、检索多样化以及实验设计。行列式点过程（DPP）为此任务提供了原理清晰、校准良好的多样性度量，但其MAP目标——选取规模为k的子集S最大化logdet(L_S)——是NP-hard的，且标准的贪心和采样算法在候选集规模n上呈超线性缩放。这一开销在多样性至关重要的数据中心场景中恰恰是 prohibitive的，因为此时n覆盖数百万到数十亿的候选样本、特征或嵌入。本文将DPP-MAP重述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，并证明其最优性条件构成一种先前未被研究的、具有特征向量依赖性的非线性特征值问题（NEPv）。该NEPv允许采用具有基于谱隙的局部收缩保证的自洽场（SCF）迭代，从而得到一种原理性的迭代求解器，其中多样性目标驱动着一个依赖于特征向量的算子。由此产生的算法OurMethod仅需核矩阵-向量乘积，运行时间为O((ndk+nk²)t)（t为较小的迭代次数），在n上呈近线性缩放，并可直接兼容机器学习中常见的低秩核和特征图核。本文聚焦于松弛、求解器和可扩展性分析；完整的真实数据基准测试留待计划的实证研究。

</details>


### [126] [Spectral Retrieval-Augmented Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19412)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SpecReTF的新型检索增强时间序列预测方法，通过将时间序列转换为窗口频域表示，并结合幅度与相位信息的相似度度量以及指数移动平均加权方案，解决了现有检索方法中频谱盲区和时间近因性两大局限性，在非平稳时间序列预测任务上优于传统时域检索方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于检索的时间序列预测方法存在两个根本性局限：(1) 频谱盲区——忽略了捕捉周期性结构的关键频域特征；(2) 时间近因性——对所有历史数据一视同仁，未能强调更近、更相关的模式。

Method: SpecReTF方法包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将时间序列转换为窗口化频域表示；(2) 使用结合幅度和相位信息的组合度量计算相似性；(3) 应用指数移动平均加权方案以平衡近因性与历史上下文。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SpecReTF在多样化的非平稳时间序列预测任务中优于时域检索方法，取得了更高的预测精度。

Conclusion: SpecReTF通过引入频域表示和指数移动平均加权机制，有效克服了传统检索方法在非平稳时间序列预测中的局限性，为检索增强的时间序列预测提供了更优的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Time series forecasting leverages historical patterns to predict future values, but traditional methods face challenges when dealing with complex, non-stationary patterns that are difficult to memorize during training. Retrieval-augmented approaches have emerged as promising solutions by retrieving similar historical patterns to enhance predictions. However, existing retrieval methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: spectral blindness, which overlooks critical frequency-domain characteristics that capture underlying periodic structures, and temporal recency, which treats all historical data equally without emphasizing recent, more relevant patterns. In this paper, we propose SpecReTF, a novel retrieval method that addresses these issues by converting time series into windowed frequency representations, measuring similarity with a combined metric that captures both amplitude and phase information. To balance recency and historical context, we apply an exponential moving average weighting scheme that emphasizes recent windows. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SpecReTF outperforms time-domain retrieval methods, achieving superior forecasting accuracy across diverse, non-stationary time series.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测利用历史模式来预测未来值，但传统方法在处理复杂、非平稳模式时面临挑战，因为这些模式在训练过程中难以记忆。检索增强方法作为一种有前景的解决方案应运而生，通过检索相似的历史模式来增强预测。然而，现有检索方法存在两个根本性局限：频谱盲区——忽略了捕捉底层周期性结构的关键频域特征；以及时间近因性——对所有历史数据一视同仁，未能强调更具相关性的近期模式。本文提出了SpecReTF，一种新颖的检索方法，通过将时间序列转换为窗口化频域表示，并采用结合幅度和相位信息的组合度量来计算相似性，从而解决了上述问题。为了平衡近因性与历史上下文，我们应用了指数的移动平均加权方案来强调近期窗口。在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SpecReTF优于时域检索方法，在多样化的非平稳时间序列上取得了更高的预测精度。

</details>


### [127] [Does Text Actually Help? Uncovering and Resolving Text Collapse in Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19413)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态时间序列预测中文本分支的“文本坍缩”问题（文本分支退化为内容无关的变换），并提出REST-TS方法，通过让文本分支专门学习数值残差的结构化成分，强制其提取真实内容，实现SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态时间序列预测框架中，文本分支存在“文本坍缩”现象——文本分支收敛为与输入内容无关的变换，贡献的判别信号可忽略不计。其根本原因是数值输入与输出之间存在强自相关性，数值主干天然占主导地位，而携带互补关键信息的文本分支被系统性低度利用。

Method: 提出REST-TS（Residual-Exclusive Supervision for Text in Time Series），将不对称性转化为设计原则：数值主干独立产生数值预测，文本分支被专门监督去预测残差（数值无法解释的预测缺口）中的结构化成分。由于数值路径无法减少这些损失，文本分支被迫从输入描述中提取真实内容。

Result: 在多个真实世界领域和骨干架构上的评估表明，REST-TS实现了最优性能，并且相比现有框架，文本分支的利用率持续更高，强有力地证明了在残差上监督文本分支能迫使其从输入中提取真实内容。

Conclusion: 文本坍缩是多模态时间序列预测中数值-文本不对称性导致的系统性缺陷，REST-TS通过残差专属监督策略有效解决了这一问题，为多模态时间序列预测提供了新的设计范式。

Original Abstract: Multimodal time series forecasting, which pairs numerical sequences with domain-relevant textual reports, promises to inject world knowledge into forecasting pipelines. However, we uncover a critical failure mode in existing frameworks that we term text collapse: the text branch converges to a content-independent transformation, contributing negligible discriminative signal regardless of the input description. We argue that text collapse is a consequence of a fundamental asymmetry in time series forecasting: the numerical input is strongly autocorrelated with the output, making the numerical backbone inherently dominant, while the text branch, despite carrying complementary and often critical information, is insufficiently utilized, leading to its systematic underexploitation. To address this, we propose \textbf{REST-TS} (\textbf{R}esidual-\textbf{E}xclusive \textbf{S}upervision for \textbf{T}ext in \textbf{T}ime \textbf{S}eries), which turns the asymmetry into a design principle: the numerical backbone produces its own independent numerical forecast, and the text branch is exclusively supervised to predict the structured components of the residual, the prediction gap that numbers cannot explain. Because no numerical pathway can reduce these losses, the text branch must extract genuine content from the input description. Evaluated across diverse real-world domains and backbone architectures, REST-TS achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently demonstrates greater text-branch utilization than existing frameworks, providing strong empirical evidence that supervising the text branch on the residual compels it to extract genuine content from the input.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态时间序列预测将数值序列与领域相关的文本报告配对，有望为预测流程注入世界知识。然而，我们揭示了现有框架中存在一个关键故障模式——我们称之为文本坍缩：文本分支收敛为一种与内容无关的变换，无论输入描述如何，其贡献的可判别信号都微乎其微。我们认为文本坍缩是时间序列预测中一个根本性不对称现象的后果：数值输入与输出之间存在强自相关性，使得数值主干天然占据主导地位，而文本分支尽管携带互补且通常关键的信息，却未被充分利用，导致其被系统性低度利用。为解决这一问题，我们提出REST-TS（时间序列中文本的残差专属监督），将不对称性转化为设计原则：数值主干独立生成其自身的数值预测，而文本分支被专门监督以预测残差（数值无法解释的预测缺口）中的结构化成分。由于任何数值路径都无法减少这些损失，文本分支必须从输入描述中提取真实内容。在多个真实世界领域和骨干架构上的评估表明，REST-TS实现了最优性能，并且相比现有框架，文本分支的利用率持续更高，这为以下观点提供了强有力的实证证据：在残差上监督文本分支能够迫使其从输入中提取真实内容。

</details>


### [128] [MortarBench: Evaluating Mortgage Loan Origination Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19416)
*Matthew Toles,Yunan Lu,Manav Munjal,Bojun Liu,Yuanhao Deng,Stephanie Selig,Derek Rindner,Cheng Li,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MortarBench，一个贷款发放智能体基准测试，揭示了SOTA大语言模型在该任务上表现不佳且存在姓名偏见，并提出了CRIT置信度校准框架以提升准确率、改善风险管理并减少偏见。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管企业已开始使用贷款智能体辅助人工贷款员，但缺乏公开基准来评估其性能，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了MortarBench基准测试，通过金融数据合成与变异流水线生成覆盖广泛边缘案例且符合真实分布的样本；提出了CRIT（置信度校准框架）来提升模型表现。

Result: 闭源SOTA大语言模型在MortarBench上最高仅达到77.1%的精确匹配准确率；存在与非英文姓名相关的系统性偏差；CRIT框架将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善了风险管理导向并减少了偏见。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在贷款发放任务中性能有限且存在偏见，所提出的CRIT框架能有效提升性能、改善风险控制并减轻偏见。

Original Abstract: Loan origination is the process by which a lender creates a new loan, from application and underwriting through approval and funding. This process serves a critical role in evaluating the eligibility and level of risk posed by an applicant. Recently, firms have begun using mortgage loan agents to augment human loan officers, despite a lack of any public benchmark. To fill this gap, we present MortarBench, a loan origination agent benchmark. MortarBench uses a financial data synthesis and mutation pipeline to generate examples with broad edge case coverage that match real-world distributions and questions. We find that state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) perform poorly, with closed-source models achieving at most 77.1\% exact match accuracy. We also discover systematic biases in LLM perception of foreignness related to non-English names. Noting these weaknesses, we introduce CRIT, a confidence calibration framework. Our method increases accuracy to 80.5\% while improving risk management steering and reducing bias.

中文摘要翻译: 贷款发放是贷方从申请、承保到审批、放款创建新贷款的过程。该过程在评估申请人的资格和风险水平方面起着关键作用。近年来，尽管缺乏任何公开基准，企业已开始使用抵押贷款智能体来辅助人工贷款员。为填补这一空白，我们提出了MortarBench，一个贷款发放智能体基准测试。MortarBench使用金融数据合成与变异流水线，生成覆盖广泛边缘案例且符合真实分布与问题的样本。我们发现，最先进的大语言模型表现不佳，闭源模型最高仅达到77.1%的精确匹配准确率。我们还发现大语言模型在感知与非英语姓名相关的"外国性"时存在系统性偏见。注意到这些不足，我们引入了CRIT，一个置信度校准框架。我们的方法将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善了风险管理导向并减少了偏见。

</details>


### [129] [3D-DLP: Self-Supervised 3D Object-Centric Scene Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19451)
*Ellina Zhang,Madhaven Iyengar,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-DLP，一种基于3D潜在粒子的自监督目标中心表示学习方法，能从场景级RGB-D或体素观测中分解出解耦的3D实体表示，并通过操控粒子位置生成新场景，在机器人操作任务中优于缺乏3D结构信息的基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有目标中心表示学习多在2D空间进行，缺乏显式的3D结构信息，而密集3D输入又存在内存开销大、缺乏目标中心结构的问题。本文旨在学习一种紧凑、可解释且可控的3D目标中心表示。

Method: 基于Deep Latent Particles (DLP)框架，将场景级RGB-D或体素观测分解为一组3D潜在粒子，每个粒子编码解耦的属性（包括3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征），并通过端到端自监督重建目标学习可解释的逐粒子分割图。

Result: 在仿真和真实数据集上，学习的潜在空间具有可解释性和可控性，可通过操控粒子位置解码生成新的场景配置；在下游机器人操作任务中，利用紧凑的3D潜在粒子相比缺乏显式3D信息的基线或依赖密集3D输入的基线表现更好。

Conclusion: 3D-DLP证明了学习解耦的3D目标中心表示的有效性，这种表示不仅可解释和可控，还能提升下游机器人操作任务的性能，为3D场景理解与操作提供了一种紧凑而结构化的表示范式。

Original Abstract: We introduce 3D-DLP, a self-supervised object-centric representation learning model that decomposes scene-level RGB-D or voxel observations into a set of 3D latent particles. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, each particle encodes disentangled attributes, including 3D keypoint position, bounding box dimensions, and appearance features, and represents a distinct entity in the scene. The model learns interpretable per-particle segmentation maps through an end-to-end self-supervised reconstruction objective. We demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that the learned latent space is interpretable and controllable: by manipulating particle positions and decoding, we can generate novel scene configurations. Furthermore, we show that leveraging these compact 3D latent particles for downstream robotic manipulation improves performance over baselines that either lack explicit 3D information or rely on memory-intensive dense 3D inputs without object-centric structure. Code and videos are available at https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了3D-DLP，这是一种自监督的目标中心表示学习模型，能够将场景级RGB-D或体素观测分解为一组3D潜在粒子。该模型建立在深度潜在粒子（DLP）框架之上，每个粒子编码解耦的属性，包括3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征，并代表场景中的一个独立实体。模型通过端到端的自监督重建目标学习可解释的逐粒子分割图。我们在仿真和真实数据集上证明，所学到的潜在空间是可解释且可控的：通过操控粒子位置并解码，我们可以生成新颖的场景配置。此外，我们展示了将这些紧凑的3D潜在粒子用于下游机器人操作任务时，相比于缺乏显式3D信息或依赖无目标中心结构的内存密集型密集3D输入的基线方法，性能有所提升。代码和视频可在https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp获取。

</details>


### [130] [Can In-Context Learning Support Intrinsic Curiosity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19476)
*Eric Elmoznino,Sangnie Bhardwaj,Johannes von Oswald,Rajai Nasser,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,João Sacramento,Rif A. Saurous,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨能否利用序列模型的内在学习能力（上下文学习）作为即时、无需梯度更新的世界模型，来高效计算“学习进展”奖励，从而实现可扩展的自动数据选择（内在好奇心）。理论证明：在一般马尔可夫决策过程中无法无偏实现；但在非时间设置的广泛子类（如主动学习、贝叶斯实验设计）中，该方法可行且能渐近收敛到真实学习进展。实验验证了该方法能成功训练出最优探索的数据收集策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于“学习进展”的探索奖励方法需要在内循环中执行昂贵的梯度下降更新来计算每个新观测如何改善世界模型的预测能力，导致计算成本过高，难以大规模应用。本文旨在利用序列模型涌现的上下文学习能力来消除这一瓶颈，无需梯度更新即可作为世界模型。

Method: 利用序列模型的上下文学习（ICL）能力作为即时、无需梯度更新的世界模型，通过预测误差和反事实上下文操作来估计学习进展，并训练探索策略最大化该奖励。理论上分别在一般MDP和非时间设置（主动学习、贝叶斯实验设计）下分析了方法的可行性与偏差。

Result: 理论结果：1）在一般MDP中，基于ICL的奖励无法无偏估计真实学习进展，要么存在干扰项导致偏差，要么无法用ICL的预测误差实现；2）在非时间设置子类中，ICL导出的奖励能成功界合并渐近收敛到真实学习进展。实验验证：在连续和符号环境中，该方法成功训练出进行最优探索的好奇心数据收集策略。

Conclusion: 上下文学习能力可以替代昂贵的梯度更新来实现基于学习进展的探索，但这一成功局限于特定设置（非时间环境如主动学习和贝叶斯实验设计），在一般MDP中无法无偏实现。这为可扩展的内在好奇心机制提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Original Abstract: Effective machine learning depends not only on how we model data, but also on what data we choose to collect. While large sequence models have revolutionized data modeling, the problem of automated data selection, or "intrinsic curiosity", remains a significant challenge. Classic approaches incentivize exploration by rewarding an agent based on its "learning progress", which measures how much a newly acquired observation improves a world model's predictive ability. However, evaluating these rewards traditionally requires expensive inner loops of gradient descent updates within each trajectory, rendering them computationally impractical at scale. In this work, we investigate whether the emergent in-context learning (ICL) capabilities of sequence models can eliminate this bottleneck by serving as immediate, update-free world models. Specifically, we evaluate whether an exploration policy can be trained to maximize learning progress, using solely the prediction errors and counterfactual context manipulations of an in-context learner. We first prove that in general Markov decision processes, this is in fact impossible in an unbiased way: the resulting intrinsic rewards either suffer from nuisance terms that bias their estimation of true learning progress, or they cannot be implemented using an in-context learner's prediction errors. Conversely, we prove a positive result for a broad subclass of non-temporal settings, encompassing active learning and Bayesian Experimental Design: here, ICL-derived rewards successfully bound and asymptotically converge to the true learning progress. We corroborate our theory with controlled experiments across continuous and symbolic environments, demonstrating that our ICL-driven framework successfully trains curious data-collection policies that explore optimally.

中文摘要翻译: 有效的机器学习不仅取决于我们如何对数据建模，还取决于我们选择收集哪些数据。虽然大型序列模型已经革新了数据建模，但自动数据选择（即"内在好奇心"）问题仍然是一个重大挑战。经典方法通过基于代理的"学习进展"（衡量新获取的观测多大程度上改善了世界模型的预测能力）来激励探索。然而，传统上评估这些奖励需要在每条轨迹内部执行昂贵的梯度下降内循环更新，使其在规模上难以实际应用。在这项工作中，我们研究了序列模型涌现的上下文学习能力能否通过作为即时、无需更新的世界模型来消除这一瓶颈。具体来说，我们评估了是否可以训练探索策略来最大化学习进展，仅使用上下文学习器的预测误差和反事实上下文操作。我们首先证明，在一般马尔可夫决策过程中，这实际上无法以无偏方式实现：由此产生的内在奖励要么遭受干扰项导致其对真实学习进展的估计有偏，要么无法使用上下文学习器的预测误差来实现。相反，我们对非时间设置的一个广泛子类（涵盖主动学习和贝叶斯实验设计）证明了正面结果：在此，基于上下文学习的奖励成功界定了真实学习进展并渐近收敛到它。我们通过连续和符号环境中的受控实验验证了我们的理论，证明我们基于上下文学习的框架成功训练了以最优方式进行探索的好奇心数据收集策略。

</details>


### [131] [Insulin4RL: Real-Time Insulin Management in the Intensive Care Unit for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19481)
*Thomas Frost,Steve Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Insulin4RL，一个基于 MIMIC-IV 构建的医疗离线强化学习数据集，包含来自 12,209 名 ICU 患者的 37.5 万条非均匀时间间隔的胰岛素滴定决策数据，旨在解决现有 EHR 数据集因时间离散化导致的临床场景表示失真和模型泛化性不足问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前离线强化学习在医疗决策中的训练与评估严重依赖将电子健康记录（EHR）数据按固定规则时间间隔进行离散化处理，这种离散化方式扭曲了复杂临床场景的真实表示，并损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化能力。因此，需要一个具有自然非均匀时间间隔和真实临床轨迹的基准数据集。

Method: 从 MIMIC-IV 数据库中提取真实临床轨迹，构建包含非均匀时间间隔输入和动作的医疗 ORL 数据集 Insulin4RL；采用无模型的离线强化学习方法提供基线性能指标，并使用拟合 Q 评估（Fitted Q-evaluation）提供标准化评估协议。

Result: 数据集包含 12,209 名需要 ICU 胰岛素输注滴定的患者，共计超过 37.5 万个标记决策；提供了基线性能指标和标准化评估协议。

Conclusion: Insulin4RL 可作为研究离线强化学习模型在真实临床采样假设下性能的重要资源，并提出了未来可供探索的研究方向。

Original Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (ORL) offers the potential to improve the quality of clinical decision-making using historical electronic health record (EHR) data. Current training and evaluative practices in this field rely heavily on EHR datasets that have been temporally discretised into fixed, regular time intervals. Discretisation creates fictional representations of complex clinical scenarios and compromises the generalisability of retrospective model evaluations. In this paper, we introduce Insulin4RL, a healthcare ORL dataset featuring naturally irregular inputs and actions from real clinical trajectories. Derived from MIMIC-IV, Insulin4RL comprises over 375,000 labelled decisions across 12,209 patients requiring insulin infusion titration in the Intensive Care Unit. The dataset can thus be used for research into ORL model performance under realistic clinical sampling assumptions. We provide a description of the dataset's structure and characteristics, baseline performance metrics using model-free offline reinforcement learning, and a standardised evaluation protocol using fitted Q-evaluation. We conclude with suggested areas for future research that could be addressed using this resource.

中文摘要翻译: 离线强化学习（ORL）利用历史电子健康记录（EHR）数据，有望提升临床决策质量。当前该领域的训练与评估实践严重依赖按固定规则时间间隔进行离散化处理的 EHR 数据集。离散化操作扭曲了复杂临床场景的真实表示，并损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化能力。本文提出了 Insulin4RL，这是一个医疗 ORL 数据集，其输入和动作均来自真实临床轨迹中的自然非均匀采样。Insulin4RL 基于 MIMIC-IV 构建，包含来自 12,209 名需要在重症监护室进行胰岛素输注滴定的患者的超过 37.5 万个标记决策。该数据集可用于研究 ORL 模型在真实临床采样假设下的性能表现。我们提供了数据集的结构与特征描述、基于无模型离线强化学习的基线性能指标，以及使用拟合 Q 评估的标准化评估协议。最后，我们提出了未来可利用该资源进行探索的研究方向。

</details>


### [132] [Concept Flow Models: Anchoring Concept-Based Reasoning with Hierarchical Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19489)
*Ya Wang,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概念流模型(CFM)，用层次化概念决策树替代扁平瓶颈层，减少信息泄露并增强可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型(CBM)中，当概念数量接近嵌入维度时，信息泄露增加，模型会利用虚假或语义无关的相关性，削弱可解释性。

Method: CFM用层次化的概念驱动决策树替代扁平瓶颈层：从视觉嵌入构建决策层次，在每个层级分布语义概念，通过概率树遍历训练可微的概念权重，逐步缩小预测范围。

Result: 在多个基准上，CFM在保持与扁平CBM相当预测性能的同时，通过减少有效概念使用显著缓解了信息泄露，并生成逐步决策流实现透明可审计的推理。

Conclusion: 层次化概念结构能有效缓解信息泄露问题，在保持预测性能的同时提升模型可解释性，并提供可审计的层次化推理过程。

Original Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) enhance interpretability by projecting learned features into a human-understandable concept space. Recent approaches leverage vision-language models to generate concept embeddings, reducing the need for manual concept annotations. However, these models suffer from a critical limitation: as the number of concepts approaches the embedding dimension, information leakage increases, enabling the model to exploit spurious or semantically irrelevant correlations and undermining interpretability. In this work, we propose Concept Flow Models (CFMs), which replace the flat bottleneck with a hierarchical, concept-driven decision tree. Each internal node in the hierarchy focuses on a localized subset of discriminative concepts, progressively narrowing the prediction scope. Our framework constructs decision hierarchies from visual embeddings, distributes semantic concepts at each hierarchy level, and trains differentiable concept weights through probabilistic tree traversal. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CFMs match the predictive performance of flat CBMs, while substantially mitigating information leakage by reducing effective concept usage. Furthermore, CFMs yield stepwise decision flows that enable transparent and auditable model reasoning with hierarchical class structures.

中文摘要翻译: 概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）通过将学习到的特征投影到人类可理解的概念空间中，增强了模型的可解释性。近期的工作利用视觉-语言模型生成概念嵌入，减少了对人工概念标注的需求。然而，这些模型存在一个关键限制：当概念数量接近嵌入维度时，信息泄露增加，使得模型能够利用虚假或语义无关的相关性，从而削弱可解释性。在本工作中，我们提出了概念流模型（CFMs），该模型将扁平瓶颈层替换为层次化的概念驱动决策树。层次结构中的每个内部节点聚焦于一个局部化的判别性子集，逐步缩小预测范围。我们的框架从视觉嵌入构建决策层次，在每个层次级别分布语义概念，并通过概率树遍历训练可微的概念权重。在多种基准上的大量实验表明，CFMs在匹配扁平CBMs预测性能的同时，通过减少有效概念使用显著缓解了信息泄露。此外，CFMs能够生成逐步决策流，从而在层次化类别结构下实现透明且可审计的模型推理。

</details>


### [133] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm变换器中，逆尺度方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖是最终归一化后中心激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，可在无需前向/后向传播或特征分解的情况下，仅从LayerNorm尺度参数读出死方向（Fisher信息退化的方向）。该方向在9/9个LayerNorm模型的随机初始化时与实测底部奇异方向匹配至小数点后四位，在5/5个RMSNorm模型中正确消失。训练后该方向对应协方差特征值加深约10³倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预训练Transformer位于损失函数的近奇异极小值处，Fisher信息矩阵沿死方向退化。现有方法需要前向传播和激活值的特征分解才能定位死方向，无法仅从网络参数直接计算。本文旨在寻找一种仅从参数即可读出的死方向。

Method: 从理论上证明，对于LayerNorm变换器，逆尺度方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖是最终归一化后中心激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核。该方向可直接从LayerNorm的尺度参数γ读出，无需任何前向/后向传播或特征分解。在14个预训练Transformer（9个LayerNorm，5个RMSNorm；160M-35B参数；语言和视觉任务）上进行了实验验证。

Result: 1) 随机初始化时，预测方向与实测底部奇异方向在9/9个LayerNorm模型上匹配至小数点后四位，在5/5个RMSNorm模型上正确消失；2) 训练后沿此方向的协方差特征值加深约10³倍；3) 残差流的最小奇异值在13/14个模型上保持块间不变，唯一例外（Gemma4-31B）可被该方向定位；4) 该核方向的存在性可仅从参数判断归一化层类型。

Conclusion: 本文发现了LayerNorm变换器中一种极其廉价（无需任何数据或计算）的死方向读取方法，揭示了LayerNorm参数与网络奇异结构之间的内在联系，为理解Transformer的优化景观和泛化性质提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练Transformer位于损失函数近奇异极小值处，此时Fisher信息矩阵沿死方向退化（即参数空间中方向Fisher信息量为零的方向）。定位此类方向通常需要一次前向传播和激活值的特征分解，或基于采样的复杂度估计；这些方法都无法仅从网络参数本身计算出方向。本文给出了一种针对LayerNorm变换器的解决方案。对于任意输入分布，LayerNorm仿射变换的逆尺度方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖是最终归一化后中心激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，并对应参数空间中的一个死方向。该方向仅从LN尺度参数即可读出，无需前向或后向传播，也无需特征分解：这是LayerNorm特有的一种最廉价的死方向读取方式。我们在14个预训练Transformer（9个LayerNorm，5个RMSNorm；参数量160M-35B；涵盖语言和视觉任务）上进行了测试。随机初始化时，预测方向与实测底部奇异方向（一次前向传播加直接SVD）在9/9个LayerNorm模型上匹配至小数点后四位，并在5/5个RMSNorm模型上正确消失（RMSNorm缺乏产生该方向的均值减法投影器）。训练后的检查点上，沿此方向的协方差特征值加深约10³倍，并出现更多死方向；随机初始化到训练后的差距可作为沿预测坐标的奇异结构的一次前向传播、逐检查点读出指标。由此得出两个闭式结论：在13/14个Transformer上（在其自身输入分布上测量），残差流的最小奇异值保持块间不变，唯一的例外（Gemma4-31B）是一个真实的死方向，该读出方法可准确定位；此外，核方向的存在性可仅从参数判断变换器的归一化层类型。

</details>


### [134] [Calibrating Generative Models to Feature Distributions with MMD Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19496)
*Nathaniel L. Diamant,Brian L. Trippe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出核校准生成模型(kCGM)，通过最小化生成样本与目标集之间特征分布的最大均值差异(MMD)，在不牺牲有效性的前提下校准生成模型的分布偏移，适用于多种生成模型类型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成模型可能在关键特征分布上与目标集存在显著偏差（如药物分子特征与已知抗生素不匹配），而直接微调目标集容易过拟合且无法控制匹配哪些特征。

Method: kCGM通过无偏得分函数估计量最小化生成样本与目标集特征分布之间的最大均值差异(MMD)，并结合KL正则化使模型保持接近预训练分布。

Result: 在174种抗生素的目标集上，直接微调牺牲了化学有效性来匹配特征分布，而kCGM在提升目标特征匹配的同时提高了有效性；在蛋白质和DNA生成任务中也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: kCGM能够仅利用特征级别的监督信息，有效适配自回归模型、连续空间扩散模型和离散扩散模型，纠正生成模型的分布校准偏差。

Original Abstract: Generative models can produce individually plausible samples while deviating substantially from a target set in the distribution of key features. For example, a model pretrained on broad drug-like chemical space may generate molecules whose molecular features differ from those of a therapeutic class of interest, such as known antibiotics. Correcting such distributional miscalibration is challenging: direct finetuning on the target set can overfit and does not control which features are matched. To fill this gap, we introduce kernel Calibrating Generative Models (kCGM). kCGM minimizes a maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between generated and target feature distributions using an unbiased score-function estimator, with KL regularization to remain close to the pretrained model. On a target set of 174 antibiotics, direct finetuning sacrifices chemical validity for feature-distribution matching, whereas kCGM improves target feature matching while increasing validity. We further demonstrate kCGM in protein and DNA generation tasks, showing it can adapt autoregressive, continuous-space diffusion, and discrete diffusion models using only feature-level supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/smithhenryd/cgm.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型可以产生个体上合理的样本，但在关键特征的整体分布上与目标集存在显著偏差。例如，一个在广泛药物类化学空间上预训练的模型可能生成分子，其分子特征与感兴趣的治疗类别（如已知抗生素）不同。纠正这种分布校准偏差具有挑战性：对目标集直接微调会导致过拟合，且无法控制匹配哪些特征。为填补这一空白，我们引入了核校准生成模型(kCGM)。kCGM通过使用无偏得分函数估计量，最小化生成样本与目标特征分布之间的最大均值差异(MMD)，并结合KL正则化以保持接近预训练模型。在包含174种抗生素的目标集上，直接微调牺牲了化学有效性以换取特征分布匹配，而kCGM在提升目标特征匹配的同时提高了有效性。我们进一步在蛋白质和DNA生成任务中展示了kCGM，证明其能够仅利用特征级别的监督信息适配自回归模型、连续空间扩散模型和离散扩散模型。代码已开源至https://github.com/smithhenryd/cgm。

</details>


### [135] [Interactive Pareto navigation for deep multi-task learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19521)
*Augustina C. Amakor,Konstantin Sonntag,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出偏好帕累托探索（PPE）框架，通过预测-校正方法在帕累托流形上沿偏好方向探索，结合Krylov子空间方法避免显式Hessian计算，实现高效的多目标优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有加权聚合方法难以捕捉决策者偏好且计算成本高昂，特别是在深度学习场景中，需要一种既能体现决策者偏好又能考虑帕累托前沿几何结构的高效多目标优化方法。

Method: 提出PPE框架，采用预测-校正方法：预测步沿帕累托最优解流形的切平面方向推进以遵循决策者偏好，校正步返回一个新的帕累托最优折衷解。使用Krylov子空间方法（仅需矩阵-向量乘积）表征流形切空间，通过自动微分高效实现这些乘积运算。

Result: 在玩具问题以及深度学习示例上验证了该方法的有效性和性能。

Conclusion: PPE框架能够在考虑帕累托集几何结构的同时强制执行决策者偏好，通过交互式探索过程高效生成新的帕累托最优折衷解，避免了显式Hessian计算，适用于深度学习应用。

Original Abstract: In multi-task learning, handling an increasing number of objectives can quickly become challenging, both in terms of the computational resources and the decision maker's capacity to choose appropriate trade-offs. A widely used approach is thus to aggregate the individual losses in a single loss function by a weighted sum. This often fails to capture either the decision maker's preferences as a result of the shape of the Pareto front, or requires multiple adjustments and computations which becomes prohibitively expensive in deep learning applications. To address these issues, we introduce a novel framework, Preference Pareto Exploration (PPE), which enforces the decision maker's preferences while accounting for the geometry of the Pareto set in an interactive exploration process. PPE is based on a predictor-corrector method that performs predictor steps tangential to the manifold of Pareto-optimal solutions, following the decision maker's preference. The subsequent corrector step results in a new trade-off reflecting this preference. To avoid explicit Hessian computations when characterizing the tangent space of the manifold, we employ a Krylov subspace method that relies solely on matrix-vector products. These products can be efficiently obtained via automatic differentiation, ensuring both efficiency and robustness throughout the optimization process. The method's functionality and performance are demonstrated using both toy problems and examples from deep learning.

中文摘要翻译: 在多任务学习中，处理不断增加的优化目标数量很快就会变得具有挑战性，无论是在计算资源方面，还是在决策者选择合适折衷方案的能力方面。因此，一种广泛使用的方法是通过加权求和将各个损失聚合为单个损失函数。由于帕累托前沿的形状，这种方法通常无法捕捉决策者的偏好，或者在深度学习应用中需要进行多次调整和计算，这变得过于昂贵。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖的框架——偏好帕累托探索（PPE），该框架在交互式探索过程中强制执行决策者的偏好，同时考虑帕累托集的几何结构。PPE基于一种预测-校正方法，该方法沿帕累托最优解流形的切平面方向执行预测步，以遵循决策者的偏好。随后的校正步产生反映该偏好的新折衷解。为了避免在表征流形切空间时进行显式Hessian计算，我们采用了一种仅依赖于矩阵-向量乘积的Krylov子空间方法。这些乘积可以通过自动微分高效获得，从而确保整个优化过程的效率和鲁棒性。通过玩具问题和深度学习示例验证了该方法的功能和性能。

</details>


### [136] [Techniques for Peak Memory Reduction for LoRA Fine-tuning of LLMs on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19528)
*Hassan Dbouk,Matthias Reisser,Prathamesh Mandke,Likhita Arun Navali,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套互补技术组合，在不牺牲模型质量的前提下大幅降低大语言模型在消费级硬件上微调时的峰值内存占用，实验表明在3B参数模型上可实现26-28倍的内存缩减。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在消费级硬件上使用LoRA微调大语言模型时，峰值内存往往超出设备限制，特别是对于数十亿参数和长上下文训练数据的模型，严重阻碍了本地个性化部署。

Method: (1) 基础模型量化与即时反量化；(2) 结合选择性激活缓存和磁盘卸载的内存高效检查点技术；(3) 使用语义相关token子集进行softmax近似；(4) logits掩码。

Result: 在Llama-3.2 3B和Qwen-2.5 3B模型上，峰值内存分别降低26倍和28倍，使得在资源受限设备上微调成为可能。

Conclusion: 通过一组互补的显存优化技术，可以在不牺牲模型质量的前提下，显著降低LLM微调的峰值内存占用，从而在消费级硬件上实现大模型的本地微调。

Original Abstract: Fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs) using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on an end-user's data offers personalized experiences while keeping data private, but faces severe memory constraints on consumer hardware. Peak memory during fine-tuning often exceeds device limits, especially for models with billions of parameters and long-context training data. This paper introduces a suite of complementary techniques to reduce memory footprint without sacrificing model quality: (1) base model quantization with on-the-fly dequantization, (2) memory-efficient checkpointing combining selective activation caching and disk offloading, (3) softmax approximation using semantically relevant token subsets, and (4) logits masking. Experiments on Llama-3.2 3B and Qwen-2.5 3B demonstrate up to $26\times$ and $28\times$ reduction in peak memory, enabling fine-tuning on resource-constrained devices.

中文摘要翻译: 使用低秩适配（LoRA）在终端用户数据上对大语言模型（LLM）进行微调，可以在保护数据隐私的同时提供个性化体验，但在消费级硬件上面临严峻的内存限制。微调期间的峰值内存往往超出设备限制，尤其是对于具有数十亿参数和长上下文训练数据的模型。本文引入了一套互补技术，在不牺牲模型质量的前提下降低内存占用：（1）基础模型量化与即时反量化；（2）结合选择性激活缓存和磁盘卸载的内存高效检查点技术；（3）使用语义相关token子集进行softmax近似；（4）logits掩码。在Llama-3.2 3B和Qwen-2.5 3B上的实验表明，峰值内存分别降低了26倍和28倍，从而能够在资源受限设备上进行微调。

</details>


### [137] [Tracking Representation Dynamics in Large Language Models with Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19542)
*Naman Malhotra,Jay Ambadkar,Abhinav Gupta,Kushal Kasivel,Abbas Schwarz,Kamillo Ferry,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用持久同调（persistent homology）追踪大语言模型在监督微调过程中激活空间的拓扑变化，发现拓扑重组主要发生在训练早期，且不同对齐目标会引发可区分的拓扑轨迹，为理解对齐过程提供了表征层面的新视角。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型通常通过监督微调进行对齐，但其内部表征在训练过程中如何演化尚不清楚，现有行为指标难以揭示表征层面的变化。

Method: 使用持久同调（persistent homology）方法，追踪四种参数规模在1B到7B之间的Transformer语言模型在微调过程中激活空间的拓扑结构变化，涵盖helpful、harmless和混合数据三种对齐目标。

Result: 拓扑重组主要发生在训练早期；密集检查点分析显示拓扑活动先出现瞬时峰值后迅速稳定；不同对齐目标诱发可区分的拓扑轨迹；指令微调模型与预训练模型呈现不同的演化模式。

Conclusion: 持久同调为对齐过程提供了互补视角，揭示了行为指标无法体现的表征层面变化。

Original Abstract: Large language models are commonly aligned through supervised fine-tuning, yet little is known about how their internal representations evolve during this process. We study alignment dynamics using persistent homology by tracking the topology of activation spaces throughout fine-tuning. Across four transformer language models ranging from 1B to 7B parameters and three alignment objectives corresponding to helpful, harmless, and mixed training data, we find that the majority of topological reorganization occurs during the earliest stages of training. A dense checkpoint analysis reveals a transient peak in topological activity followed by rapid stabilization. We further show that different alignment objectives induce distinguishable topological trajectories, while instruction-tuned and pretrained models exhibit qualitatively different patterns of evolution. Our results suggest that persistent homology provides a complementary perspective on alignment, revealing representation-level changes that are not apparent from behavioral metrics alone.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型通常通过监督微调进行对齐，但关于其内部表征在该过程中如何演化，我们知之甚少。本文利用持久同调（persistent homology）追踪微调过程中激活空间的拓扑结构，研究了对齐动力学。实验涵盖四种参数规模从1B到7B的Transformer语言模型，以及对应helpful、harmless和混合训练数据的三种对齐目标。我们发现，绝大多数拓扑重组发生在训练的最早阶段。密集检查点分析揭示了一个拓扑活动的瞬时峰值，随后迅速趋于稳定。我们进一步表明，不同的对齐目标会诱发可区分的拓扑轨迹，而指令微调模型与预训练模型则展现出性质不同的演化模式。我们的结果表明，持久同调为对齐过程提供了互补视角，揭示了仅凭行为指标无法观察到的表征层面变化。

</details>


### [138] [Predicting Mergeability of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19549)
*Lin Tang,Wei Zhang,Jing Li,Hongyu Chen,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MergeProbe，一种轻量级预测器，能在LoRA适配器训练早期（前几个百分点）通过测量低秩更新与梯度对齐度、共享表示扰动等信号，预测其合并后的性能，从而将适配器合并从事后工程步骤转变为可预判的测量问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的LoRA适配器合并通常在两个适配器都训练和评估完成后才能发现是否可合并，这种滞后反馈成本高昂——单独表现优异的适配器在合并后可能产生破坏性干扰。因此，需要一种方法在训练早期就预测适配器的可合并性。

Method: MergeProbe通过测量训练早期（前几个百分点）的若干信号来预测合并性：低秩更新在不同任务间的对齐程度、梯度对齐程度，以及它们对共享表示的扰动程度。将这些信号打包成轻量级预测器，估算成对和集合级别的性能保留度，并根据估算结果做出具体决策：直接合并、重新加权、剪枝或路由。

Result: 在涵盖数学、代码、科学、指令遵循和安全五个领域的MERGE-PEFT基准上，MergeProbe在强干扰感知合并基线中取得了最佳的平均保留和最差情况保留，同时部署开销远低于完整任务路由方法。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器合并可以从一个事后工程步骤转变为一个可预判的测量问题，MergeProbe能够在训练早期有效预测合并性，并做出优于现有方法的合并决策。

Original Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) makes it cheap to train many domain- and task-specific language model adapters, but whether two adapters can be merged is usually discovered only after both have been fully trained and evaluated. This late feedback is costly: adapters that are strong in isolation can interfere destructively once their updates are combined. We ask whether this outcome can be anticipated. We formalize adapter mergeability as the degree to which an adapter preserves its single-task utility after merging, and show that it can be forecast from signals measured in the first few percent of training -- chiefly how the low-rank updates and their gradients align across tasks and how much they disturb shared representations. We package these signals into MergeProbe, a lightweight predictor that estimates pairwise and set-level retention and turns the estimate into a concrete decision: merge directly, reweight, prune, or route. On MERGE-PEFT, a five-domain benchmark spanning math, code, science, instruction following, and safety, MergeProbe attains the best average and worst-case retention among strong interference-aware merge baselines while adding far less deployment overhead than full task routing. This turns LoRA merging from a post-hoc engineering step into an anticipatory measurement problem.

中文摘要翻译: 低秩适配（LoRA）使得训练多个领域和任务特定的语言模型适配器变得廉价，但两个适配器是否可合并通常只有在两者都完成训练和评估后才能发现。这种滞后反馈成本高昂：单独表现优异的适配器在更新合并后可能产生破坏性干扰。我们探究是否可以提前预判这一结果。我们将适配器可合并性形式化为适配器在合并后保持其单任务效用的程度，并证明该指标可以从训练前几个百分点的信号中预测——主要是低秩更新及其梯度在不同任务间的对齐程度，以及它们对共享表示的扰动程度。我们将这些信号打包成MergeProbe，一种轻量级预测器，用于估算成对和集合级别的性能保留度，并将估算结果转化为具体决策：直接合并、重新加权、剪枝或路由。在涵盖数学、代码、科学、指令遵循和安全五个领域的MERGE-PEFT基准上，MergeProbe在强干扰感知合并基线中取得了最佳的平均保留和最差情况保留，同时部署开销远低于完整任务路由方法。这将LoRA合并从一个事后工程步骤转变为一个可预判的测量问题。

</details>


### [139] [Displacement Is Not Direction: Evaluating Fidelity Metrics for Quantized LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19558)
*Miloš Nikolić,Ali Hadi Zadeh,Enrique Torres Sanchez,Andreas Moshovos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了以KL散度（KLD）为代表的保真度指标在量化模型质量评估中的有效性，发现KLD在全集上虽与基准分数强相关，但在接近基准性能的“静默区”相关性崩溃，且逐提示层面预测能力弱，不能作为跨模型路由器的可靠指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实践中常用KL散度等保真度指标作为低成本代理来预估模型质量，但这种做法的可靠性缺乏系统性验证。本文旨在检验KLD是否真的能有效反映模型在下游基准上的性能。

Method: 本文基于Qwen3.6-35B-A3B的28个量化变体和Devstral-Small-2-24B的41个量化变体，在多个下游基准上评测KLD与基准分数的相关性。通过全集分析、近基线静默区分析、逐提示故障预测分析、跨模型路由分析以及结构分解（将KLD分解为不一致“体积”与不一致“方向”两部分）来全面检验KLD的有效性。

Result: KLD在全集上与基准分数强相关（Qwen: ρ=-0.72, Devstral: ρ=-0.86, p<0.001），但在近基线静默区相关性崩溃（Qwen: ρ=+0.00, Devstral: ρ=-0.24, p=0.36）。该崩溃在14种测量变体下均存在。逐提示层面，KLD对代码故障的预测能力弱（失败/通过几何均值比仅[1.08,1.22]）；作为跨模型路由器，准确率仅42.3%-49.4%。结构分解表明KLD主要测量不一致的“体积”，与不一致“方向”的关系弱且依赖于任务。

Conclusion: KLD作为模型质量代理的有效性存在严重局限：仅在全集范围有效，在接近基线的静默区完全失效；不能可靠地用于逐提示故障预测，也不能作为跨模型路由器。根本原因在于KLD本质衡量的是与参考模型不一致的“量”，而非不一致的“方向”（即质量高低的方向）。

Original Abstract: Fidelity metrics, such as per-token KL divergence (KLD) against a high-precision reference, are often used in practice as low-cost proxies for benchmark quality. We test this practice on a 28-quant cohort of Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and a 41-quant cohort of Devstral-Small-2-24B, evaluated across a suite of downstream benchmarks. We find that KLD is strongly correlated with benchmark score over the full cohort ($ρ=-0.72$ on Qwen and $ρ=-0.86$ on Devstral, both with $p<0.001$). However, this relationship collapses to non-significance in the near-baseline silent zone ($ρ=+0.00$ on Qwen and $ρ=-0.24$, $p=0.36$, on Devstral). This collapse persists across 14 measurement variants, including different KLD aggregations, perplexity formulations, top-1 agreement, calibration corpora, and context lengths. At the per-prompt level, KLD has only weak failure-prediction power on code, with failed-vs-passed geometric-mean ratios in $[1.08,1.22]$ across five models on LiveCodeBench, and fails as a cross-model router, achieving only $42.3\%-49.4\%$ accuracy on disagreement prompts. We trace the collapse to a structural decomposition: KLD primarily measures the volume of disagreement with the reference, with silent-zone composite $ρ=+0.94$ ($p<0.001$) on Qwen and $+0.55$ ($p=0.03$) on Devstral, while its relationship to the direction of those disagreements is weak and task-conditional.

中文摘要翻译: 保真度指标（如针对高精度参考模型的逐token KL散度，KLD）在实践中常被用作基准质量的低成本代理。本文在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B的28个量化变体队列和Devstral-Small-2-24B的41个量化变体队列上检验了这一做法，并在多个下游基准上进行评估。我们发现，在全队列上KLD与基准分数强相关（Qwen上ρ=-0.72，Devstral上ρ=-0.86，均p<0.001）。然而，在近基线的静默区，这种关系崩溃至不显著（Qwen上ρ=+0.00，Devstral上ρ=-0.24，p=0.36）。这种崩溃在14种测量变体下持续存在，包括不同的KLD聚合方式、困惑度公式、top-1一致性、校准语料库以及上下文长度。在逐提示层面，KLD在代码任务上仅具有微弱的故障预测能力，五个模型在LiveCodeBench上的失败与通过的几何均值比为[1.08,1.22]；作为跨模型路由器，KLD在分歧提示上仅达到42.3%-49.4%的准确率。我们将此崩溃追溯到结构分解：KLD主要衡量与参考模型的不一致体积，静默区的复合ρ在Qwen上为+0.94（p<0.001），在Devstral上为+0.55（p=0.03），而其与这些不一致的方向的关系则很弱且依赖于任务。

</details>


### [140] [Understanding Key Features of Time Series Foundation Models from Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19560)
*Alireza Jafari,Judy Fox,Geoffrey C. Fox,Madhav Marathe,Aniruddha Adiga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种现代预测架构（经典神经网络、数值Transformer、预训练时序基础模型及LLM）在美国区域流感预测中的表现，发现融合多种预训练预测器的混合专家模型整体最优，数值Transformer表现可靠，而LLM方法性能较差。住院信息作为辅助协变量可在特定场景下提升预测鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 季节性流感每年在美国造成大量发病和死亡，准确的短期预测对公共卫生决策至关重要，但现代预测架构在传染病监测数据上的比较行为尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 系统评估方法：使用ILI监测数据和流感相关住院时间序列，在时间和空间泛化设置下对1-4周前瞻预测，比较经典神经网络、数值Transformer、预训练时序基础模型和LLM预测方法。

Result: （1）融合多种预训练预测器的混合专家模型整体性能最强；（2）数值Transformer产生可靠预测；（3）预训练在长周期预测中收益最大，尤其当预训练域与流感动态机制对齐时；（4）LLM方法性能不如数值预测器；（5）住院信号在特定设置下提供互补性改进。

Conclusion: 研究为流感防控中的模型选择、预训练策略及辅助信号使用提供了可操作的指导建议。

Original Abstract: Seasonal influenza infects millions of people and causes substantial morbidity and mortality in the United States each year, making accurate short-term forecasting a core public-health need. Reliable forecasts of epidemic time series can inform vaccination timing, hospital staffing, and resource allocation, yet the comparative behavior of modern forecasting architectures on infectious-disease surveillance data remains insufficiently characterized. We address this gap through a systematic evaluation of regional influenza forecasting using influenza-like illness surveillance and influenza-associated hospitalization time series under both temporal and spatial generalization settings for 1-4-week-ahead prediction. We compare classical neural network architectures, numerical transformer-based models, pretrained time series foundation models, and LLM-based forecasting approaches. Across tasks, we demonstrate that a mixture-of-experts model that fuses multiple pretrained forecasters achieves the strongest overall performance, indicating that heterogeneous pretrained representations provide complementary predictive information. Our results further show that numerical transformer-based models produce reliable forecasts, while pretraining provides the largest gains at longer horizons, particularly when the pretraining domain is mechanistically aligned with influenza dynamics. In contrast, LLM-based time series methods underperform relative to numerical forecasters in this setting. Finally, we examine hospitalization information as both an auxiliary covariate and a pretraining source. Hospitalization signals provide complementary improvements in selected settings and clarify when additional surveillance streams enhance the robustness of multi-horizon forecasting. These findings provide actionable guidance on model selection, pretraining strategy, and auxiliary-signal use for influenza preparedness.

中文摘要翻译: 季节性流感每年在美国感染数百万人，并导致大量的发病和死亡，使得准确的短期预测成为核心公共卫生需求。流行病时间序列的可靠预测可为疫苗接种时机、医院人员配备和资源分配提供信息，然而现代预测架构在传染病监测数据上的比较行为尚未得到充分刻画。我们通过系统评估区域性流感预测来填补这一空白，使用流感样疾病监测和流感相关住院时间序列，在时间和空间泛化设置下进行1-4周前瞻预测。我们比较了经典神经网络架构、数值型Transformer模型、预训练时间序列基础模型以及基于LLM的预测方法。综合各任务，我们发现融合多种预训练预测器的混合专家模型取得了最强的整体性能，表明异质性预训练表征能提供互补的预测信息。我们的结果进一步表明，数值型Transformer模型能产生可靠的预测，而预训练在更长预测周期中带来的收益最大，尤其是当预训练领域与流感动力学机制对齐时。相比之下，基于LLM的时间序列方法在该场景下表现不及数值预测器。最后，我们考察了住院信息作为辅助协变量和预训练源的作用。住院信号在选定的设置中提供了互补性改进，并阐明了额外监测流如何增强多步前瞻预测的鲁棒性。这些发现为流感防控中的模型选择、预训练策略和辅助信号使用提供了可操作的指导。

</details>


### [141] [Advances in Scientific Machine Learning for Coupled Fluid Flow and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19562)
*Gabriel F. Barros,Rômulo M. Silva,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 综述了科学机器学习(SciML)在耦合流体流动与输运现象建模中的最新进展，重点介绍了基于SVD的线性降阶技术（如DMD）和基于神经网络的非线性方法（如PINNs和β-VAEs），并展示了这些方法在高性能计算策略下的两项新贡献：浊流代理建模和热流解缠非线性模式提取。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高保真数值模拟不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程与标量输运方程耦合的流体系统（如浊流和热对流）计算成本极高，因为这些系统具有强非线性耦合和多尺度行为。亟需构建高效、准确的代理模型来替代全阶数值模拟，以显著降低计算开销。

Method: 综述了线性降阶方法（基于SVD的DMD）和非线性神经网络方法（PINNs、β-VAEs）。新贡献包括：(1) 利用PINNs对浊流进行代理建模；(2) 利用β-VAEs从热流中提取解缠的非线性模式。结合高性能计算策略，如自适应网格细化和科学浮点数据压缩。

Result: 通过锁交换流和Rayleigh-Bénard对流等经典基准问题验证了所提方法的有效性。结果表明，SciML方法能够在特定数据范围和建模假设下，以远低于全阶模拟的计算成本，实现对复杂耦合系统的快速、准确近似。

Conclusion: SciML方法能够显著降低耦合流体系统的计算成本，同时保持较高的近似精度。实时预测和不确定性量化等更广泛的能力仍是活跃的研究方向，其可行性高度依赖于具体问题。

Original Abstract: This chapter reviews recent advances in Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) for modeling coupled fluid flow and transport phenomena governed by the incompressible Navier-Stokes and scalar transport equations. Such systems, found in applications like turbidity currents and thermal convection, feature strong nonlinear coupling and multiscale behavior that make high-fidelity simulations computationally expensive. To address this, the chapter surveys state-of-the-art SciML methods for building efficient surrogate models, including linear reduced-order techniques based on Singular Value Decomposition (such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition) and nonlinear neural network approaches like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) and $β$-Variational Autoencoders ($β$-VAEs). It first covers the authors' work combining these models with High Performance Computing strategies, including Adaptive Mesh Refinement/Coarsening (AMR/C) and scientific floating-point data compression. It then presents two new contributions: surrogate modeling of turbidity currents via PINNs, and the extraction of disentangled nonlinear modes from thermal flows using $β$-VAEs. Governing equations and representative benchmarks, including lock-exchange flows and Rayleigh-Bénard convection, illustrate these methodologies. The chapter is intentionally long, covering both the mathematical and physical foundations of coupled fluid flow and the computational aspects of state-of-the-art modeling. Overall, it demonstrates how SciML enables fast, accurate approximations of complex coupled systems within the specific data regimes and modeling assumptions considered, while substantially reducing computational cost relative to full-order simulations. Broader capabilities such as real-time prediction and uncertainty quantification remain active research directions whose feasibility depends strongly on the problem at hand.

中文摘要翻译: 本章回顾了科学机器学习(SciML)在模拟由不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程和标量输运方程控制的耦合流体流动与输运现象中的最新进展。这类系统常见于浊流和热对流等应用，具有强非线性耦合和多尺度行为，使得高保真模拟的计算成本极高。为此，本章综述了构建高效代理模型的最先进SciML方法，包括基于奇异值分解的线性降阶技术（如动态模式分解）和非线性神经网络方法（如物理信息神经网络和β-变分自编码器）。首先介绍了作者将这些模型与高性能计算策略（包括自适应网格细化/粗化和科学浮点数据压缩）结合的工作。随后提出了两项新贡献：基于PINNs的浊流代理建模，以及利用β-VAEs从热流中提取解缠的非线性模式。控制方程和代表性基准问题（包括锁交换流和Rayleigh-Bénard对流）对这些方法进行了说明。本章篇幅较长，涵盖了耦合流体流动的数学和物理基础，以及最先进建模的计算方面。总体而言，本章展示了SciML如何在特定数据范围和建模假设下，实现对复杂耦合系统的快速、准确近似，同时相对于全阶模拟大幅降低计算成本。实时预测和不确定性量化等更广泛的能力仍是活跃的研究方向，其可行性强烈依赖于具体问题。

</details>


### [142] [On the QUEST for Uncertainty Quantification via Highest Density Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19569)
*Sam Goring,Tom Kuipers,Nicola Paoletti,David S. Watson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最高密度区域（HDR）的不确定性量化框架QUEST，用于解决传统基于逐点预测风险的方法在非条件期望目标统计量下产生反直觉结果的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于适当评分规则的不确定性量化方法（如方差）衡量的是逐点预测风险，当目标统计量不是条件期望时会产生反直觉结果，且不满足一些期望的UQ公理。

Method: QUEST基于Lebesgue测度在分布峰值处的集中程度来度量不确定性，通过鲁棒参数α进行调节，并与信息论和经济学中的经典统计量建立联系。

Result: QUEST的认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量满足UQ公理（包括分布扩散下的单调性和位置偏移不变性），在选择性预测基准上优于方差和微分熵等标准方法。

Conclusion: QUEST为不确定性量化提供了一种新的、公理化的框架，能够克服传统方法的局限，在安全关键应用中具有更好的可靠性和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for reliable decision-making in safety-critical applications in probabilistic machine learning. For regression problems, dominant scalar UQ approaches - notably, those based on proper scoring rules - measure uncertainty via pointwise predictive risk. This can lead to counterintuitive results when the target statistic is not the conditional expectation. We propose an alternative framework, in which uncertainty is characterised by the volume of the most probable subset of a distribution's support. QUEST (Quantifying Uncertainty via highest dEnSiTy regions) is a novel approach to UQ based on the concentration of Lebesgue measure at a distribution's peak(s), evaluated at one or more values of a robustness parameter $α$. We establish connections between our measures and classical statistics from information theory and economics. We show that, unlike popular alternatives based on proper scoring rules, QUEST measures of epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty satisfy a set of axioms adapted from the UQ literature, including monotonicity under distributional spread and invariance to location shifts. Selective prediction benchmarks confirm that QUEST performs favourably against standard measures such as variance and differential entropy.

中文摘要翻译: 不确定性量化（UQ）对于概率机器学习中安全关键应用的可靠决策至关重要。对于回归问题，主流的标量UQ方法——特别是基于适当评分规则的方法——通过逐点预测风险来衡量不确定性。当目标统计量不是条件期望时，这可能导致反直觉的结果。我们提出了一种替代框架，其中不确定性由分布支持集上最可能子集的体积来表征。QUEST（通过最高密度区域量化不确定性）是一种基于Lebesgue测度在分布峰值处集中程度的新型UQ方法，通过一个或多个鲁棒参数α的值进行评估。我们建立了我们的度量与信息论和经济学中经典统计量之间的联系。我们证明，与基于适当评分规则的流行替代方法不同，QUEST的认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量满足从UQ文献中改编的一组公理，包括分布扩散下的单调性和位置偏移不变性。选择性预测基准证实，QUEST在性能上优于方差和微分熵等标准度量。

</details>


### [143] [Unsupervised Causal Abstractions Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19594)
*Théo Saulus,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Dhanya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从低层测量数据中直接学习高层结构因果模型（SCM）的问题。作者利用低秩因果发现的假设，证明了低秩图生成的观测数据会诱导出形成因果抽象的低层潜变量，给出了这些潜变量的可识别性结果，并提出了一个实用的学习目标来获得高层SCM。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果抽象的应用主要遵循假设检验范式，即由专家提出候选高层模型，然后评估低层系统是否实现了该模型。本文研究与之互补的问题：直接从低层测量数据中学习高层模型，旨在自动发现高层因果结构而非依赖人工假设。

Method: (1) 证明低秩图生成的观测数据会诱导出构成因果抽象的潜变量；(2) 提供这些潜变量的可识别性分析；(3) 提出一个实用的优化目标来学习高层SCM。

Result: (1) 揭示了低秩图与因果抽象之间的理论联系；(2) 给出了潜变量的可识别性理论结果；(3) 提出了可直接用于学习高层SCM的实用目标函数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了低秩因果发现与因果抽象之间的桥梁，为从低层观测数据自动学习高层因果模型提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Original Abstract: Causal abstractions formalize when a high-level structural causal model (SCM) captures the interventional behavior of a lower-level SCM. Existing applications of this notion largely follow a hypothesis-testing paradigm: an expert proposes a candidate high-level model and then evaluates if the low-level system implements it. We study the complementary problem of learning a high-level model directly from low-level measurements. Our contributions leverage hypotheses from low-rank causal discovery, and can be summarized as follows: (1) we show that observations generated by a low-rank graph induce latents that form a causal abstraction, (2) we provide identifiability results about these latents, and (3) we propose a practical objective to learn this high-level SCM.

中文摘要翻译: 因果抽象形式化地描述了何时一个高层结构因果模型（SCM）能够捕捉低层SCM的干预行为。该概念的现有应用主要遵循假设检验范式：由专家提出一个候选高层模型，然后评估低层系统是否实现了该模型。本文研究了与之互补的问题——直接从低层测量数据中学习高层模型。我们的贡献利用了低秩因果发现的假设，可以概括如下：(1) 我们证明了由低秩图生成的观测数据会诱导出构成因果抽象的潜变量；(2) 我们提供了关于这些潜变量的可识别性结果；(3) 我们提出了一个用于学习该高层SCM的实用目标函数。

</details>


### [144] [IHBench: Evaluating Post-Interruption Recovery in Voice Agents with Structured Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19595)
*Ahmad Salimi,Wentao Ma,Yuzhi Tang,Dongming Shen,Mu Li,Alex Smola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IHBench是一个评估语音代理在结构化工作流中处理用户打断后恢复能力的基准，覆盖10个企业领域和6种打断类型。实验发现闭源模型在任务完成、长对话鲁棒性和模态一致性上显著优于开源模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准仅关注打断时机（如barge-in检测、端点检测、话轮转换），未评估打断后代理能否正确恢复工作流、处理用户插话、避免重复已传达内容，缺乏对打断后恢复能力的系统性评测。

Method: 构建IHBench基准，涵盖10个企业领域的状态机驱动工作流，在话语中间注入6种打断类型，为每次打断生成评估标准，从任务完成度和恢复质量两个维度评分。使用LLM作为评判者，并进行了人工评估验证。

Result: 评估了27个音频-语言模型配置。闭源模型在任务完成上胜率更高，随对话长度增加性能下降速度约为开源模型的1/3.3，且无音频-文本模态差距，而开源模型在这三方面均表现较差。人工研究验证了LLM评判者的可靠性，跨基准分析表明恢复质量是一个独立的能力维度。

Conclusion: 打断后恢复能力是一个独立于现有基准评测维度的重要能力维度，闭源模型在此方面显著优于开源模型，该方向值得进一步研究。

Original Abstract: Voice agents deployed in structured workflows (customer service, healthcare scheduling, account management) must handle frequent user interruptions while maintaining progress through multi-step procedures. Existing benchmarks for speech-capable models focus on the timing of interruptions: barge-in detection, endpointing, and turn-taking dynamics. They leave unmeasured what happens after the interruption: does the agent resume the workflow at the correct step? Does it address the user's interjection? Does it avoid re-delivering content the user already heard?
  We introduce IHBench (Interruption Handling Benchmark), a benchmark that evaluates post-interruption recovery in voice agents executing state-machine-driven workflows across 10 enterprise domains. Six interruption types are injected at controlled points mid-utterance, with per-interruption evaluation rubrics generated alongside the data. Each interruption is scored on two axes: task fulfillment and recovery quality.
  We evaluate 27 audio-language model configurations from OpenAI, Google, and the open-weight community. Models vary widely, and recovery quality depends strongly on the interruption type. Across our experiments, closed-weight models are consistently more robust to interruptions than open-weight ones: they win far more often on task fulfillment, degrade roughly 3.3x more slowly as conversations grow longer, and show no audio-versus-text modality gap, whereas the open-weight models lose ground on all three. A human study validates the LLM judge against human annotators, and a cross-benchmark analysis against AudioMultiChallenge indicates that recovery quality is a largely distinct capability axis.

中文摘要翻译: 部署在结构化工作流（客户服务、医疗调度、账户管理）中的语音代理必须处理频繁的用户打断，同时保持多步骤流程的推进。现有的语音能力模型基准聚焦于打断时机：即入检测、端点检测和话轮转换动态。它们没有测量打断之后发生的情况：代理是否会恢复到工作流中的正确步骤？它是否处理了用户的插话？它是否避免了重复用户已经听过的内容？我们提出了IHBench（打断处理基准），这是一个评估语音代理在10个企业领域中执行状态机驱动工作流时的打断后恢复能力的基准。六种打断类型在话语中段的受控点注入，并随数据生成每次打断的评估标准。每次打断在两个维度上评分：任务完成度和恢复质量。我们评估了来自OpenAI、Google和开源社区的27个音频-语言模型配置。模型表现差异显著，恢复质量强烈依赖于打断类型。在我们的实验中，闭源模型对打断的鲁棒性始终优于开源模型：它们在任务完成度上胜率更高，随着对话变长性能下降速度约慢3.3倍，并且没有音频-文本模态差距，而开源模型在这三方面均表现不佳。一项人工研究验证了LLM评判者与人工标注者的一致性，与AudioMultiChallenge的跨基准分析表明，恢复质量在很大程度上是一个独立的能力轴。

</details>


### [145] [Comparing Linear Probes with Mahalanobis Cosine Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19603)
*Zhuofan Josh Ying,Peter Hase,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验两方面证明了，线性探针在测试集上的马氏余弦相似度（MCS）与其OOD AUROC之间存在线性关系，其根源在于两者均为探针信噪比（SNR）的sigmoid型函数。该发现为比较线性探针提供了比欧氏余弦相似度更具理论依据且更有效的替代方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Ying等人（2026）发现探针的MCS与OOD AUROC之间存在近乎完美的线性关系（R²=0.98），本文旨在将此经验发现推广到不同模型、层和概念域，并从理论上证明这一现象的普适性，同时解释其何时失效。

Method: 理论推导：假设类别平衡且投影分布为高斯分布，证明了OOD AUROC和MCS均为探针在测试数据上信噪比（SNR）的sigmoid型函数，从而两者呈线性关系；并通过跨模型、跨层、跨概念域的实验进行验证。

Result: 1. 在平衡高斯假设下，证明了OOD AUROC与MCS因同为SNR的sigmoid函数而呈线性关系；2. 理论预测了线性关系失效的条件，并通过实验验证；3. MCS相比欧氏余弦相似度具有理论依据且实验效果更优。

Conclusion: 马氏余弦相似度（MCS）是理论上可解释、实践中更有效的线性探针比较指标，可替代传统的欧氏余弦相似度。

Original Abstract: Linear probes are widely used in interpretability research and often compared by cosine similarity. The Mahalanobis cosine similarity (MCS) between two directions, which reweights the inner product by test data covariance, is a natural task-aware refinement. Ying et al. (2026) report that a probe's MCS to a reference probe trained on the out-of-distribution (OOD) data near-perfectly linearly predicts the probe's OOD AUROC (R^2 = 0.98). Here, we extend this empirical finding across models, layers, and concept domains, and prove this general phenomenon in closed form: For balanced classes whose projections are Gaussian, OOD AUROC and MCS to the reference probe are linear because both are sigmoid-shaped functions of the probe's signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) on the test data. The theory also predicts when this linearity fails, which we verify empirically. MCS offers a theoretically grounded and empirically effective alternative to Euclidean cosine similarity for comparing linear probes.

中文摘要翻译: 线性探针在可解释性研究中被广泛使用，并且通常通过余弦相似度进行比较。两个方向之间的马氏余弦相似度（MCS）通过测试数据协方差对内积进行重新加权，是一种自然的任务感知精炼。Ying等人（2026）报告称，探针与在分布外（OOD）数据上训练的参考探针之间的MCS能够近乎完美地线性预测该探针的OOD AUROC（R²=0.98）。本文将此经验发现推广到不同模型、层和概念域，并以闭式形式证明了这一普遍现象：对于投影分布为高斯分布的平衡类别，OOD AUROC与参考探针的MCS呈线性关系，因为两者都是探针在测试数据上信噪比（SNR）的sigmoid型函数。该理论还预测了这种线性关系何时失效，我们通过实验对此进行了验证。马氏余弦相似度为比较线性探针提供了一种具有理论依据且实际有效的替代方案，优于欧氏余弦相似度。

</details>


### [146] [Latent Confounded Causal Discovery via Lie Bracket Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19610)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于Kan-Do-Calculus的范畴论和几何推论，提出两种因果发现算法（BRIDGE和SKFM），通过干预诱导流的几何结构推断含潜在混淆变量的因果模型，大幅压缩了搜索空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在存在潜在混淆变量时面临超指数级搜索空间和可识别性难题，本文希望利用KDC的范畴论与信息几何框架，将潜在结构推断转化为干预流场几何可积性检验问题，从而绕过传统组合搜索的限制。

Method: 1) BRIDGE：利用观测与干预分布间的Radon-Nikodym导数构造局部因果向量场，通过Lie括号闭包性失败计算Frobenius残差作为潜在混淆标识，筛选候选边后交给下游评分/可微发现方法；2) SKFM：学习摊销干预场并通过谱方法分解潜在曲率，直接暴露Lie空间方向作为BRIDGE的补充端点。

Result: 实验表明两种算法均能发现含潜在混淆变量的因果模型，并将可能的DAG超指数空间压缩多个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文开创了一种新的因果发现范式：直接从干预诱导流的几何结构中推断潜在结构，而非对离散图空间进行暴力搜索。

Original Abstract: Recent work on Kan-Do-Calculus (KDC) has established that the boundary between passive observation and active intervention in causal inference is a category-theoretic bi-adjunction, with interventions modeled by left Kan extensions and conditioning by right Kan extensions. This paper introduces two causal discovery algorithms under latent confounding, building on the information-geometric and categorical consequences of KDC.
