中文摘要翻译: 安全性是在机器人技术和自动驾驶等现实领域部署强化学习（RL）智能体的核心关切。尽管基准测试在RL的进步中发挥了关键作用，但现有的具备高保真3D物理模拟的安全基准计算速度缓慢，限制了大规模实验和快速原型开发。为填补这一空白，我们提出CRAX（基于JAX加速的约束强化学习基准）。该基准构建于具有逼真3D动力学的MuJoCo XLA（MJX）物理引擎之上，利用向量化操作和硬件加速，相较于基于CPU的同类安全基准实现了高达约100倍的加速。该基准包含6个环境套件和3个智能体特定任务，每个任务涵盖三个难度等级。对6种主流安全RL方法的评估表明，没有任何一种方法在所有任务中占据主导地位，并揭示了性能与安全性之间的权衡关系。我们还发现，跨难度级别的课程学习以及安全迁移训练能够比直接在更困难设置下训练取得更好的性能表现。

</details>


### [208] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedMGS，一种面向多模态联邦图学习中模态不平衡问题的隐式语义合成方法，通过图感知的潜在语义表示恢复缺失模态信息，在四个任务上取得了最高17.41%的性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MM-FGL方法面临两种粒度的模态不平衡挑战：客户端级别（部分客户端缺失整个模态）和节点级别（单个节点缺失视觉或文本属性）。现有研究主要针对非图场景或集中式场景，难以直接适配联邦图学习。

Method: 提出FedMGS框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；(2) 原型引导的潜在语义合成器，为缺失模态建立跨客户端的语义锚点；(3) 可靠性校准的语义融合机制，在预测读出前调控恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上的大量实验表明，FedMGS持续优于竞争基线，性能提升最高达17.41%，同时实现了最佳的效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 将模态不平衡的MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成问题，在表示空间中直接恢复缺失的模态语义，能最大程度与原始数据语义分布对齐，缓解缺失模态带来的高方差问题。

Original Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态联邦图学习（MM-FGL）提供了一种自然的协作训练范式，但其实际部署面临两种粒度的模态不平衡挑战。客户端级不平衡出现在某些客户端完全缺失整个模态时，而节点级不平衡则出现在个别节点缺失视觉或文本属性的情况下。尽管已有若干相关研究，我们的调查表明它们主要针对非图场景或集中式场景，难以直接适配。为应对这些挑战，我们将模态不平衡的MM-FGL形式化为一个隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成问题。该范式直接在表示空间中恢复缺失的模态语义，从而最大程度地与原始数据的语义分布对齐，缓解缺失模态引入的高方差问题。为此，我们提出FedMGS（联邦模态感知图合成方法），该方法集成了三个核心组件：可用性感知图编码器防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；原型引导的潜在语义合成器为缺失模态建立跨客户端的语义锚点；可靠性校准的语义融合机制在预测读出前调控恢复的潜在表示的影响。在四个任务上的大量实验表明，FedMGS持续优于竞争基线，性能提升最高达17.41%，同时实现了最佳的效率-性能权衡。

</details>


### [209] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全无需人工标注数据的意图分类合成对话生成框架，利用主题和风格属性提升数据多样性，并设计了两种后置风格化模型（Univ和Exam），实验表明该方法能达到使用人工标注数据93.3%的性能，且发现风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在快节奏的工业环境中，人工标注的种子数据往往难以获取，而传统的高质量意图分类合成数据生成依赖此类标注数据，因此需要一种完全无需人工标注数据的合成对话生成方法。

Method: 提出一个完全基于意图定义的合成对话生成框架，利用两种不同类型的主题和风格属性提升数据多样性；提出两种后置风格化模型Univ和Exam，将LLM生成的合成话语转换为更多样化、更接近人类语言风格；采用LLM作为评判器的过滤流程提升数据质量。

Result: 在工业数据集和公开数据集上，该方法达到了使用人工标注训练数据性能的93.3%；实验发现风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键，可防止模型学习虚假的风格相关性；在生成过程中融入风格属性比事后风格自适应更有效。

Conclusion: 提出的零人工标注合成对话生成框架能有效替代人工标注数据，风格多样性是决定合成数据效用的关键因素，且生成阶段融入风格属性优于事后调整。

Original Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 生成用于意图分类的高效用合成数据通常需要人工标注的种子数据，这在快节奏的工业环境中往往难以获取。本文提出了一个完全无需人工标注数据、仅依赖意图定义的合成对话生成框架。该框架利用两种不同类型的主题和风格属性来提升数据多样性。此外，我们提出了两种新颖的后置风格化模型Univ和Exam，将LLM生成的合成话语转换为更加多样化、更接近人类语言风格。为了提升数据质量，我们采用了LLM作为评判器的过滤流程。在工业数据集和公开数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法能达到使用人工标注训练数据性能的93.3%。关键在于，研究发现风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更为关键，因为它能防止模型学习到虚假的风格相关性。此外，研究还表明在生成过程中融入风格属性比事后风格自适应更为有效。

</details>


### [210] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入离散潜在动力学模型，将直接优势估计（DAE）扩展至部分可观测环境，并降低了其计算复杂度，在Arcade学习环境中验证了其高样本效率和可扩展性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DAE方法在完全可观测环境中表现优异，但存在两个主要局限：(1) 依赖完整环境可观测性，限制了在现实部分可观测场景中的应用；(2) 需要建模状态转移概率，对高维观测数据计算开销巨大。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: (1) 将DAE的理论框架扩展至部分可观测领域，仅需最小改动；(2) 引入离散潜在动力学模型，高效近似转移概率，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade学习环境（ALE）上的实验表明，DAE能够随函数逼近器容量有效扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文成功将DAE推广到部分可观测场景并降低了计算开销，验证了该方法在大规模深度强化学习中的实用性和高效性。

Original Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 直接优势估计（DAE）已被证明能提升深度强化学习算法的样本效率。然而，其对完整环境可观测性的依赖限制了其在现实场景中的适用性，且建模转移概率的要求对高维观测数据带来了巨大的计算开销。在本工作中，我们解决了这两个局限性。首先，我们将DAE的理论框架扩展到部分可观测领域，仅需最小的修改。其次，我们通过引入离散潜在动力学模型来高效近似转移概率，从而降低了计算复杂度。我们在Arcade学习环境上评估了该方法，发现DAE能随函数逼近器容量有效扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

</details>


### [211] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级、模型无关的防御框架，通过在输入样本中添加基于统计分布的伪特征填充，增加输入维度，使对抗攻击在计算上不可行，从而增强DNN在CPS中抵御FDIA的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CPS基础设施使DNN容易受到攻击者利用以规避检测，而传统防御机制难以应对FDIA，因此需要一种轻量级且高效的防御框架。

Method: 在DNN输入层之前增加一个额外的填充层，使用基于输入统计分布的伪特征值对输入样本进行填充，以随机化和数据感知的方式增加输入维度，使对抗扰动不可迁移且填充结构不可预测。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点、30节点、118节点和300节点电力系统上的状态估计实验中，该填充策略显著提升了模型鲁棒性，对性能影响极小，并能有效缓解传统防御无法拦截的攻击。

Conclusion: 该方法轻量级、模型无关、无需修改核心架构，在真实CPS场景中具有高度可部署性，可有效增强DNN对FDIA的防御能力。

Original Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

中文摘要翻译: 深度神经网络（DNN）在各种任务中取得了显著的准确率，包括在信息物理系统（CPS）中用于检测关键操作期间的虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）。然而，CPS的独特基础设施使得DNN易受攻击者利用以规避检测。此外，CPS的独特性质给传统的FDIA防御机制带来了挑战。本文提出了一种创新的防御框架，通过引入一个额外的输入层，该层使用基于输入统计分布推导出的伪特征值对输入样本进行填充，从而增强DNN抵御此类攻击的能力。这种填充以随机化和数据感知的方式增加了输入维度，使得精心设计的扰动的不可迁移性以及填充结构的不可预测性导致对抗攻击在计算上变得不可行。我们的方法轻量级、模型无关，无需修改核心架构，因此在真实世界的CPS环境中具有高度可部署性。我们使用IEEE 14节点、30节点、118节点和300节点系统，在关键电网应用（如状态估计）上评估了该框架。对抗性设置下的实验表明，我们的填充策略显著提升了模型的鲁棒性，且对性能影响极小，并能有效缓解那些会绕过传统防御的攻击。

</details>


### [212] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了扩散模型中时间步嵌入的必要性，理论证明在某些条件下无显式时间条件也能达到全局最优，实验表明移除时间嵌入的模型在结构保真度和FID等指标上甚至超越有条件的模型，揭示了隐式噪声尺度推理的可能性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型严重依赖显式的时间步嵌入来调节不同噪声尺度下的去噪过程，本文旨在探究这些时间信号是否真正必要，并挑战长期以来的时间条件化范式。

Method: 通过理论框架证明在特定条件下扩散训练目标的全局最小值可以在没有显式时间步条件的情况下实现；在U-Net和扩散Transformer架构上移除时间步嵌入进行消融实验；在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上评估时间无关模型。

Result: 时间无关模型能够保持高结构保真度，在FID、精确率和召回率等竞争性指标上甚至超越有条件的对应模型；架构可以在特定假设下从受损输入中隐式推断噪声尺度。

Conclusion: 显式时间条件化是冗余的，挑战了长期以来的时间条件化范式，为更高效且聚焦于结构的生成架构铺平了道路。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型严重依赖显式的时间步嵌入来调节不同噪声尺度下的去噪过程。在这项工作中，我们通过分析时间信号对U-Net和扩散Transformer架构的影响，挑战了这些时间信号的必要性。除了经验证据之外，我们提供了一个理论框架，证明在特定条件下，扩散训练目标的全局最小值可以在没有显式时间步条件的情况下实现。我们的发现揭示了当时间步嵌入被完全移除时令人惊讶的鲁棒性。在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上的大量消融研究表明，这些时间无关模型能够保持高结构保真度，甚至在FID、精确率和召回率等竞争性指标上超越其有条件版本。我们的分析表明，这些架构在特定假设下可以从受损输入中隐式推断噪声尺度，使得显式时间条件化变得冗余。这项研究挑战了长期以来的时间条件化范式，并为更高效且聚焦于结构的生成架构铺平了道路。

</details>


### [213] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepGaLA，一种用于微分方程求解器的不确定性量化神经网络代理模型，可在贝叶斯逆问题中实现高效且可靠的后验推断，尤其在训练数据有限和高维参数空间中表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统贝叶斯逆问题数值方法计算成本高，尤其是在复杂前向模型和高维参数空间中似然评估代价高昂，迫切需要一种既能提供不确定性感知预测又能保持计算效率的代理模型。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA，一种神经网络代理模型替代微分方程求解器，提供不确定性感知预测；并采用延迟接受马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（delayed-acceptance MCMC）作为诊断工具，评估代理诱导后验近似的保真度。

Result: 数值实验表明，DeepGaLA的前向模型逼近精度与高斯过程代理相当，但在参数维度增长时效率更高；此外，该方法能够融入微分方程约束，包括非线性情形。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经网络代理模型能够为复杂系统中的逆问题实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

Original Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

中文摘要翻译: 微分方程的反问题广泛存在于科学和工程领域，其目标是从含噪或不完整的观测数据中推断未知模型参数。传统的数值方法在处理这类问题时通常计算成本高昂，特别是在贝叶斯框架下，对于复杂的前向模型和高维参数空间，似然评估变得极其昂贵。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了DeepGaLA——一种用于微分方程求解器的神经网络代理模型，该模型能够提供不确定性感知的预测，在训练数据有限时减少过度自信的推断。为在实践中评估代理模型诱导的后验近似的保真度，我们证明，短程运行的延迟接受马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法可以作为有效的诊断工具。在一系列数值实验中，DeepGaLA的前向模型逼近精度与已建立的高斯过程代理模型相当，同时在参数维度增长时能更好地保持计算效率。此外，该方法能够融入微分方程约束，包括非线性情形。总体而言，这些结果表明，不确定性量化的神经代理模型能够为复杂系统中的逆问题实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

</details>


### [214] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可控的合成数据玩具框架，通过可观测的机制研究持续学习中的遗忘现象，利用生成器-分离器流水线定义真实潜在特征，引入表示强度和叠加（特征间的方向重叠）等可测量量，并借助SINDy拟合保留动态。实验表明：叠加随时间增加但在任务边界出现瞬降；高特征稀疏性导致更多叠加但不必然引发遗忘；任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长。这些结果修正了“叠加导致遗忘”的简单直觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 持续学习系统经常遗忘先前获得的知识，但由于真实数据集纠缠了许多因素，导致遗忘的驱动机制在实践中难以被隔离和研究。作者希望构建一个可控的玩具世界框架，使这些机制变得可观察和可测试。

Method: (1) 构建合成生成器-分离器流水线，定义真实潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠性的任务；(2) 引入表示强度和叠加（特征间的方向重叠）的可测量量；(3) 通过SINDy（稀疏动力学识别）拟合保留度、叠加和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系；(4) 基于有效秩进行任务级分析，表征表示能力在任务间的分配情况。

Result: (1) 叠加随时间增加，但在任务边界出现瞬时下降，表明存在边界特异性干扰而非稳态漂移；(2) 更高的特征稀疏性导致更多叠加，但并不必然引发遗忘——当表示强度足够强时，即使存在重叠也能减少遗忘；(3) 任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明稀疏条件下更广泛的能力使用。

Conclusion: 研究结果修正了“更多叠加导致更多遗忘”的常见直觉，表明重叠与表示强度和能力分配之间存在交互作用。该玩具分析为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

Original Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

中文摘要翻译: 持续学习（CL）系统常常遗忘先前获得的知识，但由于真实数据集纠缠了许多因素，遗忘的驱动机制在实践中难以被隔离。我们提出了一个可控的玩具世界框架，使这些机制变得可观察和可测试。通过使用合成生成器-分离器流水线，我们定义了真实潜在特征，构建了具有可调稀疏性和重叠性的任务，并引入了表示强度和叠加（特征间的方向重叠）的可测量量。然后，我们通过拟合保留度、叠加和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系（通过SINDy）来研究保留动态——表示强度的时间变化。基于有效秩的互补任务级分析表征了表示能力在任务间的分配情况。我们的控制实验得出三个要点：（1）叠加随时间增加，但在任务边界出现瞬时下降，表明存在边界特异性干扰而非稳态漂移。（2）更高的特征稀疏性导致更多叠加，但并不必然引发遗忘；当表示保持较强时，即使存在重叠也能减少遗忘。（3）任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明在稀疏条件下更广泛的能力使用。这些结果共同修正了“更多叠加导致更多遗忘”的常见直觉，表明重叠与表示强度和能力分配之间存在交互作用。我们的玩具分析为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

</details>


### [215] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化算法的两阶段超参数搜索方法，用于提升物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的求解精度和鲁棒性，在有限计算资源下显著优于标准训练方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: PINNs在求解PDE时由于物理信息损失函数的高非凸性和多目标结构，面临收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感等问题。传统局部或基于梯度的超参数搜索容易陷入次优区域，需要更鲁棒的全局搜索机制。

Method: 提出两阶段进化算法方法：第一阶段使用低保真度训练（截断epoch）快速筛选候选超参数配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外循环问题；第二阶段仅对最有希望的候选进行完整的标准梯度优化训练，以精细化求解结果。

Result: 在对流方程、Klein-Gordon方程和Helmholtz方程三个经典问题上的实验表明，所提方法在有限计算资源下一致优于标准训练方法，显著降低了平均误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法结合两阶段探索-利用策略能够有效提升PINNs的训练鲁棒性和求解精度，为物理信息神经网络的超参数优化提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

中文摘要翻译: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）通过将物理定律嵌入神经网络训练中来求解偏微分方程（PDEs）。然而，由于物理信息损失函数的高度非凸性和多目标结构，其性能常受到收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感等问题的影响。在这种情景下，外循环超参数搜索是一个在异质参数空间上的带噪黑箱优化问题，经典的局部或基于梯度的策略容易陷入次优区域。进化算法凭借其基于种群的探索能力以及处理混合、不可微搜索空间的特性，为发现更优配置提供了更鲁棒的机制。本文提出并研究了一种基于进化算法的两阶段方法，该方法结合了PINNs训练中的探索与利用部分，以在固定计算预算下提升求解精度和鲁棒性。第一阶段，我们进行低保真度训练（使用截断epoch）快速筛选候选配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外循环问题；第二阶段，仅对最有希望的候选使用标准梯度优化器进行完整训练，以精细化求解结果。在对流方程、Klein-Gordon方程和Helmholtz方程三个经典问题上的评估表明，我们的方法一致优于标准训练方法，并在有限计算资源内实现了显著更低的平均误差。

</details>


### [216] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在偏差缓解框架中引入覆盖约束的方法，通过整数线性规划优化缓解策略，在保证交叉性子群充分代表性与数据效率之间取得平衡，并刻画了公平性的代价。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器学习模型在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）的交叉点上可能表现出歧视性结果或性能下降，这源于缺乏原则性的偏差量化指标以及交叉性子群在训练数据中的代表性不足。

Method: 扩展了一个偏差缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以确保各群体（包括交叉性子群）的充分代表性；将偏差缓解形式化为整数线性规划问题，优化所有缓解策略；通过允许小的近似偏差来换取更高的数据效率，并刻画公平性的代价（最小数据修改成本与公平容忍度的函数关系）。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该框架在多个分类器上保持了预测准确性；覆盖约束虽源于统计考量，但对保持下游ML性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在偏差缓解与数据修改成本之间做出明智的权衡，既满足法律合规中特定的公平性阈值要求，也为数据治理中数据购买等决策提供了实践指导。

Original Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习模型已被证明在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）的交叉点上会表现出歧视性结果或性能下降。这部分源于两个相互关联的挑战：缺乏用于量化（潜在交叉性）偏差的原则性度量，以及训练数据中交叉性子群的代表性不足。我们将一个最近的偏差缓解框架进行扩展，引入覆盖约束，以确保各群体（包括交叉性子群）具有充分的代表性。由于为所有群体实现完全零偏差可能数据效率不高（即可能需要大量数据），我们的解决方案用偏差上的小近似误差换取更高的数据效率，同时满足覆盖约束。我们还将偏差缓解形式化为一个整数线性规划问题，对所有缓解策略进行优化，并刻画了公平性的代价——最小数据修改成本——作为公平容忍度的函数。这对于法律合规（法规可能规定特定的公平性阈值）和数据治理（使从业者能够在偏差减少与数据修改（特别是数据购买）成本之间做出明智的权衡）都至关重要。我们在公开可用的数据集上评估了我们的技术，结果表明通过我们的框架进行偏差缓解能在多个分类器上保持预测准确性，并且覆盖约束虽源于统计考量，但对于保持下游机器学习性能至关重要。

</details>


### [217] [The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20547)
*Przemyslaw Musialski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出李代数注意力机制，将token定义为矩阵李群元素（纯变换，无特征载荷），利用相对位姿的对数范数作为注意力分数，无需表示论或学习核，适用于仿射全帧群，在SE(2)、SO(3)、Aff(2)上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注意力机制中token通常携带特征向量，依赖不可约表示或满射指数方法，无法处理包含尺度和剪切的非紧致非阿贝尔仿射群；且注意力核通常需要学习，缺乏闭式几何解释。

Method: 将注意力token定义为矩阵李群元素（纯变换），注意力分数采用相对位姿的对数范数的负平方：$s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$，基于块加权Frobenius内积。该方法无需不可约表示、球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan乘积或学习核，自然满足对角G-作用等变性和上循环条件。

Result: 在SE(2)、SO(3)、Aff(2)上的序列补全实验中，闭式分数匹配了学习的MLP核并在SE(2)上超越，参数减少50-80倍；向量token基线方法等变性误差达5-12个数量级。

Conclusion: 李代数注意力机制为群元素token提供了简洁、闭式、无需表示论的工具，能处理现有方法无法覆盖的仿射全帧群，且参数效率高、等变性优异。

Original Abstract: We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.

中文摘要翻译: 我们将注意力token置于群上：一个token是矩阵李群$G$的一个元素$g_i$——一个纯粹的变换，没有特征载荷，也没有承载它的外部作用$ρ(g)$。据我们所知，这是第一个token为裸矩阵李群元素的注意力构建：其分数是相对位姿的闭式代数范数而非学习核，并且它能达到每个基于不可约表示或满射指数的方法都必须排除的仿射全帧群。我们称之为李代数注意力。一旦token是群元素，其余部分便无需通常的表示论机制。一对元素的相对几何是正则的，即$g_i^{-1} g_j$，因此成对不变量$w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$是内在的而非设计的；对角$G$-作用下的等变是重言式的，上循环条件自动成立。注意力分数是负的代数范数平方，$s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$：在块加权Frobenius内积下的正则邻近核，无需不可约表示、球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan乘积或学习核。该构建适用于任意矩阵李群，在选定的包含相对位姿的对数图表上，包括非紧致非阿贝尔的带尺度和剪切的仿射群——这些是任何向量token注意力方法都无法达到的：无论是不可约表示传统方法还是满射指数方法。在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的三个序列补全实验证实了这一点：闭式分数在相同不变量上匹配了学习的MLP核并在SE(2)上超越，减少了50到80倍的分数参数，而向量token基线的等变性误差达5到12个数量级。

</details>


### [218] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架，通过智能体将PDE理论、约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的符号程序，结合演化搜索与梯度优化自动发现PDE的解析形式或近似解，在多个非线性问题上得到了先前未知的封闭形式描述。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数学家理解PDE解依赖数学结构而非数值表格，但传统分析需逐个手工推导，数值模拟和神经网络都无法直接产出这些结构，因此需要一种能自动发现PDE解析表示的框架。

Method: ASYS框架包含一个智能体，将PDE理论、问题约束和累积搜索经验转化为可测试的可微分符号程序；通过演化搜索优化数学形式，同时利用梯度优化拟合连续参数，实现归纳偏置的自动化注入而非盲目符号回归。

Result: 在涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦的五类问题上，ASYS生成了可解释表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，这些在先前没有封闭形式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式可能性，超越了手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似。

Original Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

中文摘要翻译: 数学家通过数学结构而非计算数值表格来理解偏微分方程的解。历史上，这依赖于数学分析，需要针对每个问题单独手工推导。数值模拟和神经网络都无法直接产生这些结构。我们提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)，一个先验引导的框架，其中智能体将PDE理论、公开问题约束以及累积的搜索经验转化为可测试的可微分符号程序。数学形式在演化搜索下精炼，而其连续参数通过基于梯度的优化来拟合。这使得搜索成为一种归纳偏置注入的自动化形式，而非盲目的符号回归。对于已知解析形式的问题，ASYS自然地恢复了这些形式；对于其他问题，ASYS构建了解析近似，可以引导数学家进行进一步分析。在我们的实验中，涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦的五类问题，ASYS产生了可解释的表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，这些在以往没有任何封闭形式描述的场景中取得。ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式的可能性，超越了手工解析解、基于网格的数值解和神经网络近似。

</details>


### [219] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对深度学习中对“平坦极小值泛化更好”的直觉缺乏重参数化不变性的理论缺陷，基于Fisher信息矩阵的黎曼几何定义了具有不变性的黎曼尖锐度（Riemannian sharpness），从理论上证明了SGD的隐式偏好与平坦极小值的泛化优势，并通过实验验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准欧氏平坦性度量（如Hessian矩阵的迹或最大特征值）在保函数重参数化下不具有不变性，Dinh等人的工作对此提出了质疑。本文旨在解决这一理论缺陷，建立一套具有重参数化不变性的平坦性理论基础。

Method: 基于Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何，定义黎曼尖锐度（Riemannian sharpness）并证明其在光滑保函数重参数化下的不变性；将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为与FIM成比例的协方差结构，推导随机微分方程的平稳分布，证明概率质量指数级集中在黎曼平坦极小值处；通过PAC-Bayes泛化界将几何偏好与测试性能联系起来。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验证实，黎曼尖锐度能够可靠地追踪泛化性能，而欧氏尖锐度则不能；其缩放行为与理论预测的η/B（学习率/批次大小）一致。

Conclusion: 本文提供了严格且具有重参数化不变性的理论框架，解释了为何平坦极小值能够泛化，弥合了直觉与理论之间的鸿沟。

Original Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习领域存在一个广泛认同的直觉：随机梯度下降（SGD）隐式偏好平坦极小值，且平坦极小值具有更好的泛化性能。然而，标准的欧氏平坦性度量——例如损失Hessian矩阵的迹或最大特征值——在保持网络函数不变的重参数化下并不具有不变性，这削弱了这一叙事的理论基础。在本研究中，我们通过将平坦性建立在由Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何之上，解决了这一问题。我们在数学上定义了黎曼尖锐度（Riemannian sharpness），并证明其在光滑、保函数重参数化下具有不变性，这直接回应了Dinh等人在《Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets》一文中提出的批评。我们指出，这种不变性是真实FIM的一个性质；实践中使用的对角经验估计器（以及下文所有实验中使用的）仅近似地继承不变性，而要实现任意重参数化下的精确不变性则需要诸如K-FAC之类的结构化估计器。我们将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为具有与FIM成比例的协方差结构，推导了由此产生的随机微分方程的平稳分布，进而证明概率质量指数级地集中在黎曼平坦极小值处。一个由SR显式控制的PAC-Bayes泛化界将这一几何偏好与测试性能正式联系起来。我们在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验证实，SR能够以欧氏尖锐度所不具备的方式可靠地追踪泛化性能，且其随η/B的缩放行为与理论预测一致。综合这些结果，本文为平坦极小值为何能够泛化提供了严格且具有重参数化不变性的解释。

</details>


### [220] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对上下文密集型智能体（agent）工作负载的KV缓存压缩问题，提出了一种结合4比特量化、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、非对称K/V处理及块级缩放等设计的稳健方案，并基于AMD GPU实现了UltraQuant优化路径，在键值缓存压力大的场景下显著降低了首令牌延迟并提高了输出吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在上下文密集型智能体工作负载中，长前缀被多个短轮次复用，并发性决定GPU利用率，导致KV缓存面临巨大压力。现有FP8 KV缓存方案在高并发、长上下文的多次轮交互场景下，缓存压力成为瓶颈，需要更极致的4比特量化压缩方案以平衡任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量。

Method: 采用TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量锚点，vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署锚点。具体设计包括：(1) 非对称K/V处理；(2) Walsh-Hadamard旋转；(3) 移除QJL（量子化跳跃查找）；(4) 块级缩放变体。在AMD GPU上还实现了优化解码注意力核以及UltraQuant——一种FP4近似路径，结合FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放和CDNA4原生缩放MFMA支持。

Result: 在长上下文、多轮智能体工作负载下，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次中，P50首令牌延迟降低3.47倍（全轮次平均降低2.3倍），输出吞吐量相比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4比特KV缓存压缩结合精心设计的量化策略（非对称K/V、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、块级缩放）和硬件适配优化（UltraQuant），能够在保持任务质量的前提下，显著提升智能体工作负载的推理效率，为高并发长上下文场景提供了可行的部署方案。

Original Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 上下文密集型智能体（agent）工作负载给键值（KV）缓存带来了异常压力：长前缀在多个短轮次中被复用，而并发性决定了服务系统能否保持GPU利用率。我们针对此场景研究了4比特KV缓存压缩，采用TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量锚点，vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署锚点。本文报告三项贡献。第一，我们将4比特KV缓存置于多轮智能体工作负载的框架中，其中任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量必须被联合衡量。第二，我们描述了使4比特路径具有鲁棒性的实际设计选择，包括非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除以及块级缩放变体。第三，我们在AMD GPU上提出了服务优化方案，包括优化的解码注意力核以及UltraQuant——一种FP4近似路径，该路径使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放以及CDNA4上的原生缩放MFMA支持。在长上下文、多轮智能体工作负载上，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次中，P50首令牌延迟降低3.47倍（全轮次平均降低2.3倍），输出吞吐量相比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

</details>


### [221] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对批量式痕迹蒸馏中跨批次反馈矛盾的问题，提出边缘优势累积（MAA）方法，通过构建可跨批次比较的差分信号、EMA累积操作级证据和语义身份合并确保可追溯性，作为后处理架构在16个设置中14个取得最佳结果，并节省约75%优化阶段token消耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有批量式痕迹蒸馏方法缺乏跨批次、操作级别的证据累积机制，无法区分稳定有效的操作与偶然命中，因为同一内存操作在不同批次中可能收到矛盾的反馈信号。

Method: 提出边缘优势累积（MAA）方法：1）构建差分信号使其跨批次可比；2）通过指数移动平均（EMA）为每个操作累积带符号的证据；3）通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。该方法为后处理架构。

Result: 在4个基准和4个目标模型的16个设置中，MAA在14个设置上取得最佳结果，持续优于现有批量级蒸馏基线，在多数设置中匹配或超越在线方法，同时减少约75%优化阶段的token消耗。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了跨批次证据累积问题，显著提升了批量式痕迹蒸馏的性能和效率。

Original Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

中文摘要翻译: 在批量式痕迹蒸馏中，同一内存操作在不同批次中可能收到相互矛盾的反馈。现有方法缺乏跨批次、操作级别的证据累积机制，因此无法区分稳定有效的操作与偶然命中。本文将这一需求形式化为两个结构条件——可对齐性和可比性，并提出了边缘优势累积（MAA）方法。MAA构建差分信号使其跨批次可比，通过指数移动平均为每个操作累积带符号的证据，并通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。作为一种后处理架构，MAA在4个基准和4个目标模型的16个设置中，在14个设置上取得了最佳结果，持续优于现有的批量级蒸馏基线，并在大多数设置中匹配或超越了在线替代方法，同时将优化阶段的token消耗减少了约75%。

</details>


### [222] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contagion Networks框架，量化LLM作为评估者时其评估偏差在智能体网络中的传播。实验表明偏差可在同模型智能体间传播（γ∈[0.157,0.352]），且增大评估委员会规模（k=1→k=3）可减少72.4%的有效传染。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体系统中作为评估者时，其系统性评估偏差会通过智能体网络传播，但缺乏形式化的方法来度量这种偏差传播的机制和程度。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks形式化框架，通过跨智能体传染矩阵Γ_N量化偏差传播；在3智能体实验中采用DeepSeek-chat模型，设置三种评估偏差画像（结构化、平衡、基于证据），计算Γ_3矩阵及谱半径ρ(Γ_N)，并测试不同评估委员会规模（k=1,2,3）的影响。

Result: 1) 评估偏差在同模型智能体间一致传播（γ∈[0.157,0.352]）；2) 识别出三种传播机制（由谱半径ρ(Γ_N)决定）；3) 同模型智能体的传染系数比跨模型系数弱3-5倍，处于抑制机制；4) 评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。

Conclusion: 评估偏差在多智能体系统中会传播，但通过增加评估委员会规模可以有效缓解，为构建更鲁棒的LLM评估系统提供了可行的缓解策略。

Original Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

中文摘要翻译: 当大语言模型在多智能体系统中担任评估者时，其系统性评估偏差会通过智能体网络传播。本文提出传染网络（Contagion Networks），一个用于衡量评估者偏差如何在交互的LLM智能体间传播的形式化框架。在使用DeepSeek-chat模型、三种不同评估偏差画像（结构化、平衡、基于证据）的受控3智能体实验中，我们测量了跨智能体传染矩阵Γ_3，发现评估偏差在智能体间一致传播（γ∈[0.157,0.352]），即使底层模型相同。我们识别出由谱半径ρ(Γ_N)控制的三种传播机制，并证明同质模型智能体产生的传染系数比先前工作中观察到的跨模型系数弱3-5倍（MM-EPC：γ≈0.85-1.3），处于抑制机制。我们证明将评估委员会规模从k=1增加到k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染，提供了一种可行的缓解策略。我们开源了Contagion Network实验框架。

</details>


### [223] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashRT 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种面向低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务的图绑定检查点与恢复机制，实现完整执行边界的快照、恢复、分支与回滚，在RTX 5090上实现亚毫秒级GPU端快照与恢复，TTFT相比冷预填加速3.9x~27x。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主流LLM服务系统通过分页或基数KV缓存复用前缀工作，仅管理KV缓存这一单一位置执行片段，不适用于低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务中交互式LLM代理、语音系统和机器人策略反复分支、重置、中断和重新进入的场景。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种图绑定检查点与恢复机制，在提交边界处保存完整可恢复状态。FlashRT是白盒后端内核运行时，其NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于活动状态是封闭的命名缓冲区集合，胶囊可以快照、恢复、分支或回滚整个执行边界，包括KV、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据。

Result: 在RTX 5090上：胶囊恢复在存储状态级别字节精确，在贪婪解码下token一致；仅KV的消融实验发散，表明循环状态至关重要；GPU端快照与恢复亚毫秒级；TTFT加速比从2k token时的3.9x提升至16k token时的27x。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上保持相同的正确性和结构性质。

Conclusion: 胶囊不是高吞吐量KV缓存服务的替代品，而是定义了一个互补的、以延迟为先的服务点，用于显式执行状态复用。

Original Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

中文摘要翻译: 主流LLM服务系统主要通过分页或基数键值（KV）缓存来复用前缀工作。这对于高吞吐量、高并发服务非常有效，但它只管理执行状态的单一位置片段：KV缓存。我们研究相反的场景：低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务，其中交互式LLM代理、语音系统和机器人策略在紧凑的响应性预算下反复进行分支、重置、中断和重新进入。我们提出执行状态胶囊，一种在提交边界处保存完整可恢复状态的图绑定检查点与恢复机制。FlashRT是一个白盒、面向后端的内核运行时，其经过评估的NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于活动状态是封闭的命名缓冲区集合，胶囊可以快照、恢复、分支或回滚整个执行边界，包括KV、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据。这将复用从token寻址的KV片段转移到图绑定的执行状态边界。在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别是字节精确的，在贪婪解码下是token一致的。仅KV的消融实验出现发散，表明循环状态是承载负载的。GPU端快照和恢复是亚毫秒级的，TTFT加速比从2k token时的3.9x增长到16k token时的27x。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上，相同的正确性和结构性质仍然成立。胶囊不是高吞吐量KV缓存服务的替代品；它们定义了一个互补的、以延迟为先的服务点，用于显式执行状态复用。

</details>


### [224] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多任务上下文学习框架用于摊销分层贝叶斯预测推断，通过将先验信息表示为上下文数据集的前缀，使Transformer能够在测试时适应不同先验，在多个具有挑战性的评估中匹配oracle贝叶斯预测器且速度快数个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可扩展贝叶斯推断方法计算成本高或需限制性建模假设；Prior-Data Fitted和上下文模型虽作为摊销替代方案，但与训练先验的支撑紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时适应新先验的显式机制，导致分布漂移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架：将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀（prefix）；Transformer在由先验任务和目标任务组成的序列上进行训练，学习跨先验族自适应调整预测分布。

Result: 在多个难度递增的评估（包括元分布外先验和高维潜在结构先验）中匹配oracle贝叶斯预测器，计算速度快数个数量级；在真实世界时空温度预测基准上也验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效实现了对多样化先验的摊销贝叶斯推断，兼具理论上的准确性和实际中的计算高效性。

Original Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

中文摘要翻译: 贝叶斯预测推断为不确定性量化、数据效率和鲁棒泛化提供了原则性框架。然而，精确推断往往难以处理，可扩展的近似方法可能仍然计算昂贵或需要限制性建模假设，从而降低预测性能。Prior-Data Fitted模型和上下文模型最近作为一种摊销替代方案出现，通过学习直接将数据集映射到预测分布，但现有方法与训练先验的支撑紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时适应新先验的显式机制，导致分布漂移下的鲁棒性有限。我们提出了一种用于摊销分层贝叶斯预测推断的多任务上下文学习框架，该框架将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀。一个在先验任务和目标任务序列上训练的Transformer学习跨先验族自适应调整其预测。在多个难度递增的评估中（包括元分布外先验和具有高维潜在结构的先验），我们的方法匹配oracle贝叶斯预测器，同时速度快数个数量级。我们还在一个真实世界的时空温度预测基准上进一步证明了其实用性。代码可在 https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/ 获取。

</details>


### [225] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“基于可预测性的隐私”（Privacy via Predictability）的细粒度隐私框架，通过衡量攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力增量来量化隐私泄露，并与差分隐私（DP）进行了对比分析，证明了二者不可比较，但可互补使用以实现更精细的隐私控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）虽然提供了严格的个体级隐私保证，但其最坏情况性质导致隐私与准确性之间存在昂贵的权衡。现有方法缺乏针对特定攻击者知识和特定敏感信息的细粒度隐私度量，因此需要一种能够显式融入攻击者核心知识、被攻破数据以及特定查询家族的隐私框架。

Method: 本文提出“基于可预测性的隐私”（Predictability）框架，将隐私泄露定义为攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力相比于仅依据被攻破数据时的增量。引入广义矩估计（GMM）方法分析当被攻破数据由平稳、遍历、混合过程生成时的渐近可预测性，并基于此推导出一种可预测性校准的输出扰动方案用于经验风险最小化（ERM）。

Result: 证明可预测性与差分隐私一般情况下不可比较（各自可小可大）；在最坏情形下（仅一个个体未被攻破且所有二元查询均视为敏感时），可预测性蕴含互信息差分隐私。通过GMM框架提供了渐近分析，并导出了预测性校准的扰动方案。

Conclusion: 可预测性提供了一种比差分隐私更细粒度的隐私度量，能够针对特定敏感信息和特定攻击者模型进行定制。该框架与差分隐私是互补关系，可以联合使用以实现更精细的隐私控制。

Original Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

中文摘要翻译: 差分隐私（DP）即使在面对最强大的攻击者时也能提供严格的个体级隐私保证，但其最坏情况性质可能导致隐私与准确性之间代价高昂的权衡。本文引入了“基于可预测性的隐私”（Privacy via Predictability），一个细粒度的框架，该框架显式地融入了攻击者的核心知识、由随机过程生成的部分被攻破数据集以及一个指定的查询家族。可预测性将隐私泄露量化为：攻击者在观察算法输出后，对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力相较于仅依据被攻破数据时所能推断出的信息所获得的增量。我们证明可预测性与DP在一般情况下是不可比较的：其中一个可以很小而另一个很大。然而，在最坏情形下，即仅有一个个体未被攻破且所有二元查询均被视为敏感时，可预测性蕴含互信息差分隐私。更一般地，可预测性提供了一种针对特定敏感信息和特定攻击者模型定制的更细粒度隐私度量。我们引入了一个通用框架，利用广义矩估计（GMM）方法，来分析当被攻破数据由平稳、遍历、混合过程生成时的渐近可预测性。基于该分析，我们推导出一种用于经验风险最小化（ERM）的可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。我们的方法与DP是互补的，可以 alongside DP 一起使用，以实现细粒度的隐私控制。

</details>


### [226] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准（multicalibration）中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，提出了达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性预测器算法，并推广到结果不可区分性（OI）、全知预测器和泛预测器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有极小极大最优的多校准算法都是随机化的，确定性预测器的样本复杂度远差于最优率。多个先前工作（如CLNR26、OKK25、BHHLZ25）明确提出了随机化是否必要这一开放问题。

Method: 提出一种新的确定性多校准算法，达到$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的极小极大最优样本复杂度；然后将该算法推广到满足结果不可区分性（OI）的确定性预测器，进一步应用于全知预测器和泛预测器。

Result: 给出了首个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性多校准算法；推广后得到了满足OI的最优确定性预测器，以及具有最优样本复杂度的确定性全知预测器和泛预测器，解决了多个开放问题。

Conclusion: 随机化并非多校准达到最优样本复杂度的必要条件，确定性预测器同样可以达到最优率。

Original Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

中文摘要翻译: 如果一个模型在预测条件组权重集合$G$上满足多校准（multicalibration），那么它不仅总体上无偏，而且在按每个$g \in G$重新加权上下文后仍然保持无偏（即条件于预测时无偏）。这一性质对许多下游应用非常有用，也是可信机器学习的一个基本要求。在本工作之前，所有已知能达到$\varepsilon$-多校准极小极大最优$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$样本复杂度的预测器都是随机化的，而确定性预测器的样本复杂度则差得多。随机化对于多校准的最优样本复杂度是否必要这一问题，由[CLNR26]以及多项先前工作明确提出。我们通过提出一个输出确定性预测器的极小极大最优多校准算法，解决了这一开放问题。随后我们将该算法推广，以产生满足关于有限或有限覆盖的测试集合的结果不可区分性（OI）的最优确定性预测器。作为应用，这也给出了具有最优样本复杂度的确定性全知预测器（omnipredictors）和泛预测器（panpredictors），解决了[OKK25]和[BHHLZ25]提出的开放问题。

</details>


### [227] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究DiffusionGemma（扩散语言模型）的推理透明度，将其分解为变量透明度和算法透明度。尽管DiffusionGemma在连续潜空间中进行了大量计算，初始看似不透明，但通过可解释的token瓶颈映射信息流，其变量透明度可与自回归模型媲美。然而其算法透明度更差，因为所有token在每个去噪步骤都可能变化。作者通过案例研究发现了非时间顺序推理、token/序列涂抹等扩散特有现象，并证明其在可监控性上与Gemma 4相当。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma）在连续潜空间中进行大量计算后，其推理透明度是否比自回归模型更差。

Method: 将透明度分解为变量透明度（理解中间计算状态）和算法透明度（重构推理过程）。通过可解释的token瓶颈映射去噪步骤间的信息流来改善变量透明度；通过一系列可解释性案例研究来探索算法透明度。

Result: 1) 变量透明度：初始不透明序列深度是Gemma 4的28.6倍，但通过token瓶颈可降至1.1倍。2) 算法透明度：扩散模型更难，发现了非时间顺序推理、token/序列涂抹、中间上下文推理等新现象。3) 可监控性：与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: DiffusionGemma的变量透明度可通过技术手段大幅改善，但算法透明度仍是开放问题；整体可监控性表现良好。

Original Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

中文摘要翻译: LLM推理透明度是理解模型决策、减轻误用和错配、以及调试令人惊讶的模型行为的关键能力。然而，DiffusionGemma在其连续潜空间中执行了更大比例的计算；这是否使其推理透明度降低？我们通过将透明度分解为两个组成部分来研究这个问题：变量透明度，即我们是否理解模型计算状态的中间快照；以及算法透明度，即我们能否使用这些快照重建模型得出其输出的过程。直观来看，DiffusionGemma的变量透明度较差：其不透明序列深度，即可解释模型状态之间发生的串行计算量，最初看起来是相应的自回归Gemma 4模型的28.6倍。然而，我们证明了可以通过一个可解释的token瓶颈来映射去噪步骤之间流动的信息，且下游性能没有下降。将这些中间状态视为可解释的，可将不透明序列深度降低至Gemma 4的1.1倍。扩散模型的算法透明度比自回归模型更难，因为画布中的所有token预测在每个去噪步骤中都可以改变，这赋予了模型在去噪过程中实现复杂分布式算法的能力。为了开始弥合这一差距，我们进行了一系列可解释性案例研究，发现了新的扩散特有现象的初步证据，例如非时间顺序推理、token和序列涂抹以及中间上下文推理。最后，我们测试了可监控性，这是透明度的一个关键应用，用于衡量模型输出对下游任务是否有用。我们发现DiffusionGemma的可监控性与Gemma 4相当。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Multistatic J-Band Radar TX/RX Chipset in SiGe BiCMOS with Integrated x16 Frequency Multiplier Chain and High EIRP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19536)
*Stephan Hauptmeier,Kennet Braasch,Till Ziegler-Bellenberg,Diana P. Cortes N.,Tobias T. Braun,Michael Höft,Nils Pohl*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出并测量了一款多基地J波段雷达芯片组，包含发射和接收MMIC，集成了×16倍频链用于低频本振分布。发射芯片采用四路功率合成放大器，在292 GHz处实现11.2 dBm输出功率，配合透镜EIRP达41 dBm；接收芯片转换增益43.3 dB。芯片组在130 nm SiGe BiCMOS工艺上实现，测距可达150米。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多基地雷达架构可同时维持高发射功率和高接收灵敏度，但需要低相位噪声、可扩展的本振信号分配方案。现有毫米波雷达芯片在高频本振分布和功率合成方面存在挑战，本文旨在通过集成×16倍频链解决低成本低频本振分布问题，并实现高性能的多基地雷达芯片组。

Method: 采用130 nm SiGe BiCMOS工艺（f_T/f_max=500/610 GHz）设计发射和接收MMIC；发射芯片集成×16倍频链和四路功率合成放大器链，输出功率11.2 dBm，配合PTFE准直透镜实现高EIRP；接收芯片集成×16倍频链、三级低噪声放大器和下变频混频器；两个芯片均集成贴片天线，支持高指向性介质透镜。

Result: 发射芯片在292 GHz处输出功率11.2 dBm，带透镜EIRP为41 dBm，无透镜为8.8 dBm；片内谐波抑制优于24 dBc，辐射带内谐波抑制约50 dBc；接收芯片转换增益43.3 dB（292 GHz）；成功演示了长达150米的多基地雷达测距。

Conclusion: 本文设计的多基地J波段雷达芯片组通过集成×16倍频链和功率合成技术，成功实现了低成本低频本振分配下的高性能毫米波雷达系统。该芯片组在发射功率、接收灵敏度、谐波抑制和测距能力方面均表现出色，适用于远程雷达探测应用。

Original Abstract: This work presents the design and measurement of a multistatic J-band radar chipset comprising a transmitter and a receiver MMIC both featuring an integrated $times$16 frequency multiplier chain for low-frequency local-oscillator distribution and scalable radar configurations. Multistatic radar architectures can sustain high transmission power and high receiver sensitivity simultaneously an advantage that is fully leveraged in the present chipset. To this end a four-way power-combining amplifier chain integrated on the transmitter MMIC delivers an output power of 11.2 dBm. The resulting measured EIRP is 41 dBm at 292 GHz with a collimating PTFE lens and 8.8 dBm without a lens. Despite the high frequency-multiplication factor an on-chip harmonic rejection better than 24 dBc was measured while a radiated in-band harmonic rejection of approximately 50 dBc was achieved through multiple filter stages. The receiver MMIC incorporates a three-stage low-noise amplifier and exhibits an overall conversion gain of 43.3 dB at 292 GHz. Integrated on-chip patch antennas facilitate system integration and the use of highly directive dielectric lenses making the chipset suitable for long-range radar measurements which are demonstrated up to 150 m. The MMICs are realized in a 130 nm SiGe BiCMOS technology with an f_T and f_max of 500 GHz and 610 GHz respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 本文介绍了一款多基地J波段雷达芯片组的设计与测量，该芯片组包括发射和接收MMIC，均集成×16倍频链，用于低频本振分配和可扩展的雷达构型。多基地雷达架构可同时维持高发射功率和高接收灵敏度，这一优势在本芯片组中得到充分利用。为此，发射MMIC上集成的四路功率合成放大器链在292 GHz处提供11.2 dBm的输出功率。配合PTFE准直透镜时，测得的等效各向同性辐射功率（EIRP）为41 dBm，无透镜时为8.8 dBm。尽管倍频倍数很高，测得的片内谐波抑制优于24 dBc，而通过多级滤波器实现的辐射带内谐波抑制约为50 dBc。接收MMIC包含三级低噪声放大器，在292 GHz处整体转换增益为43.3 dB。片上集成贴片天线有助于系统集成并支持高指向性介质透镜，使该芯片组适用于远程雷达测量，已成功演示至150米距离。MMIC采用130 nm SiGe BiCMOS工艺实现，其f_T和f_max分别为500 GHz和610 GHz。

</details>


### [229] [Degrees of Freedom and Beamforming for Large Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19666)
*Jiawang Li,Alireza Saberkari,Buon Kiong Lau,Mats Gustafsson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对大型智能表面（LIS）中多用户近场通信场景，提出了基于互阴影面积的自由度（DoF）闭式估计方法，并通过线源和面源配置下的数值采样与波束成形验证了DoF预测的准确性，同时发现极化分量对DoF的贡献不均衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在多用户大型智能表面（LIS）系统中，电磁场需要在多个近场位置进行整形、分辨和聚焦，而空间自由度（DoF）的准确估计是实现高效采样和波束成形的理论基础，目前缺乏简洁的闭式解析表达。

Method: 推导了基于互阴影面积的DoF闭式表达式；针对线源配置，通过将源或观测线划分为单位DoF区间提出解析采样方案；针对面源配置，采用离散经验插值方法（DEIM）数值确定采样点；通过最大比传输（MRT）和迫零（ZF）波束成形进行验证。

Result: 数值奇异值谱的膝点与理论DoF估计高度吻合；波束成形结果表明可形成约等于DoF数量的独立波束，超出此限制会导致干扰增加和性能下降；面源配置进一步证实目标区域可支持的独立波束数量与DoF分析一致；极化分析表明电场分量对DoF贡献不均衡，总场DoF是单个极化分量的两倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于互阴影面积的DoF估计方法可准确预测LIS系统的空间自由度，为近场多用户通信中的采样和波束成形设计提供了有效的理论指导，同时揭示了极化维度对自由度的倍增效应。

Original Abstract: Spatial degrees of freedom (DoF), sampling, and beamforming are fundamental to multi-user large intelligent surfaces (LISs), where electromagnetic fields must be shaped, resolved, and focused at multiple near-field locations. This work estimates the number of DoF using closed-form expressions derived from the mutual shadow area for representative LIS configurations. The resulting DoF predictions are validated through numerical singular-value spectra, whose spectral knee points closely match the theoretical estimates. For line-source configurations, an analytic sampling scheme is developed by partitioning the source or observation line into unit-DoF intervals, enabling the selection of spatial samples. Beamforming results using maximum-ratio transmission and zero-forcing demonstrate that approximately the number of DoF independent beams can be formed. Attempting to exceed this limit results in increased interference and degraded performance. For surface-based LIS configurations, sampling points are instead determined numerically using the discrete empirical interpolation method. The corresponding beamforming results further confirm that the target region can support approximately as many independent beams as predicted by the DoF analysis. Finally, a polarization-aware study reveals that the electric-field components contribute unequally to the DoF and that the total-field DoF is twice that of a single polarization component.

中文摘要翻译: 空间自由度（DoF）、采样和波束成形是多用户大型智能表面（LIS）系统的基础，其中电磁场必须在多个近场位置进行整形、分辨和聚焦。本文利用互阴影面积的闭式表达式，针对代表性LIS配置估算了自由度数量。通过数值奇异值谱对DoF预测结果进行了验证，谱膝点与理论估计高度吻合。针对线源配置，通过将源或观测线划分为单位DoF区间，提出了一种解析采样方案，实现了空间样本的选择。采用最大比传输和迫零的波束成形结果表明，可形成约等于DoF数量的独立波束。试图超出此限制会导致干扰增大和性能下降。针对面源配置，采用离散经验插值方法数值确定采样点。相应的波束成形结果进一步证实，目标区域可支持的独立波束数量与DoF分析的预测一致。最后，极化感知研究表明，电场各分量对DoF的贡献不均衡，且总场DoF是单个极化分量的两倍。

</details>


### [230] [Generalized Pinching-Antenna Systems: A Radio-Stripe-Based Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19715)
*Yanqing Xu,Zhiguo Ding,Tsung-Hui Chang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无线电条带（RS）的广义钉型天线（RS-GPA）框架，通过沿电缆部署有源天线处理单元（APU）实现灵活、低功耗的无线接入，并设计了上下行链路的稀疏APU激活与波束成形算法，显著降低了总功耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于介质波导的无源钉型天线依赖导波到自由空间的无源耦合，灵活性受限。本文旨在通过有源APU架构克服这一局限，实现可部署灵活、频率适用性广且功耗可控的广义钉型天线系统。

Method: 提出RS-GPA框架；建立距离相关的APU-用户信道模型；针对下行链路设计电路功率感知的稀疏APU激活与波束成形问题，开发重加权群稀疏波束成形算法，并基于单用户分析提出几何引导的低复杂度多用户算法；针对上行链路设计联合APU激活与用户功率控制问题，开发几何引导的稀疏激活方案。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的RS-GPA框架相比基准方案大幅降低了总功耗；几何引导算法在功耗性能上与群稀疏设计几乎一致，但运行时间显著降低。

Conclusion: RS-GPA框架是一种高效、低复杂度的有源钉型天线实现方案，通过有源APU的灵活激活和优化算法，在保证通信性能的同时实现了显著的功耗节省。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates radio stripes (RSs) as a practical realization of generalized pinching antennas and proposes an RS-based generalized pinching-antenna (RS-GPA) framework. Unlike dielectric-waveguide-based passive pinching antennas that rely on passive coupling from a guided wave into free space, RSs employ active antenna processing units (APUs) deployed along a shared cable for local transmission, reception, and signal processing. This cable-like active architecture offers flexible installation and broad frequency applicability, while allowing selected APUs to act as discrete and controllable radiation or reception points for location-flexible wireless access. Based on the proposed RS-GPA framework, we establish the system and channel models by accounting for the distance-dependent APU-user channels. For downlink transmission, we formulate a circuit-power-aware sparse APU activation and beamforming problem and develop a reweighted group-sparse beamforming algorithm. To reveal the activation principle, we analyze the single-user downlink case and characterize when an additional APU should be activated by balancing transmit-power saving and circuit-power cost. Inspired by this insight, a geometry-guided low-complexity multiuser algorithm is proposed. For uplink transmission, we formulate a joint APU activation and user power control problem and develop a geometry-guided sparse activation design. Numerical results show that the proposed RS-GPA framework substantially reduces the total consumed power compared with benchmark schemes, while the geometry-guided algorithm achieves near-identical consumed-power performance to the group-sparse design with significantly lower runtime.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了无线电条带（RS）作为广义钉型天线的一种实际实现方式，并提出了基于RS的广义钉型天线（RS-GPA）框架。与依赖导波到自由空间无源耦合的介质波导无源钉型天线不同，RS采用沿共享电缆部署的有源天线处理单元（APU），用于本地发射、接收和信号处理。这种类电缆的有源架构具有灵活的安装性和广泛的频率适用性，同时允许选定的APU作为离散可控的辐射或接收点，实现位置灵活的无线接入。基于所提出的RS-GPA框架，我们通过考虑距离相关的APU-用户信道，建立了系统和信道模型。针对下行链路传输，我们构建了电路功率感知的稀疏APU激活与波束成形问题，并开发了一种重加权群稀疏波束成形算法。为揭示激活原理，我们分析了单用户下行链路场景，刻画了何时应激活额外APU以平衡发射功率节省与电路功率开销。受此启发，提出了一种几何引导的低复杂度多用户算法。针对上行链路传输，我们构建了联合APU激活与用户功率控制问题，并开发了几何引导的稀疏激活设计方案。数值结果表明，与基准方案相比，所提出的RS-GPA框架显著降低了总功耗，而几何引导算法在功耗性能上与群稀疏设计几乎一致，且运行时间显著降低。

</details>


### [231] [An Optimization Framework for Certain Separable Problems using Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19720)
*Rohit Negi,Soummya Kar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对实时应用中参数可分离的约束优化问题，提出了一种基于离线学习与在线处理的两阶段策略，利用ADMM迭代过程结合神经网络协处理器，在资源受限设备上实现轻量化在线计算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实时应用中的参数化约束优化问题需要在资源有限的设备上高效求解，传统的在线求解方法计算复杂度过高，难以满足实时性要求。

Method: 提出两阶段策略：1）离线学习阶段，利用参数可分离结构，采用基于ADMM的迭代求解过程，并引入学习型函数表示（如神经网络）；2）在线处理阶段，在设备上借助神经网络协处理器进行轻量级在线计算，即使参数变化也能高效求解。

Result: 通过精心设计ADMM过程，证明了即使参数发生变化，对应的参数化优化问题实例仍可通过设备上的轻量级在线计算配合神经网络协处理器高效求解。

Conclusion: 所提出的离线学习与在线处理相结合的策略能够显著降低资源受限设备上参数化优化问题的在线实现复杂度，为实时应用提供可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: This paper studies a class of parametric constrained optimization problems that are motivated by applications in real time applications. Under a parameter-separable problem structure that naturally arises in these applications, the paper proposes a two phase strategy, based on offline learning and online processing, to address these optimization problems on resource limited devices. Specifically, by exploiting the separable structure, an iterative Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) based solution procedure is developed that enables the use of certain learning based function representations (learned offline but readily computable online) to reduce the overall online on-device implementation complexity. By carefully crafting the ADMM procedure, it is shown that even as the parameters vary, the corresponding instances of the parametric optimization problem may be solved by lightweight online computations in the device with the assistance of a neural network co-processor.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究一类由实时应用需求驱动的参数化约束优化问题。针对这些应用中自然出现的参数可分离问题结构，本文提出了一种基于离线学习与在线处理的两阶段策略，以在资源受限的设备上解决这些优化问题。具体而言，通过利用可分离结构，开发了一种基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的迭代求解过程，该过程允许使用某些基于学习的函数表示（离线学习但在线可直接计算），从而降低整体在线设备实现的复杂度。通过精心设计ADMM过程，证明了即使参数发生变化，对应的参数化优化问题实例仍可借助神经网络协处理器，在设备上通过轻量级的在线计算得以求解。

</details>


### [232] [Cyclic-Prefix OFDM Probing for Spatial-ISI-Free Distributed Acoustic Sensing via Frequency-Domain Channel Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19724)
*Huan Huang,Zhiyang Xue,Ziang Chen,Zhongxing Tian,Dongdong Zou,Gangxiang Shen,Yi Cai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CP-OFDM的相位敏感光时域反射计（φ-OTDR）分布式声学传感（DAS）系统，通过将分布式瑞利背向散射建模为有限记忆传感多径信道，利用CP-OFDM波形同时实现无空间符号间干扰（ISI）的相位解调与前向通信数据恢复，首次演示了共享波形的光纤集成感知与通信（ISAC）。实验和仿真表明，该方法在抑制旁瓣泄漏和提升相位重建精度上显著优于传统匹配滤波脉冲压缩方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统匹配滤波脉冲压缩DAS存在非零压缩旁瓣，导致确定性的距离单元间泄漏（空间ISI）和虚假响应，影响重建瑞利背向散射迹线的准确性。现有方法无法从根本上消除这种波形引入的空间ISI，也缺乏同时实现感知与通信的共享波形方案。

Method: 提出CP-OFDM DAS系统，使用数据承载的CP-OFDM波形作为传感探针。将分布式瑞利背向散射建模为有限记忆传感多径信道；当有用OFDM长度和循环前缀长度覆盖传感多径记忆时，通过移除CP、单抽头频域均衡和逆离散傅里叶变换，无失真地重建每个距离单元的系数，实现无空间ISI的相位解调。同时从同一探针中恢复前向通信数据。

Result: 在5.2 km仿真链路中（组内间隔5.31–5.83 m的十组同时强/弱事件），所提接收机将离事件泄漏抑制了最多29.55 dB（相位迹线均方误差）。在5.2 km光纤外差相干实验中（占用带宽111.984 MHz），500 Hz PZT振动在5.071 km和5.066 km处被盲定位（5 V和1 V驱动），波形恢复相关系数分别为0.990和0.962。同一探针恢复图像时测得零误码率，中位误差矢量幅度为-23.14 dB。

Conclusion: CP-OFDM辅助的频域信道重建方法能有效实现无空间ISI的DAS，并展示了共享波形光纤ISAC的潜力。

Original Abstract: Matched-filter-based pulse-compression distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) suffers from nonzero compression sidelobes that cause deterministic inter-range-bin leakage, i.e., spatial inter-symbol interference (ISI), and false responses in reconstructed Rayleigh-backscatter traces. We propose a cyclic-prefix orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (CP-OFDM) DAS system for $φ$-OTDR, using a data-bearing CP-OFDM waveform as the sensing probe. It also recovers forward communication data, providing an initial demonstration of shared-waveform integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). To our knowledge, this is the first formulation of distributed Rayleigh backscattering as a finite-memory sensing multipath channel. Based on this formulation, we prove that, if the useful OFDM and CP lengths cover the sensing multipath memory, CP removal, one-tap frequency-domain equalization, and inverse discrete Fourier transform reconstruct each range-bin coefficient without deterministic waveform-induced spatial ISI, enabling spatial-ISI-free phase demodulation. For a simulated 5.2-km link with ten simultaneous strong and weak events spaced by 5.31--5.83 m within groups, the proposed receiver suppresses off-event leakage and improves phase-trace mean-square error by up to 29.55 dB over matched-filter pulse compression. In a heterodyne coherent experiment over a 5.2-km fiber link with 111.984-MHz occupied bandwidth, 500-Hz PZT vibrations are blindly localized at 5.071 and 5.066 km under 5- and 1-V drives, respectively, and their waveforms are recovered with correlation coefficients of 0.990 and 0.962. The same data-bearing probe also recovers an image with zero measured bit-error rate and a median error vector magnitude of -23.14 dB. These results validate CP-OFDM-aided frequency-domain channel reconstruction for spatial-ISI-free DAS and demonstrate its potential for shared-waveform optical-fiber ISAC.

中文摘要翻译: 基于匹配滤波脉冲压缩的分布式声学传感（DAS）因存在非零压缩旁瓣，会导致确定性的距离单元间泄漏（即空间符号间干扰，ISI）以及重建瑞利背向散射迹线中的虚假响应。本文提出一种用于φ-OTDR的循环前缀正交频分复用（CP-OFDM）DAS系统，采用承载数据的CP-OFDM波形作为传感探针。该系统同时恢复前向通信数据，首次实现了共享波形集成感知与通信（ISAC）。据我们所知，本文首次将分布式瑞利背向散射建模为有限记忆传感多径信道。基于该模型，我们证明：当有用OFDM长度和循环前缀长度覆盖传感多径记忆时，通过移除循环前缀、单抽头频域均衡和逆离散傅里叶变换，可重建每个距离单元的系数，且不引入由波形决定的确定性空间ISI，从而实现无空间ISI的相位解调。在模拟的5.2 km链路上，组内间隔5.31–5.83 m的十组同时强/弱事件条件下，所提接收机抑制了离事件泄漏，并相比匹配滤波脉冲压缩将相位迹线均方误差提升了最多29.55 dB。在占用带宽111.984 MHz的5.2 km光纤外差相干实验中，500 Hz PZT振动在5 V和1 V驱动下分别被盲定位在5.071 km和5.066 km处，波形恢复相关系数分别为0.990和0.962。同一承载数据探针恢复图像时测得零误码率，中位误差矢量幅度为-23.14 dB。这些结果验证了CP-OFDM辅助频域信道重建可实现无空间ISI的DAS，并展示了其在共享波形光纤ISAC中的应用潜力。

</details>


### [233] [ConsisFormer: Compute-Efficient Transformer for Wireless Foundation Models Based on Channel Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19953)
*Yuwei Wang,Li Sun,Tingting Yang,Liwen Jing,Yuxuan Shi,Maged Elkashlan,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出ConsisFormer，一种基于无线信道短时一致性、通过自适应令牌聚合和特征序列插值降低计算复杂度的Transformer架构，作为无线基础模型骨干，在保持性能的同时将计算复杂度降低83%以上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无线基础模型主要基于Transformer架构，虽然性能优越，但其计算复杂度随输入序列长度呈平方级增长，在严格的推理延迟约束下难以部署。亟需一种计算高效的Transformer设计以降低WFM的计算成本。

Method: 1）**自适应令牌聚合（ATA）模块**：利用无线信道在相邻时频实例上共享相似散射体簇的短时一致性，动态合并相邻CSI令牌，减少自注意力计算中令牌序列长度；2）**特征序列插值（FSI）方法**：基于Transformer块输出的稀疏特征序列恢复完整CSI表示，保持性能不受影响；3）**聚合自编码器（AAE）预训练范式**：通过压缩与恢复从稀疏化CSI令牌中学习鲁棒的信道表示。

Result: 在信道预测、LoS/NLOS分类、波束预测和定位等多种任务上，所提设计将无线基础模型的计算复杂度降低超过83%，且性能损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ConsisFormer通过利用无线信道的短时一致性，结合自适应令牌聚合与特征序列插值，实现了计算高效且性能保持的Transformer设计，为无线基础模型在6G网络中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Wireless foundation models (WFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for AI-native 6G networks, enabling universal channel representations adaptable to diverse communication and sensing tasks. Existing WFMs are predominantly built upon the Transformer architecture, which delivers superior performance but incurs computational complexity proportional to the square of the input sequence length, posing a significant barrier to their deployment under stringent inference latency constraints. To address this issue, in this paper, we propose ConsisFormer, a compute-efficient Transformer design based on short-term consistency of wireless channels, as a WFM backbone. By utilizing the observation that adjacent time or frequency instances share similar clusters of scatterers and thus exhibit similar channel characteristics, we develop an adaptive token aggregation (ATA) module to dynamically merge neighboring channel state information (CSI) tokens, thereby reducing the length of the token sequence involved in self-attention calculations to lower the computational cost. Furthermore, we propose a feature sequence interpolation (FSI) method to recover the full CSI representation based on the sparse feature sequence outputted from the Transformer blocks, thus keeping the performance unaffected while ensuring low complexity. Moreover, we propose an aggregated auto-encoder (AAE) pre-training paradigm for WFMs, enabling robust channel representation learning from sparsified CSI tokens via compression and recovery. Simulation results show that the proposed design reduces the computational complexity of WFM by over $83\%$ with negligible performance loss on various tasks including channel prediction, LoS/NLOS classification, beam prediction, and localization.

中文摘要翻译: 无线基础模型（WFM）最近作为AI原生6G网络的一种有前景的范式出现，能够实现适应多种通信与感知任务的通用信道表示。现有WFM主要基于Transformer架构构建，该架构虽然性能优越，但其计算复杂度与输入序列长度的平方成正比，这给在严格推理延迟约束下的部署带来了重大障碍。为解决这一问题，本文提出ConsisFormer——一种基于无线信道短时一致性的计算高效Transformer设计，用作WFM骨干网络。利用相邻时间或频率实例共享相似散射体簇从而表现出相似信道特性这一观察，我们开发了一种自适应令牌聚合（ATA）模块，动态合并相邻信道状态信息（CSI）令牌，从而减少参与自注意力计算的令牌序列长度以降低计算成本。此外，我们提出了一种特征序列插值（FSI）方法，基于Transformer块输出的稀疏特征序列恢复完整的CSI表示，从而在确保低复杂度的同时保持性能不受影响。进一步地，我们提出了一种聚合自编码器（AAE）预训练范式用于WFM，通过压缩与恢复从稀疏化CSI令牌中学习鲁棒的信道表示。仿真结果表明，所提设计在信道预测、视距/非视距分类、波束预测和定位等多种任务上将WFM的计算复杂度降低超过83%，且性能损失可忽略不计。

</details>


### [234] [Amplitude-Phase-Frequency Block Modulation for OFDM-ISAC with SI-Free PAPR Reduction and Pilotless Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20011)
*Bensheng Yang,Min Fan,Haitao Zhao,Haiming Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种振幅-相位-频率块调制（APFBM）方案，在不划分资源的前提下实现OFDM通感一体化波形设计，通过Stokes球映射和分组相位优化实现无旁瓣信息PAPR降低，并利用Viterbi最大似然序列检测实现无导频信道感知。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于OFDM的通感一体化系统要么在时域或频域上分开占用资源，要么依赖专用导频进行信道估计，导致系统灵活性受限且开销增大。本文旨在设计一种无需资源划分、无需导频、且能同时支持可靠通信、低PAPR和精确信道感知的统一波形。

Method: 提出APFBM方案：1）将信息符号映射到Stokes球上的Jones向量，建立每块确定的相位参考；2）利用信号结构中的公共相位自由度，采用分组相位优化算法降低PAPR，无需侧信息；3）接收端利用Viterbi最大似然序列检测算法联合恢复优化相位并估计块级信道幅度和相位，直接从通信波形中提取感知观测量。

Result: 推导了闭式的误码率和感知精度表达式。数值仿真和软件无线电平台上的空口实测验证了方案能有效降低PAPR、实现精确信道感知、可靠相位恢复和稳定CSI重建。

Conclusion: 所提方案以适度的频谱效率降低为代价，实现了无需侧信息PAPR降低和无导频感知的统一波形设计。

Original Abstract: Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM)-based integrated sensing and communication systems demand a unified waveform that simultaneously supports reliable data transmission, low peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR), and accurate channel sensing. Existing approaches multiplex communication and sensing across separate time or frequency resources, or rely on dedicated pilots for channel estimation, limiting system flexibility and increasing overhead. This paper proposes an amplitude-phase-frequency block modulation (APFBM) scheme for OFDM that achieves waveform-level integration of communication and sensing without resource partitioning. Information symbols are represented on the Stokes sphere and mapped to energy-normalized Jones vectors through an unambiguous rule that establishes a deterministic phase reference per block. This mapping exposes a commonphase degree of freedom inherent in the signal structure. At the transmitter, a grouped phase optimization algorithm exploits this structural freedom to reduce the PAPR without side information (SI). At the receiver, the same deterministic phase structure enables a Viterbi-based maximum-likelihood (ML) sequence detection algorithm that jointly recovers the optimization phases and estimates the block-wise channel amplitude and phase. No dedicated sensing pilots are required, as the sensing observables are extracted directly from the communication waveform. Closed-form error-rate and sensing-accuracy expressions are derived. Numerical simulations and over-the-air measurements on a software-defined radio link confirm effective PAPR reduction, accurate channel sensing, reliable phase recovery, and stable channel state information reconstruction. The proposed scheme trades a moderate reduction in spectral efficiency for a unified waveform design that simultaneously delivers SI-free PAPR reduction and pilotless sensing.

中文摘要翻译: 基于正交频分复用（OFDM）的通感一体化系统需要一种统一的波形，能够同时支持可靠的数据传输、低峰均功率比（PAPR）和精确的信道感知。现有方法将通信与感知在时间或频率资源上分开复用，或依赖专用导频进行信道估计，从而限制了系统灵活性并增加了开销。本文提出了一种用于OFDM的振幅-相位-频率块调制（APFBM）方案，在不划分资源的前提下实现通信与感知的波形级融合。信息符号在Stokes球上表示，并通过一个明确的映射规则映射到能量归一化的Jones向量上，该规则为每个块建立了确定的相位参考。这一映射暴露了信号结构中固有的公共相位自由度。在发送端，分组相位优化算法利用该结构自由度降低PAPR，且无需侧信息（SI）。在接收端，相同的确定性相位结构使得基于Viterbi的最大似然（ML）序列检测算法能够联合恢复优化相位并估计块级信道幅度和相位。由于感知观测量直接从通信波形中提取，因此无需专用感知导频。推导了闭式的误码率和感知精度表达式。数值仿真和软件无线电链路上的空口实测结果证实了有效的PAPR降低、精确的信道感知、可靠的相位恢复以及稳定的信道状态信息重建。所提方案以适度的频谱效率降低为代价，实现了无需侧信息的PAPR降低与无导频感知相统一的波形设计。

</details>


### [235] [Evaluation of EEG Foundation Models for Event-Based Burst-Suppression Detection in ICU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20074)
*Elisa Vasta,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Andrea Cossettini,Luca Benini,Tilman Beck,Emanuela Keller,Una Pale*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文首次评估EEG基础模型在ICU低导联脑电图上进行爆发抑制检测的表现。REVE-base模型在事件级F1分数上达到最优（0.868），相比EEGNet和自适应阈值法分别降低52.1%和36.2%的每分钟爆发次数误差，且全微调优于冻结骨干等适配策略，在标注数据有限时预训练表征优势显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 爆发抑制（BS）是ICU镇静深度监测中的重要EEG模式，但自动检测因患者间变异大、标注数据稀缺而面临挑战。尽管EEG基础模型在多项下游任务中展现出潜力，其在爆发抑制检测中的有效性尚未被探索。

Method: 对比三种EEG基础模型（REVE-base、LUNA-large、LuMamba-Tiny）与自适应阈值基线和任务特定EEGNet基线，采用事件级检测评估（而非传统窗口分类），并开展消融实验比较全微调、冻结骨干训练、两阶段微调和LoRA适配策略。

Result: REVE-base取得最优事件级F1分数（0.868±0.167），每分钟爆发次数误差较EEGNet和自适应阈值分别降低52.1%和36.2%；全微调策略最优，较冻结骨干训练在LUNA-large上F1提升+0.102；仅用25%标注数据时，预训练REVE-base比随机初始化F1提升+0.723。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型在ICU低导联EEG爆发抑制检测中显著优于传统方法和任务特定模型，全微调是最有效的适配策略，且预训练表征在小样本场景下优势突出，支持FM用于可扩展的ICU EEG监测。

Original Abstract: Burst suppression (BS) is a clinically relevant electroencephalographic (EEG) pattern used to monitor sedation depth and brain activity in critically ill patients, particularly during induced coma in Intensive Care Units (ICUs). Automatic burst detection remains challenging because BS patterns vary substantially between patients and annotated datasets are scarce. Recently, EEG Foundation Models (FMs) have shown promise across several downstream EEG applications, but their usefulness for BS detection remains unexplored. We present the first study to evaluate EEG FMs for burst detection in reduced-montage ICU EEG without patient-specific calibration. We compare REVE-base, LUNA-large and LuMamba-Tiny with an adaptive thresholding baseline and a task-specific EEGNet baseline. Additionally, we complement conventional EEG window-based classification with event-based burst detection evaluation. This helps assessing clinically whether burst episodes are correctly detected, reducing the impact of expected annotation variability. The best model, REVE-base, achieved the highest event-based F1-score ($0.868 \pm 0.167$) and reduced burst-per-minute error by 52.1% and 36.2% compared to EEGNet and adaptive thresholding respectively, supporting FMs for scalable EEG monitoring in ICU. Ablation experiments showed that full fine-tuning was the most effective adaptation strategy with respect to frozen-backbone training, two-step fine-tuning, and LoRA-based adaptation, improving event-based F1-score over frozen-backbone training by up to $+0.102$ for LUNA-large. With reduced labeled datasets, pretrained REVE-base outperformed random initialization by $+0.723$ event-based F1 points at 25% of the cohort, demonstrating the benefit of pretraining FM representations when adapted to burst detection with limited labeled data.

中文摘要翻译: 爆发抑制（BS）是一种具有临床相关性的脑电图模式，用于监测危重症患者的镇静深度和大脑活动，尤其是在重症监护室（ICU）诱导昏迷期间。由于BS模式在不同患者之间存在显著差异，且标注数据集稀缺，自动爆发检测仍具有挑战性。最近，脑电图基础模型（EEG Foundation Models, FMs）在多个下游EEG应用中展现出潜力，但它们对BS检测的有效性尚未被探索。我们提出了首项研究，评估EEG FMs在无需患者特定校准的情况下，对ICU低导联脑电图进行爆发检测的性能。我们比较了REVE-base、LUNA-large和LuMamba-Tiny与自适应阈值基线以及任务特定EEGNet基线的表现。此外，我们在常规EEG窗口分类基础上补充了基于事件的爆发检测评估，这有助于从临床角度评估爆发事件是否被正确检测，减少预期中的标注变异性影响。最佳模型REVE-base取得了最高的基于事件的F1分数（0.868±0.167），与EEGNet和自适应阈值相比，每分钟爆发次数误差分别降低了52.1%和36.2%，支持FMs用于ICU中可扩展的EEG监测。消融实验表明，相对于冻结骨干训练、两阶段微调和基于LoRA的适配策略，全微调是最有效的适配策略，在LUNA-large上将基于事件的F1分数比冻结骨干训练提升了多达+0.102。在标注数据集减少的情况下，预训练的REVE-base在仅使用25%队列数据时，基于事件的F1分数比随机初始化高出+0.723，证明了在标注数据有限的爆发检测任务中，预训练FM表征的优势。

</details>


### [236] [Reliable ORIS-assisted FSO Communications via HARQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20222)
*Georgios D. Chondrogiannis,Athanasios P. Chrysologou,Vasilis K. Papanikolaou,Alexandros-Apostolos A. Boulogeorgos,Nestor D. Chatzidiamantis,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由光学可重构智能表面（ORIS）辅助、混合自动重传请求（HARQ）增强的自由空间光（FSO）链路。推导了端到端反射信道的统计模型，获得了HARQ-CC的闭合中断概率表达式和HARQ-IR的中断上界，分析了分集阶数和时延特性，验证了HARQ显著提升ORIS辅助FSO的可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: FSO链路易受障碍物遮挡、大气湍流、指向抖动和几何损耗影响，传统方案难以同时应对这些挑战。ORIS可创建虚拟视距路径绕过障碍，HARQ可通过重传和合并恢复受损帧，但两者联合的性能分析尚缺乏系统的理论模型。

Method: 1. 联合考虑大气湍流、ORIS指向误差和几何衰减，推导Tx-ORIS-Rx反射链路的统计模型；2. 推导HARQ-CC的中断概率闭合表达式和HARQ-IR的解析中断上界（适用于任意最大重传轮数）；3. 进行高信噪比分析以刻画中断行为和分集阶数；4. 通过平均传输轮数和条件平均轮数表征时延特性。

Result: 1. 获得了HARQ-CC的中断概率闭合表达式和HARQ-IR的中断上界；2. 高SNR分析揭示了两种方案的分集阶数；3. 数值与蒙特卡洛结果验证了分析的正确性；4. HARQ显著改善了ORIS辅助FSO的可靠性，HARQ-IR在中断和时延方面均优于HARQ-CC，即使重传轮数较少也是如此。

Conclusion: HARQ方案能有效提升ORIS辅助FSO链路的可靠性，其中HARQ-IR相比HARQ-CC具有更低的中断概率和更低的时延，为ORIS与HARQ联合优化提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: This paper studies a free-space optical (FSO) link assisted by an optical reconfigurable intelligent surface (ORIS) and enhanced by a hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ) scheme.
