The ORIS creates a virtual line-of-sight path around obstacles, while HARQ recovers frames corrupted by turbulence, pointing jitter, and geometric loss through retransmission and combining. We first derive a tractable statistical model for the end-to-end transmitter-ORIS-receiver (Tx-ORIS-Rx) reflected channel by jointly accounting for atmospheric turbulence, ORIS-induced pointing errors, and geometric attenuation. Building on these results, we obtain closed-form outage probability (OP) expressions for HARQ with Chase combining (HARQ-CC) and analytical outage upper bounds for HARQ with incremental redundancy (HARQ-IR), valid for an arbitrary maximum number of transmission rounds. We further conduct a high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) analysis that provides a thorough characterization of the outage behavior and reveals the diversity order of both schemes. In addition, we characterize the delay behavior of the truncated HARQ process through the mean number of transmission rounds and the conditional mean number of rounds given successful decoding. Finally, numerical and Monte Carlo results validate the proposed analysis and show that HARQ substantially improves ORIS-assisted FSO reliability, with HARQ-IR achieving lower outage and delay than HARQ-CC, even for a small number of retransmission rounds.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种由光学可重构智能表面（ORIS）辅助、并采用混合自动重传请求（HARQ）方案增强的自由空间光（FSO）链路。ORIS可绕过障碍物创建虚拟视距路径，而HARQ则通过重传与合并技术恢复因湍流、指向抖动和几何损耗而损坏的帧。我们首先联合考虑大气湍流、ORIS引起的指向误差和几何衰减，推导了发射端-ORIS-接收端（Tx-ORIS-Rx）反射链路的可处理统计模型。基于此，我们获得了采用Chase合并的HARQ（HARQ-CC）的中断概率（OP）闭合表达式，以及采用增量冗余的HARQ（HARQ-IR）的解析中断上界，上述结果在任意最大传输轮数下均有效。我们进一步进行了高信噪比（SNR）分析，充分刻画了中断行为，并揭示了两种方案的分集阶数。此外，我们通过平均传输轮数和给定成功解码条件下的条件平均轮数，表征了截断HARQ过程的时延特性。最后，数值和蒙特卡洛结果验证了所提分析的正确性，并表明HARQ显著提升了ORIS辅助FSO的可靠性，其中HARQ-IR即使在使用较少重传轮数时，也能实现比HARQ-CC更低的中断概率和更低的时延。

</details>


### [237] [Hybrid TRP-UE Sensing for Enhanced Target Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20413)
*Necati Kagan Erkek,Marco Di Renzo,Arman Shojaeifard,Yasser Mestrah,Remun Koirala,Mohammad Heggo,Kunjan Shah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合TRP-UE感知机制，在3GPP兼容的ISAC信道模型下评估，证明在室内工厂等挑战性传播环境中，UE辅助感知能显著提升TRP感知性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 提高对无源移动感知目标的精准定位能力，特别是在室内工厂等复杂传播环境下的网络感知性能。

Method: 提出一种混合TRP-UE感知机制，将基于TRP的网络感知与UE辅助感知相结合，提升感知性能。

Result: 基于3GPP兼容的ISAC信道模型的评估结果表明，在室内工厂等挑战性传播环境中，UE辅助感知显著提升了TRP感知的性能。

Conclusion: 混合TRP-UE感知机制能够有效提升ISAC网络在复杂环境下的感知能力，UE辅助感知是对TRP感知的重要补充。

Original Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) refers to the capability for the network to provide communications services whilst also being able to sense the environment in a scalable manner. One of the key functions of ISAC is the accurate localization of passive and mobile sensing targets. This paper introduces a novel hybrid TRP-UE sensing mechanism that improves network-based sensing performance. Evaluation results are provided using 3GPP-compliant ISAC channel models. The results demonstrate the significant benefit in complimenting TRP-based sensing with UE-assisted sensing in challenging propagation environments such as indoor factory.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）是指网络在提供通信服务的同时，能够以可扩展的方式感知环境的能力。ISAC的关键功能之一是对无源移动感知目标进行精准定位。本文提出了一种新颖的混合TRP-UE感知机制，用于提升基于网络的感知性能。采用3GPP兼容的ISAC信道模型提供了评估结果。结果表明，在室内工厂等具有挑战性的传播环境中，UE辅助感知对基于TRP的感知具有显著的补充效益。

</details>


### [238] [Max-Min Rate Fairness Optimization for Multi-User Pinching-Antenna NOMA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20450)
*Mahmoud AlaaEldin,Amy Inwood,Xidong Mu,Michail Matthaiou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段结构化优化框架，用于联合优化多波导PAS辅助下行NOMA系统中的PA位置和基站发射预编码，以最大化最小用户速率。第一阶段使用内点法忽略PA信道相位进行粗略优化，第二阶段通过相位归零和交替迭代精调，显著提升了系统性能并降低了计算时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: PAS虽能通过重新定位PA克服信号阻塞，但多PA间的干扰导致信道相干和快速振荡，使得PA位置与预编码的联合优化问题高度非光滑、非凸，现有方法难以有效求解。

Method: 提出两阶段结构化优化框架：第一阶段忽略PA信道相位，使用内点法进行PA位置和功率分配的粗略优化；第二阶段考虑相位影响，先通过相位归零局部调整PA位置使信道相位对齐，再采用前向-后向PA位置精调与SCA复发射预编码优化的交替迭代过程，直至收敛。

Result: 仿真表明所提框架在计算时间远低于启发式基准的同时显著优于它们；相比可比的MIMO下行NOMA系统也展现出较大增益；揭示了PA数量、用户数和发射功率对系统性能的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段优化框架有效解决了PAS多用户NOMA系统中PA位置与预编码的联合优化难题，在性能和计算效率上均优于现有方案。

Original Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASs) can overcome signal blockage by repositioning dielectric radiating elements, called pinching antennas (PAs), along meter-scale waveguides to create line-of-sight links. Since each waveguide is driven by a single radio-frequency (RF) chain, non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) is well suited for PAS-based multi-user communications. This paper studies a PAS-enabled multi-user downlink NOMA system with multiple waveguides, each equipped with multiple PAs. The PA positions and base-station transmit precoding are jointly optimized to maximize the minimum user rate. The resulting problem is highly non-smooth and non-convex because of the rapidly oscillating coherent sums caused by inter-PA interference. To tackle this challenge, we propose a two-stage structured optimization framework. In the first stage, coarse PA-position and power-allocation optimization is performed using an interior-point algorithm while neglecting the PA channel phases, which gives solutions near the true optima. In the second stage, PA positions and transmit precoding are fine-tuned while accounting for the PA channel phase shifts. This stage first applies phase zeroing, where each PA is locally repositioned to align the corresponding channel phase toward zero and promote constructive coherent combining. It then uses an alternating procedure that iteratively performs forward-backward PA position refinement and successive-convex-approximation-based complex transmit precoding optimization until convergence, thereby reducing residual phase mismatch. Simulation results show that the proposed framework significantly outperforms heuristic optimization benchmarks with much lower computational time. They also demonstrate large gains over a comparable multiple-input multiple-output downlink NOMA system and reveal the impact of the number of PAs, users, and transmit power on system performance.

中文摘要翻译: 捏合天线系统（PAS）通过沿米级波导重新定位称为捏合天线（PA）的介电辐射元件来创建视距链路，从而克服信号阻塞。由于每个波导由单个射频（RF）链驱动，非正交多址（NOMA）非常适用于基于PAS的多用户通信。本文研究了一种PAS驱动的多波导下行NOMA系统，每个波导配备多个PA。我们联合优化PA位置和基站发射预编码，以最大化最小用户速率。由于PA间干扰导致快速振荡的相干和，所得问题高度非光滑且非凸。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一个两阶段结构化优化框架。在第一阶段，忽略PA信道相位，使用内点法进行粗略的PA位置和功率分配优化，得到接近真正最优的解。在第二阶段，考虑PA信道相位偏移，对PA位置和发射预编码进行精调。该阶段先应用相位归零，即局部调整每个PA位置以使其信道相位对齐到零，促进建设性相干合并；然后采用交替过程，迭代执行前向-后向PA位置精调与基于逐次凸近似（SCA）的复发射预编码优化直至收敛，从而减少残余相位失配。仿真结果表明，所提框架显著优于启发式优化基准，且计算时间更低。同时，相较于可比的MIMO下行NOMA系统，该方法也展现出较大增益，并揭示了PA数量、用户数和发射功率对系统性能的影响。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Robust probabilistic measurement of structural-functional module consistency in infant brain development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19739)
*Lingbin Bian,Feihong Liu,Qian Wang,Han Zhang,Dinggang Shen,the UNC/UMN Baby Connectome Project Consortium*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机模块的结构-功能模块一致性（SFMC）的鲁棒概率度量方法，用于评估不同个体间脑网络模块的异质性，并发现从0到5岁期间SFMC逐渐下降，初级脑区一致性高于高级认知区。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统脑网络模块划分未考虑个体间变异性，且结构与功能模块的群体规模可能不一致，缺乏一种能够鲁棒评估二者一致性的概率性度量方法。

Method: 引入随机模块概念，将每个脑区分配到群体级子网络的过程建模为一个概率事件，通过分配概率表征脑区属于某个模块的可能性，从而计算结构-功能模块一致性（SFMC）。

Result: 基于BCP数据集，SFMC在0-5岁间呈下降趋势；初级脑区（如视觉区）一致性较高，而高级认知区（如注意、控制、默认模式网络相关区域）一致性较低；与传统的结构-功能耦合方法相比，随机模块方法揭示了更显著的发育重组变化。

Conclusion: 随机模块方法能鲁棒地评估脑结构与功能模块的一致性，并能捕捉到个体间模块变异性，为脑网络发育研究提供了新的分析工具。

Original Abstract: Brain network is commonly divided into modules for analyzing their functionally segregated roles for group-level analysis in neuroimaging studies. Here, we introduce stochastic modules within brain networks for a robust probabilistic measurement of structural-functional module consistency (SFMC) in a group of subjects. Specifically, a stochastic module can be regarded as the chance of a brain region across subjects potentially being assigned to a group-level sub-network, characterized as an assignment probability for this brain region. This novel method has two advantages for evaluating inhomogeneous modules in brain networks. The first is that it can robustly evaluate the consistency between brain structural and functional modules whose population sizes are not necessary the same, and the second is that it is able to take into account the inter-individual variability of the modules for the groups. Moreover, compared with the conventional structural-functional coupling approach, our stochastic module-based method reveals a more pronounced decline in the coupling between structure and function, indicating stronger developmental reorganization. Our results using the dataset from Baby Connectome Project (BCP) show that the SFMC decreases from 0 to 5 years old, and is greater in primary brain regions, such as visual areas, while lower in more advanced cognitive regions, including those related to attention, control, and default mode network.

中文摘要翻译: 脑网络通常被划分为模块，用于在神经影像学研究中分析群体水平上功能分离的角色。本文在脑网络中引入随机模块，以对一组被试的结构-功能模块一致性（SFMC）进行鲁棒的概率度量。具体而言，随机模块可被视为一个脑区在不同被试间可能被分配到某个群体级子网络的机会，表征为该脑区的一个分配概率。该新方法在评估脑网络中非均匀模块时具有两个优势：其一，能够鲁棒地评估群体规模不必相同的脑结构与功能模块之间的一致性；其二，能够考虑群体间模块的个体间变异性。此外，与传统的结构-功能耦合方法相比，基于随机模块的方法揭示了结构与功能之间更显著的耦合下降，表明更强的发育重组。我们使用婴儿连接组计划（BCP）数据集的结果显示，SFMC在0至5岁期间呈下降趋势，且在视觉区等初级脑区中较高，而在注意力、控制及默认模式网络相关的高级认知区域中较低。

</details>
